An exact error identity guides the choice of observables, motivating a construction based on repeated commutators of the target with the Hamiltonian. For Pauli observables and Pauli-rotation circuits, the update can be estimated without ancillary qubits or controlled operations for overlap estimation. Across six-qubit spin, fermionic, and molecular benchmarks, the targeted update extends the median time within the target-error tolerance by up to a factor of $4.2$ relative to standard variational quantum simulation at equal shot budgets. These results show that directing the variational update toward the target observable can extend accurate simulation without increasing the measurement cost per time step.

</details>


### [108] [Stabilizing temporal quantum-enhanced sensitivity via sub-optimal measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24805)
*Wangsheng Zheng,Yaoling Yang,Abolfazl Bayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立多参数非平衡量子传感的普适时间标度律，揭示次优测量下精度呈二次标度并叠加有界振荡，提出分组分时测量协议以消除振荡、稳健恢复二次标度，并通过三个实例与贝叶斯估计验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强灵敏度依赖随时间超线性（超线性）标度的精度，但需最优测量；最优测量通常复杂且依赖时间，实际难以实现，而次优测量往往无法保持稳健超线性标度并出现振荡。

Method: 在一般多参数非平衡量子传感框架下建立普适时间标度律；刻画次优测量下传感精度的普适行为（二次标度乘以有界振荡函数）；提出将测量划分为不同组、各组在不同时刻执行的协议，以聚合集体精度。

Result: 次优测量下精度表现为受有界振荡调制的二次标度；分组分时测量协议可在多参数体制下稳定获得稳健的二次标度；通过三个不同实例及贝叶斯估计完成验证。

Conclusion: 该协议适用于任意有信息量的测量，向量子增强灵敏度的实际实现迈出关键一步。

Abstract: In non-equilibrium probes, quantum-enhanced sensitivity is quantified through super-linear scaling of precision with respect to time, as a central metrology resource. However, this requires optimal measurements, which are often complex and time-dependent, making it challenging in practice. Sub-optimal measurements typically fail to retain robust super-linear scaling and show oscillations. Here, we begin by establishing a universal temporal scaling law in a general multi-parameter non-equilibrium quantum sensing framework. Within this setting, we identify the universal behavior of the sensing precision with sub-optimal measurements which shows quadratic scaling modulated by an additional bounded oscillatory function. To remove the oscillatory part, we propose a protocol in which measurements are partitioned into different groups, each performed at different times. The collective precision obtained from this protocol stabilizes a robust quadratic scaling even for sub-optimal measurements in the multi-parameter regime. We validate our protocol through three distinct examples as well as Bayesian estimation. Our protocol is applicable to every informative measurement and takes a key step towards practical realization of quantum-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [109] [Trade-offs and experimental feasibility of nonlocal polygamy with two-outcome Bell inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24835)
*Josep Batle,Tomasz Rybotycki,Tomasz Białecki,Piotr Gawron,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多体量子系统中 Bell 不等式的"同时违反"现象，考察每方使用二结果可观测量、每方 2 或 3 种测量设置的配置，给出可在 IBM Quantum 硬件上展示的配置，并推导若干多夫配置（polygamous configurations）的界与权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与 Bell 非定域性在多体系统不同子系统间的共享约束并不相同，理解这些约束及其极限，是量子关联理论的核心问题；同时，将这类理论约束与实际量子计算机的可验证能力对接，具有实验与理论双重意义。

Method: 以二结果可观测量为基本工具，限定每方测量设置为 2 种或 3 种，系统考察多体 Bell 不等式的同时违反情形；筛选出可由 IBM Quantum 硬件实现的配置，并借助数学方法推导 polygamous 配置下的界与权衡关系。

Result: 辨识出一批能够同时在 IBM Quantum 硬件上展示 Bell 不等式违反的测量配置；针对若干 polygamous 配置，给出了相应的界与权衡关系（trade-off relations）。

Conclusion: 研究将量子计算机的实验能力与刻画量子关联极限的数学方法相连接，为在多体量子系统上同时检验 Bell 非定域性的共享结构提供了可行路径与理论边界。

Abstract: Entanglement and Bell nonlocality have different sharing constraints across subsystems of a multipartite quantum system. We examine simultaneous violations of Bell inequalities with two-outcome observables and two or three measurement settings per party. We identify configurations whose simultaneous violations can be demonstrated on IBM Quantum hardware, and derive bounds and trade-off relations for several polygamous configurations. The results connect experimental capabilities of quantum computers with mathematical methods for studying the limits of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [110] [Explanation of the Observed Energy Exchange through the vacuum in Optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24900)
*Vincenzo Macrì,Franco Nori,Alessandro Ferreri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出标准大失谐近似忽略的高阶光力相互作用可自然产生声子-声子耦合，从微观上解释了实验中观察到的真空介导热传递，并进一步提出基于该相互作用提取净功的循环协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准大失谐区仅保留辐射压力相互作用、将高阶镜-场相互作用视为可忽略，无法微观解释Fong等人实验（Nature 576, 243 (2019)）中机械薄膜间由真空介导的热传递，该现象的物理起源仍存争议。

Method: 采用全量子模型，在标准光力框架内保留此前被忽略的高阶光力相互作用，分析其产生的声子-声子耦合；并在此基础上设计一个由声子-声子相互作用驱动的循环过程以实现净功提取。

Result: 被忽略的高阶光力相互作用自然生成声子-声子耦合，能够定量复现实验观测到的能量交换；所提出的循环协议可实现净功提取。

Conclusion: 高阶光力相互作用具有基础性作用，为超越线性近似、描述下一代光力实验提供了微观理论框架。

Abstract: In cavity optomechanics, the standard large-detuning regime is commonly described by retaining only the radiation-pressure interaction, while higher-order mirror--field interactions are assumed to be negligible. This approximation, however, fails to provide a microscopic explanation for the vacuum-mediated heat transfer observed between the mechanical membranes in the experiment of Fong, et.al.,[Nature \textbf{576}, 243 (2019)], whose physical origin has remained under active debate. Here, using a fully quantum model, we show that the neglected higher-order optomechanical interactions naturally generate phonon-phonon coupling and quantitatively account for the observed energy exchange within the standard optomechanical framework. Building on this microscopic description, we further propose a protocol in which phonon-phonon interaction drives a cyclic process enabling net work extraction. Our results establish the fundamental role of higher-order optomechanical interactions and provide a microscopic framework for describing next-generation optomechanical experiments beyond the linear approximation.

</details>


### [111] [A thermal microwave bus for neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24933)
*Matthew J. H. Kendall,Christopher J. Watson,Michael Ben Shem,Jonathan D. Breeze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种四能级中性原子-微波腔混合架构，通过腔介导实现长程两比特门，并验证其在量子纠错中的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列中高保真两比特门依赖Rydberg阻塞，但其本质上是短程相互作用，需通过原子穿梭（shuttling）获得长程连通性，代价高且影响保真度。

Method: 设计四能级架构：基态量子比特提供长寿命，Rydberg态与微波腔耦合以实现长程腔介导门。先研究两种既有协议（Tavis-Cummings模型下的色散iSWAP、驱动腔生成的受控相位门）的热依赖性，在Rydberg衰减、热光子、有限腔线宽下仿真并给出闭式保真度界限；再提出双色拉曼门，仅虚占据Rydberg态并抵消色散位移以抑制原子与腔衰减。

Result: 双色拉曼门实现保真度F=0.997。在微波腔内的完整光镊阵列中，用腔介导门闭合toric code的周期性边界，可将纠错轮次缩短2.3倍；应用于Bivariate bicycle码族时，将gross code的一轮缩短4.8倍。

Conclusion: 腔介导的长程两比特门可有效弥补Rydberg阻塞短程性，双色拉曼门兼具高保真与抗衰减优势，并为量子纠错码提供显著的时间开销缩减。

Abstract: High-fidelity two-qubit gates in neutral-atom arrays rely on the Rydberg blockade, which is intrinsically short ranged and requires long range connectivity to be achieved through atom shuttling. We propose a four level architecture, where the typical ground state qubit can be leveraged for its long lifetime and the Rydberg states couple to a microwave cavity, allowing for long range cavity mediated gates. We first find the thermal dependence of two established protocols, the dispersive iSWAP native to the Tavis-Cummings model and the Controlled phase gate generated from driving the cavity. We simulate them under the presence of Rydberg decay, thermal photons, finite cavity linewidth and find fidelities which accompany closed form bounds. We then introduce the bichromatic Raman gate, which only virtually populates the Rydberg states and cancels dispersive shifts to mitigate both atomic and cavity decay, achieving a fidelity of $F = 0.997$. Finally, we consider a full optical tweezer array in a microwave cavity, and show that using a cavity mediated gate to close the periodic boundaries of the toric code shortens an error correction round by a factor of 2.3 for realistic array sizes. This was then applied to the wider family of Bivariate bicycle codes, and we find that it shortens a round of the gross code by a factor of 4.8.

</details>


### [112] [Efficient Heralding of Loss-Tolerant Photonic GHZ States for Device-Independent Conference Key Agreement over Long Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24941)
*Yazeed K. Alwehaibi,Makoto Ishihara,Shakib Daryanoosh,Ewan Mer,Shang Yu,Wojciech Roga,Ian A. Walmsley,Masahiro Takeoka,Raj B. Patel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了GHZ态的单模光子数编码在损耗下的容错差异，提出可预报真空-n光子GHZ态的星型网络协议，实现低探测效率下的无漏洞DI-CKA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）需要在有损量子网络中分发多体纠缠。虽然无损耗时不同单模光子数编码的GHZ态局域等价，但损耗下其容错性差异显著，因此需要寻找更耐损耗的编码与分发方案。

Method: 理论分析计算基GHZ态（真空与n光子相干叠加）与固定光子数GHZ态的临界探测效率，推导精确解析条件；设计异构源的星型网络协议以直接预报真空-n光子GHZ态；对四用户情形刻画损耗下的预报态；考察理想Pauli测量与位移测量，并分析基于SPDC的实现及源不对称性对测量角色分配的影响。

Result: 计算基GHZ态可在显著更低探测效率下实现无探测漏洞的宇称-CHSH违背；协议长距离标度为O(η_c^{n/2})；四用户下可调源参数在任意有限距离保持真多体纠缠；在当前光电探测器可达到的探测效率下实现DI-CKA，且密钥率与通信距离与已有预报方案相当；SPDC实现中源不对称性使测量角色分配变得关键。

Conclusion: 光子数编码、源架构与测量角色分配是设计耐损耗多体量子网络与提升DI-CKA性能的关键设计参数。

Abstract: Heralded multipartite entanglement distribution is a key requirement for device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) over lossy quantum networks. Although locally equivalent in the absence of loss, different single-rail photon-number encodings of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states can exhibit substantially different loss tolerance. We show that computational-basis GHZ states, comprising a coherent superposition of vacuum and an $n$-photon component, enable detection-loophole-free parity-CHSH violations at markedly lower detection efficiencies than previously considered fixed-photon-number GHZ states, and derive exact analytical conditions for the critical detection efficiencies of both state classes. Motivated by this advantage, we introduce a star-network protocol using heterogeneous sources to directly herald vacuum-$n$-photon GHZ states with long-distance scaling $O(η_{\mathrm{c}}^{n/2})$, where $η_{\mathrm{c}}$ is the channel transmittance. For four users, we characterize the heralded state under photon loss and show that tunable source parameters preserve genuine multipartite entanglement at any finite channel distance. For both ideal Pauli measurements and experimentally accessible displacement-based measurements, our protocol enables DI-CKA at detection efficiencies achievable with current photodetectors, while retaining key rates and communication distances comparable to those of previous heralded schemes. We discuss physical implementations and analyze an SPDC-based realization, showing that source-induced asymmetry can make measurement-role assignment in the parity-CHSH test crucial. These results identify photon-number encoding, source architecture, and measurement-role assignment as design parameters for loss-tolerant multipartite quantum networks and enhanced DI-CKA performance.

</details>


### [113] [Tight entropy contraction beyond detailed balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24953)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限维量子信道建立了超越细致平衡条件的定量熵收缩界。对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，证明GNS范数收缩可导出相对熵收缩，其维度损失仅为对数级；对具忠实渐近条件期望的量子Markov半群，给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数由GNS谱隙控制的下界。所得界允许初始态与环境纠缠，且与环境系统规模无关，应用于交替储层脉冲与连续耦合能梯时达到最优规模依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子的熵收缩/对数Sobolev不等式通常依赖细致平衡（可逆性）假设，这限制了其在非可逆、非平衡量子信道上的适用性。作者希望突破该假设，为更一般的有限维量子信道建立定量熵收缩界，并刻画其维度依赖，从而为量子Markov半群的混合时间与谱隙关系提供更一般的工具。

Method: 针对与忠实条件期望相容的量子信道，利用GNS（Gelfand–Naimark–Segal）构造与相应范数，建立GNS范数收缩到相对熵收缩的定量关系；对具忠实渐近条件期望的量子Markov半群，通过该关系将完全修正对数Sobolev常数与GNS谱隙相联系，推导下界。分析中允许初始态含外部环境纠缠，并追踪界对系统维数的依赖。

Result: GNS范数收缩可推出相对熵收缩，维度常数损失仅为对数级；对具忠实渐近条件期望的量子Markov半群，获得完全修正对数Sobolev常数以GNS谱隙为下界的估计；所导出的界在初始纠缠情形下成立且与环境系统规模无关；应用于交替储层脉冲与连续耦合能量梯时，得到规模依赖最优的界。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵收缩理论从细致平衡情形推广到更一般的条件期望相容信道，给出对数级维度损失的GNS-相对熵收缩与对数Sobolev常数下界，并证明界对环境规模鲁棒、对典型应用最优，为分析非平衡量子信道的混合与弛豫提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We establish quantitative entropy contraction bounds for finite-dimensional quantum channels beyond detailed balance. For channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that GNS (Gelfand--Naimark--Segal) norm contraction yields relative entropy contraction with only a logarithmic loss in the dimensional constant. For quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful asymptotic conditional expectation, this gives a lower bound on the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev constant in terms of the GNS spectral gap. The bounds allow initial entanglement with an external environment and are independent of its system size. Applications to alternating reservoir pulses and continuously coupled energy ladders give bounds with optimal size dependence.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Microcanonical Operator Spectrum, BPS Chaos and Free Probability Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22422)
*Alexandre Belin,Yichao Fu,Federico La Rocca*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了简单算符投影到微正则能量窗口后的本征谱，证明在简单算符本征基相对哈密顿量本征基为Haar随机取向的假设下，该谱服从高斯随机矩阵统计，并讨论了其对BPS混沌与本征态热化的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单算符的矩阵元是混沌量子多体系统热化的核心，其矩通常由本征态热化假说（ETH）统计描述。但ETH只刻画单个矩，缺乏对矩阵元整体的精细刻画，尤其在哈密顿量谱存在简并时，逐矩描述不够充分，因此需要一个能同时编码所有矩的更精细表征。

Method: 将简单算符投影到微正则能量窗口，研究其本征谱以同时编码矩阵元的所有矩。假设简单算符的本征基相对哈密顿量本征基为Haar随机取向，直接分析由该随机取向诱导的概率分布，并借助自由概率论（Free Probability Theory）工具进行证明。

Result: 证明投影到小微正则窗口的算符谱服从高斯随机矩阵统计（Gaussian random matrix statistics）。该结果在哈密顿量谱简并情形下尤其有意义，并可用于讨论BPS混沌及更一般的本征态热化含义。

Conclusion: 简单算符在微正则窗口内的谱由高斯随机矩阵统计普适描述，为矩阵元提供了超越逐矩ETH的整体刻画，并对BPS混沌与更广泛的本征态热化现象具有启示意义。

Abstract: Matrix elements of simple operators are central to thermalization in chaotic quantum many-body systems. Their moments are often described statistically through the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis. In this paper, we study a more refined characterization of these matrix elements: the eigenspectrum of simple operators once projected to microcanonical energy windows. The spectrum encodes all moments of the matrix elements at once, and is particularly relevant when the spectrum of the Hamiltonian is degenerate. Assuming that the eigenbasis of the simple operator is Haar-randomly oriented with respect to that of the Hamiltonian, we prove that the spectrum of an operator projected to a small microcanonical window obeys Gaussian random matrix statistics. We do this directly by studying the probability distribution inherited from the random orientation, as well as using tools from Free Probability Theory. We discuss the implications for BPS chaos and more generally for eigenstate thermalization.

</details>


### [115] [Tripartite Form Universality in Holographic Entropy Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22504)
*Veronika E. Hubeny,Yu Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息熵不等式的"三分形式"表示，猜想其普遍性，并通过证明两个已知无限族全息熵不等式均可写为三分形式来提供有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息熵不等式（超出次可加性）刻画了全息中几何态的纠缠结构。arXiv:2309.06296 提出的"三分形式"（由单位系数的三分信息和条件三分信息项组成）为这些不等式提供了紧凑有用的封装，但并非先验保证所有不等式都能重写为该形式，因此需要验证其普遍性。

Method: 作者提出猜想（所有全息熵不等式都可写成三分形式），通过证明 arXiv:2309.15145 中发现的两个已知无限族全息熵不等式确实可以写成三分形式。作者给出每个族成员的显式三分形式表达式，并详述推导过程，其间推导出若干有用恒等式。此外，利用三分形式证明全息熵不等式满足的若干结构性质。

Result: 成功将 arXiv:2309.15145 中的两个无限族全息熵不等式全部改写为显式的三分形式表达式；推导出若干有用恒等式，可作为进一步重构相应信息量的垫脚石；并证明了全息熵不等式满足的若干结构性质。

Conclusion: 支持了"所有全息熵不等式都可写成三分形式"的猜想，尤其对这些族而言重构并非平凡；三分形式作为分析工具具有强大作用，可用于证明全息熵不等式的结构性质，并为信息量的进一步重新封装提供基础。

Abstract: To elucidate the meaning of holographic entropy inequalities (beyond subadditivity) which characterize the entanglement structure of geometric states in holography, arXiv:2309.06296 proposed the "tripartite form" for these inequalities, consisting of tripartite information and conditional tripartite information terms with unit coefficients. While this provides a compact and useful packaging of the inequalities, it is not a priori guaranteed that all inequalities can be recast in this form. Here we conjecture that they can, and present substantial evidence, by proving that the two known infinite families of holographic entropy inequalities found in arXiv:2309.15145 can indeed be written in the tripartite form. This is significant because such recasting is particularly nontrivial for these families. Apart from providing the explicit tripartite form expressions for every member of these two infinite families, we detail how we arrived at them, in the process deriving several useful identities which may serve as stepping stones to formulate further repackaging of the corresponding information quantities. We also illustrate the power of the tripartite form by proving a number of structural properties satisfied by any holographic entropy inequality.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum inversion of the vacuum hierarchy and multi-kink unbinding in the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24861)
*Jonathan Lozano-Mayo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非简并非局域双正弦-戈登模型中真空能量与多扭结束缚的量子修正，发现量子涨落可反转经典真空层级，使中央次级真空在某一耦合以上占优；矩阵乘积态与有限链计算共同证实该反转及其对多扭结结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典层面非简并双正弦-戈登模型的真空层级与多扭结束缚已明确，但量子涨落是否改变真空排序及多扭结稳定性尚不清楚，需要定量评估量子修正的效应。

Method: 在初级真空的介子质量处作正规序，进行单圈计算以得到真空能与多扭结束缚的量子修正；对 n=4 情况使用矩阵乘积态数值计算，并将体能量密度对格点间距作外推以逼近连续极限；同时用高斯有效势与集体坐标描述进行对比与解释。

Result: 单圈计算预言经典真空层级发生反转，量子涨落使中央次级真空在由经典真空劈裂与介子质量比设定的耦合以上占优。n=4 的矩阵乘积态计算证实该反转，体能量密度外推支持其在连续极限中持续存在。反转交叉点低于单圈预言，在 α=0.3 时低约 22%，且与高斯有效势结果吻合。有限链计算显示，Q=4 多扭结的中央区域随真空能差减小而扩张，越过交叉点后随系统尺寸增长。

Conclusion: 量子修正可反转非简并双正弦-戈登模型的经典真空层级并驱动多扭结走向解绑，复合体的总质量修正仍不到经典值的十分之一，表明束缚丧失是一个渐进过程。

Abstract: We study the quantum corrections to the vacuum energies and to the binding of the multi-kinks of the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model. With normal ordering defined at the meson mass of the primary vacuum, the one-loop calculation predicts an inversion of the classical vacuum hierarchy, in which quantum fluctuations favour the central secondary vacuum above a coupling set by the classical splitting of the vacua and their meson mass ratio. Matrix-product-state calculations for $n=4$ confirm the inversion, and extrapolation of the bulk energy densities in the lattice spacing supports its persistence in the continuum. The crossing lies below the one-loop prediction, by $22$ percent at $α=0.3$, and is reproduced closely by the Gaussian effective potential. Finite-chain calculations at $α=0.3$ show that the central region of the multi-kink of topological charge $Q=4$ expands as the difference of the vacuum energies falls, as a collective-coordinate description predicts, and that beyond the crossing it grows with the size of the system. The composite therefore approaches the loss of its binding with a correction to its total mass still under a tenth of the classical value.

</details>


### [117] [External Sources in Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22526)
*L. H. C. Borges,A. A. Nogueira,F. A. Barone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在赝量子电动力学（PQED）框架下研究了静止点状外源之间的相互作用，涵盖普通电荷、狄拉克点及广义拓扑源，计算了相互作用能、力、力矩与电磁场分布，并与普通平面麦克斯韦电动力学对比，揭示了PQED非局域性带来的新物理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQED作为描述平面凝聚态系统的有效理论，其非局域特性可能导致与常规局域平面电动力学不同的物理效应，但静止外源间的相互作用细节（尤其是拓扑源及力矩效应）尚未被系统研究，因此需要探究其独特表现。

Method: 在PQED框架下引入静止点状外源，考虑普通电荷、狄拉克点和广义拓扑源三类构型，解析计算相应的相互作用能、力、力矩及电磁场配置，并将结果与普通平面麦克斯韦电动力学进行对比。

Result: PQED的非局域性产生了常规局域平面理论中不存在的物理效应；拓扑源诱导出本质上的各向异性和非中心相互作用，导致相互作用物体之间出现非平凡力矩。

Conclusion: 这些相互作用的方向性特征表明，PQED有望用于平面凝聚态系统中拓扑缺陷和各向异性现象的有效描述。

Abstract: In this letter, we investigate the interaction between stationary point-like external sources within the framework of Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics (PQED). Besides ordinary electric charges, we consider configurations involving Dirac points and generalized topological sources. For the configurations analyzed, we compute the corresponding interaction energies, forces, torques, and electromagnetic field configurations. Furthermore, we compare the results obtained in PQED with those arising in ordinary planar Maxwell electrodynamics, showing that the nonlocal character of PQED gives rise to physical effects with no counterpart in the conventional local planar theory. In particular, our results show that topological sources induce intrinsically anisotropic and non-central interactions, leading to the emergence of nontrivial torques between the interacting objects. The directional character of these interactions suggests possible applications in the effective description of topological defects and anisotropic phenomena in planar condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [118] [Phase Transitions at More Unusual Values of Theta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22613)
*Dhruv Aldas,Hitoshi Murayama,Bea Noether,Digvijay Roy Varier*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过N=1有效场论方法，计算了一类QCD"表亲"理论中真空能的θ依赖，复现了此前需完整N=2机制才能得到的结果，并识别出相变可发生在θ/π的有理分式及无理数值处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整N=2超对称机制虽可精确分析真空结构，但对更广泛的理论族往往难以处理，因此需要一种更简便的方法来研究θ依赖与相变行为。

Method: 采用N=1有效场论，从带有N_f个基本超多重态的N=2 SU(N_c)规范理论出发，经反常介导超对称破缺（AMSB）变形后进行分析，取代原先的完整N=2计算框架。

Result: 复现了先前用完整N=2机制得到的结果；该方法可推广至N=2方法无法处理的无限多理论；识别出发生相变的θ值，且相变可出现在θ/π的有理分式与无理数值处。

Conclusion: N=1方法为研究此类理论的θ依赖和相变提供了更简洁且适用范围更广的工具，并揭示了相变位置在θ/π取值上的非平凡结构。

Abstract: We calculate the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy in an infinite family of cousins of QCD, where the vacuum structure can be analyzed exactly. These theories are $\mathcal{N}=1$ EFTs that descend from $\mathcal{N}=2$ $SU(N_c)$ gauge theory with $N_f$ flavors of fundamental hypermultiplets, that are then deformed by anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB). We reproduce results found in previous work that used the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ machinery but via a much simpler $\mathcal{N}=1$ method. Our method easily extends to infinitely many more theories for which the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ approach becomes intractable. We identify values of $θ$ for which the theory undergoes a phase transition, and find that they can occur at both fractional and irrational values of $θ/π$.

</details>


### [119] [Miwa deformation of WLZZ matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24360)
*T. Diudin,A. Popolitov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出在WLZZ矩阵模型正分支上定义多元正交多项式的通用方法，将此前仅在高斯情形下已知的严格超可积性与Miwa形变公式推广至更一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献仅在高斯情形下讨论了WLZZ矩阵模型的多元正交多项式及相关性质（严格超可积性、Miwa形变公式），缺乏对一般正分支的普适定义，因此需要给出统一构造并完成推广。

Method: 提出一种通用方式，为WLZZ矩阵模型的正分支定义多元正交多项式，并以此为基础重新表述与推导相关结构。

Result: 成功推广了严格超可积性性质与Miwa形变公式，使其从高斯情形扩展到WLZZ模型正分支的一般情形。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用定义使WLZZ矩阵模型正分支上的多元正交多项式理论得以统一，并为超可积性等性质的进一步研究提供了框架。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a universal way to define multivariate orthogonal polynomials for the positive branch of WLZZ matrix models. This allows us to generalize from the Gaussian case, previously considered in the literature, the strict superintegrability property and the Miwa deformation formulas.

</details>


### [120] [Semiclassical reconstruction of the Toda pole lattice via analytic continuation of the Probabilistic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22717)
*Jacques Distler,Bahaa Elshimy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Toda场论关联函数重新解释为高斯测度下随机变量的w阶矩，给出收敛性与平凡性约束，并解析延拓后在半经典极限下重构Liouville电荷极点格，且用W_n闭合条件筛选出秩一Virasoro子模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Coulomb gas表示出发，为Toda关联函数提供系统的概率论解释，并统一理解Seiberg约束的收敛区域。

Method: 将原初场的n点函数看作高斯测度下随机变量的w-矩，推导Toda动量与耦合常数的约束；分析L^2、L^p收敛条件、关联函数平凡性约束及交叉矩的局部可积条件；借助Mellin-Barnes表示解析延拓矩指数；在半经典b→0极限下分析局部可积除子。

Result: 得到扩展的Toda关联函数收敛区域与Seiberg约束的概率解释；局部收敛边界变为亚纯局部可积除子，在半经典极限下重构Liouville电荷极点格；对A_n Toda理论，W_n闭合条件筛选出秩一Virasoro-like子模，其上标量可积条件化为Liouville-like除子并恢复相应Toda电荷极点格。

Conclusion: 概率构造为Toda场论关联函数提供了统一的收敛性框架和Seiberg约束解释，Mellin-Barnes延拓与半经典分析揭示了与Liouville理论的深层结构联系及W_n对称性筛选的秩一子模。

Abstract: We develop a probabilistic construction of Toda field theory correlators from the Coulomb gas representation. Reinterpreting $n$-point functions of primaries as $w$-moments of random variables with respect to a Gaussian measure, we derive constraints on the Toda momenta and coupling constant. $L^2$ and $L^p$ convergence conditions for the associated random variables, triviality constraints on the correlators, and local integrability conditions for the cross-moments are derived. These constraints define an extended region of convergence for the Toda correlators and provide a systematic, probabilistic interpretation of the Seiberg bounds. We proceed to analytically continue the moment indices by employing a Mellin-Barnes representation of the random variables. The local convergence boundaries become meromorphic local integrability divisors, and, in the semiclassical $b\to0$ limit, these divisors reconstruct the Liouville charge pole lattice. For $A_n$ Toda theory, we show that natural $W_n$-closure conditions in this semiclassical limit select rank-one, Virasoro-like submodules inside the full Toda theory. On these slices, the scalar integrability conditions reduce to Liouville-like divisors and recover the corresponding Toda charge pole lattices.

</details>


### [121] [Toroidal Charged AdS Branes and Deformed Toda Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23079)
*Sangheon Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明AdS中磁荷p-膜运动方程约化为耦合的一维Toda型方程，并据此构造具有T^n对称性和p个平移不变方向的静态环面黑膜，求解精确解、微扰修正及热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁荷p-膜在AdS中的运动方程结构，利用可积性构造具有环面拓扑的静态黑膜解，并探讨dilaton耦合对解及热力学行为的影响。

Method: 将运动方程约化为耦合一维Toda型方程；纯Maxwell情形求得精确sinh解；引入标量场e^{aφ}耦合得到非平凡dilaton与跑动环面模；在弱耦合ε=a²下进行微扰展开，将线性系统化为Pöschl-Teller方程，显式构造O(a²)反作用；通过表面引力、视界面积和全息重整化提取热力学量。

Result: 纯Maxwell情形获得精确解：(p+2)维黑膜乘n-环面，渐近AdS_{p+2}×T^n；dilaton情形解为非初等，涉及非整数阶连带Legendre函数，并显式给出O(a²)反作用。导出Hawking温度、熵密度和质量密度，验证第一定律dM=T_H ds及对偶(p+1)维理论的共形无迹应力张量。在O(a²)阶第一定律仍成立，但dilaton源使Stefan-Boltzmann指数移动为p_eff=p+a²/2，破坏s∝T_H^p的干净幂律，而可归一化dilaton hair与熵解耦。

Conclusion: 磁荷p-膜运动方程的Toda结构为构造环面黑膜提供了有效框架；dilaton耦合虽保留第一定律，却修正标度指数并破坏简单幂律，同时dilaton hair对熵无贡献。

Abstract: We show that the equations of motion for magnetically charged $p$-branes in $(n+p+2)$-dimensional AdS reduce to coupled one-dimensional Toda-type equations, and use this structure to construct static toroidal black branes with $T^n$ symmetry and $p$ translation-invariant directions. In the pure-Maxwell case we obtain an exact $\sinh$ solution--a $(p+2)$-dimensional black brane times an $n$-torus, asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}_{p+2}\times T^n$--whose dilatonic analogue, with a scalar coupled through $e^{aφ}$, has a running torus modulus and a non-trivial dilaton. A perturbative expansion reduces the linearized system to a pair of Pöschl-Teller equations, one elementary and one solved by associated Legendre functions of non-integer degree, so that the general solution is non-elementary; at weak coupling $ε=a^2$ we construct the $O(a^2)$ backreaction explicitly. From the surface gravity, horizon area, and holographic renormalization we obtain the Hawking temperature, entropy density, and mass density, verifying the first law $d\mathcal{M}=T_H\,ds$ and the traceless conformal stress tensor of the dual $(p+1)$-dimensional theory. At $O(a^2)$ the first law persists, but the dilaton source shifts the Stefan-Boltzmann exponent to $p_{\rm eff}=p+a^2/2$, breaking the clean power law $s\propto T_H^p$ while the normalizable dilaton hair decouples from the entropy.

</details>


### [122] [Homogeneous Non Symmetric Special Kähler Geometries as Broken Isometry Metrics on Symmetric CV {K}ähler Manifolds:a new tool for $r=2$ CaNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23395)
*Pietro Fré,Mario Trigiante,Antoine Van Proeyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了与Calabi-Vesentini对称空间度量等价的可解群$\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$同构于支撑齐次非对称特殊流形L$(-1,p)$的Kähler度量的可解群$\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$，并研究其等距群结构及其在Cartan神经网络中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Alekseevskyan视角看，两个空间对应同一可解李代数上的不同二次型，作者希望详细呈现并比较这一结构；同时该识别可为基于CV Tits-Satake普适类的Cartan神经网络提供工具，引入额外非线性变换以提升表达能力。

Method: 严格证明可解群$\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$（度量等价于$\mathrm{SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)\times SO(2+p)}$）与$\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$的同构；从Alekseevskyan角度比较同一可解李代数上的两个二次型；分析L$(-1,p)$的全等距群$\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$结构及其在可解坐标上的作用。

Result: 证明L$(-1,p)$可视为陪集流形$\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}/\mathrm{H}$，其中$\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{U(1)}_L\times \mathrm{SO(p)}$，$\mathrm{U(1)}_L$的生成元在$\so(2,2+p)$中但不在标准子代数$\so(2)\oplus\so(2+p)$中；且$\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}\subset \mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$是Calabi-Vesentini流形单纯等距群的非半单子群。

Conclusion: 该识别为Cartan神经网络提供工具，可在基于CV Tits-Satake普适类的$r=2$神经网络中每层映射间引入附加非线性变换，有望提升表达能力。

Abstract: In this paper we prove in full detail the isomorphism between the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$, metric equivalent to the Calabi Vesentini symmetric space $\mathrm{SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)\times SO(2+p)}$, and the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ supporting the Kähler metric of the homogeneous non symmetric special manifold L$(-1,p)$. From an Alekseevskyan point of view the two spaces simply correspond to two different quadratic forms on the same solvable Lie algebra that we present and compare in detail. Furthermore considering the full group of isometries $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ of L$(-1,p)$ we show that $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}\subset \mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$ is a non-semisimple subgroup of the simple isometry group of Calabi-Vesentini manifolds. Altogether the Special Kähler manifold L$(-1,p)$ can be seen as a coset manifold $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}/\mathrm{H}$ where $\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{U(1)}_L\times \mathrm{SO(p)}$, the generator of $\mathrm{U(1)}_L$ being in $\so(2,2+p)$, yet not in the canonical $\so(2)\oplus\so(2+p)$ subalgebra. The action of $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ on the solvable coordinates of the CV manifold can be constructed directly. This identification provides a valuable tool for Cartan Neural Networks, introducing additional non linear transformations in every map from one layer to the next one of an $r=2$ Neural Network based on the CV Tits Satake universality class; in perspective, this new tool increases expressivity.

</details>


### [123] [Spin density from second order transport in fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23399)
*Domingo Gallegos,Roi Klein,Amos Yarom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究含自旋自由度带电流体的流体力学，论证在3+1维共形场论中自旋密度与标准二阶输运系数的线性组合及规范/混合规范-引力反常系数平方项相关，并在自由与全息理论中计算自旋密度并比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋自由度在相对论流体力学中的角色尚待澄清，尤其自旋密度与输运系数、量子反常之间的定量关系缺乏统一描述。本文旨在建立该联系并检验其普适性。

Method: 采用有效场论/流体力学展开框架（至二阶输运阶），结合共形对称性；在若干自由场论与全息对偶模型中分别计算自旋密度并作对比。

Result: 发现自旋密度等于标准二阶输运系数的线性组合，并额外包含规范反常系数与混合规范-引力反常系数的二次项；自由理论与全息理论的计算结果相互印证（或呈现一致性/差异）。

Conclusion: 自旋密度受到二阶输运与量子反常的联合支配，为反常输运与自旋流体力学提供了可检验的判据。

Abstract: We study the hydrodynamics of a charged fluid with spin degrees of freedom. We argue that, in a conformal field theory in 3+1 dimensions, the spin density is related to a linear combination of standard second-order transport coefficients, and an additional term quadratic in the gauge- and mixed gauge-gravitational anomaly coefficients. We compute the spin density in several free and holographic theories and compare the results.

</details>


### [124] [Gauge Origami of the Conifold $\times$ $\mathbb{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23556)
*Taro Kimura,Go Noshita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在环面Calabi–Yau四重体 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 上由其brane brick model构造gauge-origami系统，统一实现了magnificent-four、tetrahedron-instanton与spiked-instanton三个扇区，并用Jeffrey–Kirwan留数定义配分函数、以着色BPS晶体刻画不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: gauge origami是研究超弦/M理论中D-brane束缚态与瞬子结构的重要框架，但此前多集中在特定或简单几何上。本文旨在以环面Calabi–Yau四重体 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 为具体背景，借助brane brick model给出系统化构造，从而为在一般环面Calabi–Yau四重体上推广gauge origami提供第一步。

Method: 方法上，从 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 的brane brick model出发构造gauge-origami系统；用brick matching与相边界分别确定基本D6-与D4-brane framing，从而在一个统一的framed quiver量子力学中实现三类基本扇区。配分函数通过Jeffrey–Kirwan留数定义，不动点用着色BPS晶体描述；一般构型则由这些基本扇区通过其公共fractional D0-brane quiver组合得到。

Result: 主要结果是：brick matching与相边界分别给出D6-和D4-brane framing；在一个统一framed quiver量子力学中同时容纳magnificent-four、tetrahedron-instanton与spiked-instanton扇区；配分函数由Jeffrey–Kirwan留数定义；不动点被刻画为着色BPS晶体；任意gauge-origami构型可由基本扇区经公共fractional D0-brane quiver组合生成。

Conclusion: 结论是，该构造在 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 上统一描述了多个gauge-origami基本扇区，并为在一般环面Calabi–Yau四重体上建立gauge origami提供了可推广的第一步。

Abstract: We construct gauge-origami systems on the toric Calabi--Yau fourfold $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ from its brane brick model. Brick matchings and phase boundaries determine elementary D6- and D4-brane framings, respectively, realizing the magnificent-four, tetrahedron-instanton, and spiked-instanton sectors in a unified framed quiver quantum mechanics. We define their partition functions by Jeffrey--Kirwan residues and describe the fixed points in terms of colored BPS crystals. General gauge-origami configurations are obtained by combining these elementary sectors through their common fractional D0-brane quiver. This construction provides a first step toward gauge origami on general toric Calabi--Yau fourfolds.

</details>


### [125] [Where's Kasner? OPE Signature of Kasner Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23776)
*Nejc Čeplak,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大$N$极限下研究全息热关联函数的解析结构，揭示其对偶黑洞近奇点区域的信息。作者分析截断型黑洞膜的标量两点函数中全息OPE数据的渐近行为，发现反弹奇点的幂律指数与黑洞奇点附近时间方向的Kasner指数存在直接关系，并讨论了该关系的适用范围及向横向空间方向推广的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息热关联函数的复时间奇点可对应于探测黑洞内部并从奇点反弹的测地线，因此蕴含对偶黑洞近奇点区域的非平庸信息。然而，这类反弹奇点的解析性质（尤其是其幂律指数）与奇点处几何（如Kasner指数）之间的定量关系尚不清楚，值得系统研究。

Method: 在大$N$极限下，选取黑洞膜几何（其blackening factor 在径向坐标的逆幂次上于有限阶截断），计算对偶标量两点函数所涉及的全息OPE数据，并分析其渐近行为。通过将复时间反弹奇点与探测黑洞内部并反弹的测地线相联系，提取幂律指数并与奇点附近时间方向的Kasner指数进行比对。

Result: 对于该类几何，发现反弹奇点的幂律指数与黑洞奇点附近时间方向的Kasner指数之间存在直接关系，并明确了该关系成立的参数区间（regime of validity）。

Conclusion: 全息热关联函数中的反弹奇点可作为探测黑洞内部奇点结构的有效探针；其幂律指数由奇点处时间方向的Kasner指数决定。作者讨论了该关系的适用条件，并展望了将其推广至横向空间方向的可能性。

Abstract: At large $N$, the analytic structure of holographic thermal correlation functions contains non-trivial information about the near-singularity region of dual black holes. In particular, complex-time singularities can be related to geodesics that probe the black hole interior and bounce off the singularity. We analyse the asymptotic behaviour of holographic OPE data entering scalar two-point functions in states dual to black branes whose blackening factors truncate at finite order in inverse powers of the radial coordinate. For this class of geometries, we find a direct relation between the power-law exponent of these bouncing singularities and the Kasner exponent associated with the time direction near the black hole singularity. We discuss the regime of validity of this relation and comment on possible extensions to the transverse spatial directions.

</details>


### [126] [Projective geometry of second-order supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23938)
*Stevan Cebrian,Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了无反常二阶超对称量子力学的射影几何表述：以射影联络与保持它的向量场为核心数据，统一刻画伴侣哈密顿量、超荷、奇点零点的光滑延拓以及Schwarzian量子修正，并用Lame单隙对示例说明其与Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro结构及Bol算子的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶超对称量子力学通常以超势及其零点条件表述，但超势零点处的算子光滑延拓、中心参数与单态能量的来源、以及量子修正项（Schwarzian）的几何本质缺乏统一解释。作者希望用射影几何为无反常二阶SUSY提供一个不依赖具体坐标选择的内在表述。

Method: 以射影联络和保持该联络的向量场为基本数据，固定物理坐标与能量原点后构造伴侣哈密顿量与超荷；利用向量场的二次不变量确定中心参数与候选单态能量；通过该场的固定点结构给出量子算子穿越超势零点的光滑延拓判据；分析经典虚构相似变换所选取的仿射数据，由协变因子导出Schwarzian量子修正；通过Riemannian模型的维数约化经由体积测度重现仿射数据，并用适配坐标实现耦合常数变形（coupling-constant metamorphosis）。

Result: 射影几何数据（射影联络+保联络向量场）完全决定伴侣哈密顿量与超荷；固定点结构提供超势零点光滑延拓的显式判据；二次不变量给出中心参数与候选单态能量；虚构相似变换的仿射数据协变因子生成Schwarzian量子修正；维数约化与适配坐标分别重现仿射数据并实现耦合常数变形。Lame单隙对示例展示SUSY辅助几何与支配物理解乘积的Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro结构的关系，其Lax-Novikov积分通过射影稳定化子的一阶作用作用于Schrodinger解，在无反射极限下于物理坐标中正比于辅助的三阶Bol算子。

Conclusion: 无反常二阶超对称量子力学可在射影几何框架下统一描述，射影联络与保联络向量场是核心几何对象，其固定点、二次不变量与仿射数据分别编码超势零点延拓、中心参数与Schwarzian修正；该框架与经典可积结构（Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro、Bol算子）自然衔接，Lame单隙对为具体验证提供了范例。

Abstract: We show that anomaly-free second-order supersymmetry (SUSY) in quantum mechanics admits a projective-geometric formulation in terms of a projective connection and a vector field preserving it. Once the physical coordinate and energy origin are fixed, these data determine the partner Hamiltonians and supercharges; the field's quadratic invariant fixes the central parameter and candidate singlet energies. The fixed-point structure of this field yields explicit criteria for smooth continuation of the quantum operators through superpotential zeros. The classically fictitious similarity transformation selects affine data whose covariant factors generate the Schwarzian quantum correction. Dimensional reduction of a Riemannian model reproduces these affine data through the volume measure, while a coordinate adapted to the selected field implements coupling-constant metamorphosis. A one-gap Lame pair illustrates the relation between the SUSY auxiliary geometry and the Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro structure governing physical solution products. Its Lax-Novikov integral acts on Schrodinger solutions through the first-order action of a projective stabilizer; in the reflectionless limit it becomes proportional, in the physical coordinate, to the auxiliary third Bol operator.

</details>


### [127] [Cassini Ansatz in the Skyrme Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23991)
*N. V. Gerasimeniuk,A. A. Korneev,A. V. Molochkov,O. G. Tkachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在Skyrme模型中研究球对称解的拉伸，以Cassini卵形线的三维推广作为延展拟设，得到静态能量对纵向形变参数的线性依赖，斜率约0.14 GeV²，与多种色禁闭模型给出的弦张力一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Skyrme模型中孤子解的形变行为，并检验其能量与弦张力（色禁闭相关量）之间的联系。

Method: 采用Cassini卵形线的三维推广作为延展拟设（ansatz），计算Skyrme模型球对称解在纵向拉伸下的静态能量随形变参数的变化。

Result: 静态能量随纵向形变参数呈线性增长，斜率σ≈0.14 GeV²，与多种禁闭模型给出的弦张力数值吻合良好。

Conclusion: Skyrme模型中延展孤子的能量线性行为可再现弦张力，暗示该类模型与色禁闭机制之间存在内在关联。

Abstract: We investigate the stretching of a spherically symmetric solution in the Skyrme model. The three-dimensional generalization of the Cassini ovals is considered as ansatz of extension. The linear dependence of static energy on the longitudinal deformation parameter is obtained with a slope $σ\approx 0.14\ \mathrm{ GeV}^2$, which is in good agreement with the string tension known from various models of confinement.

</details>


### [128] [Critical Kasner scaling from Cauchy-horizon collapse near holographic phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24227)
*Ling-Long Gao,Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非极端Cauchy视界黑洞的连续标量毛发分岔，发现零模在对数分支下产生非均匀临界极限，并证明该对数系数与Cauchy视界局部数据共同决定Einstein-Rosen桥的指数坍缩率及Kasner标度关系，从而由零模匹配直接确定临界指数与振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解黑洞分岔临界点附近内部几何的演化机制，特别是零模在Cauchy视界处的行为如何控制Einstein-Rosen桥坍缩及Kasner标度。同时区分有限温度分岔与低温下非线性红外完成对内部标度的不同主导作用。

Method: 通过分析非极端Cauchy视界黑洞的连续标量毛发分岔；利用零模匹配确定临界指数与振幅；以旋转BTZ向带毛旋转黑洞的转变为例说明机制；对标量化RN-AdS₄黑膜采用完全非线性数值解验证Kasner标度。

Result: 发现外部分形在临界处虽消失，但临界零模在参考Cauchy视界处普遍产生对数分支，导致非均匀临界极限；对数系数与Cauchy视界局部数据决定Einstein-Rosen桥的指数坍缩率；若坍缩后几何进入Kasner区，边界序参量标度O∝|λ-λ_c|^β进一步给出1-p_t∝|λ-λ_c|^{2β}。数值验证了标量化RN-AdS₄黑膜的Kasner标度；低温下两个具有相同线性临界数据的带电模型分别趋近幂律与逆对数Kasner标度的不同红外端点。

Conclusion: 有限温度分岔附近的内部几何由零模透射控制，而低温标度则由非线性红外完成主导；零模匹配无需拟合非线性内部即可确定临界指数与振幅。

Abstract: We study continuous scalar-hair bifurcations from stationary black holes with a nonextremal Cauchy horizon. Although the exterior deformation vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode generically develops a logarithmic branch at the reference Cauchy horizon, producing a nonuniform critical limit. We show that the coefficient of this logarithm in the scalar perturbation, together with local Cauchy-horizon data, determines the exponential collapse rate of the Einstein-Rosen bridge. If the post-collapse geometry evolves into a Kasner regime, the boundary order-parameter scaling $O\propto|λ-λ_c|^β$ further implies $
  1-p_t\propto|λ-λ_c|^{2β}. $ Crucially, zero-mode matching fixes both the critical exponents and the associated amplitudes, without any fit to the nonlinear interior. We illustrate this mechanism in the transition from rotating BTZ to hairy rotating black holes. For scalarized RN-AdS$_4$ black branes, we quantitatively verify the predicted Kasner scaling using fully nonlinear numerical solutions. At low temperature, two charged models with identical linear critical data instead approach different infrared endpoints, yielding respectively power-law and inverse-logarithmic Kasner scaling. Thus interiors near a finite-temperature bifurcation are controlled by zero-mode transmission, whereas low temperature scaling is governed by the nonlinear infrared completion.

</details>


### [129] [Saddle-Order Universality in Ensemble-Averaged Black Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24344)
*Ankit Anand,Peng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在约化欧几里得黑洞系综上建立广义自由能的鞍点序（saddle-order）命题：主导涨落由主导鞍点附近首个稳定化项的阶数决定，普通临界点由四次软模主导，给出四分之一温度贡献而非高斯半温度结果，并在RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约化黑洞系综中的涨落通常沿用高斯近似，给出半温度标度；但临界点处软模使高斯近似失效，需要一个统一框架来刻画不同阶软模主导的涨落行为。

Method: 在正则性与稳定性假设下，对系综平均广义自由能作鞍点展开，依据主导鞍点周围首个稳定项的阶数确定领头涨落贡献；解析计算4维RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞，并将其热力学控制量映射到共同的四次正规形，通过标度函数连接临界与高斯区域。

Result: 普通临界点由四次软模支配，温度贡献为1/4而非高斯的1/2；RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS结果与命题一致，并可用共同四次正规形描述，其标度函数沿零场单阱方向连接临界与高斯区。命题进一步推广到带权齐次领头势的耦合集体模式。

Conclusion: 该命题为约化系综涨落通过黑洞临界性提供了统一描述，将高斯与非高斯（四分之一温度）标度纳入同一框架，并可拓展至耦合模式。

Abstract: We establish a saddle-order proposition for the ensemble-averaged generalized free energy in reduced Euclidean black hole ensembles. Under regularity and stability assumptions, the leading fluctuation contribution is determined by the order of the first stabilizing term around the dominant saddle. An ordinary critical point is governed by a quartic soft mode, giving a quarter-temperature contribution instead of the familiar half-temperature Gaussian result. We verify this analytically for four-dimensional RN-AdS and Kerr-AdS black holes and map their thermodynamic controls to a common quartic normal form, whose scaling function connects the critical and Gaussian regimes along the zero-field one-well direction. The proposition also extends to coupled collective modes with weighted-homogeneous leading potentials. These results provide a unified description of reduced-ensemble fluctuations through black hole criticality.

</details>


### [130] [Spin-curvature effects in slowly rotating black hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24364)
*Arpita Jana,Subhajit Mazumdar,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究缓慢旋转黑洞时空中大质量自旋-1/2量子粒子的自旋-曲率相互作用，利用WKB近似与Gordon分解计算该相互作用产生的相对论量子力，并给出至旋转参数线性阶的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平直Minkowski空间中不存在曲率-自旋耦合，而弯曲时空会通过非平凡自旋联络在Dirac方程中引入直接的曲率-自旋相互作用，使粒子偏离测地线。为刻画黑洞自转对量子粒子自旋-曲率力的影响，作者在缓慢旋转黑洞背景下开展研究。

Method: 首先在缓慢旋转黑洞背景中计算粒子共动系的tetrad（四元标架）；随后基于WKB近似与Dirac概率四流的Gordon分解方法，计算由自旋-曲率相互作用产生的相对论量子力分量，结果保留至黑洞旋转参数a的线性阶。

Result: 结果表明，旋转参数通过引入一个随径向距离衰减更快的项来修正力的大小；该衰减项比静态球对称Schwarzschild黑洞时空中的力幅度随径向距离衰减得更迅速。

Conclusion: 黑洞自转在自旋-曲率力中引入了额外的、短程衰减更快的修正项，说明旋转效应显著改变了自旋-曲率相互作用的径向依赖特征，为区分旋转与静态黑洞情形下的量子自旋效应提供依据。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-curvature interaction for a massive spin-1/2 quantum particle in a slowly rotating black hole spacetime. In contrast to the flat Minkowski space, the curved spacetime introduces non-trivial spin connections into the Dirac equation due to the direct curvature-spin interaction. The force arising from this non-trivial spin-curvature coupling makes the particle deviate from its geodesics. We first calculate the tetrads in the co-moving frame of the particle in the slowly rotating black hole background. We then compute the components of the relativistic quantum force, arising from the spin-curvature interaction, using these co-moving tetrads up to linear order in the rotation parameter ($a$) of the black hole. The procedure of computing the force is based on the WKB approximation and the Gordon decomposition method for the Dirac probability four current. Our results show that the rotation parameter modifies the magnitude of the force by introducing a term that decays more rapidly with radial distance than the force magnitude in a static spherically symmetric Schwarzschild black hole spacetime.

</details>


### [131] [Measuring correlations using local and nonlocal quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24873)
*Alexey G. Mikhaylenko,Andrew G. Semenov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子淬火（quantum quench）测量量子场系统关联函数的理论方案：通过对系统施加短时扰动，使平均场的后续演化依赖于初态关联函数，从而可从演化中反推这些关联函数。以有质量与无质量标量场论为例，分别讨论局域与非局域淬火所能测量的关联函数类型，并指出该方法可用于研究非高斯关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场系统中关联函数（尤其是非高斯关联）的直接测量通常较困难，需要一种可行的实验可操作方案来提取初态的关联信息。

Method: 采用量子淬火策略：对系统施加短时扰动（quench），并观测平均场随后的时间演化。以有质量标量场论和无质量标量场论为具体模型，比较局域（local）淬火与非局域（nonlocal）淬火两种扰动类型，分析各自能够测量到哪些关联函数。

Result: 理论证明短时扰动会使平均场的后续演化依赖于初态的关联函数；并明确了局域淬火与非局域淬火分别可测量的关联函数种类。

Conclusion: 所提方案为测量量子场关联函数提供了理论基础，且可推广应用于研究非高斯关联。

Abstract: We present a theoretical proposal for measuring correlation functions in a quantum field system based on the use of quantum quenches. Using the examples of massive and massless scalar field theories, we show that a short-term perturbation of the system leads to a dependence of the further evolution of the average field on the initial state's correlation functions. Considering two types of perturbations, namely local and nonlocal quenches, we show which correlation functions can be measured in each of these cases. The proposed procedure can be applied to study non-Gaussian correlations.

</details>


### [132] [T-duality of string charge density/bit threads correspondence in type II supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24366)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究十维 II 型超引力中弦电荷密度/比特线对应的 Abelian T-对偶，验证其在 F1-NS5-P 与 P-NS5-F1、D1-D5-P 与 D0-D4-F1 背景间的非平凡不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦电荷密度与比特线（bit threads）的对应此前主要在特定背景中成立，尚缺乏对偶不变性的检验。T-对偶会改变局部度规、膨胀子与源流，因此是检验该对应是否稳健的理想工具，并可将其从纯弦流推广到包裹膜流。

Method: 沿公共紧致圆作 Abelian T-对偶：将 IIB 的 F1-NS5-P 映射为 IIA 的 P-NS5-F1（缠绕与动量电荷互换），将 D1-D5-P 映射为 D0-D4-F1。用归一化闭一刀形式收缩空间双矢量得到守恒的有效径向流，并计算最大通量。

Result: F1 源在对偶后仍为 F1 源；径向 D1 源变为缠绕于对偶圆的 D2 源，其收缩所得有效径向流的最大通量均复现黑洞熵。尽管局部度规、膨胀子、源电流与流范数改变，膨胀子加权的横截面积密度与积分最大通量保持不变。

Conclusion: 结果为弦电荷密度/比特线对应提供了非平凡的 T-对偶检验，并把该对应从弦电流扩展到可约化为类弦流的包裹膜电流。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Abelian T-duality of the string charge density/bit threads correspondence in ten-dimensional Type II supergravity. T-duality along the common compact circle maps the Type IIB F1-NS5-P background to a Type IIA P-NS5-F1 background with the winding and momentum charges exchanged. The radial F1 source remains an F1 source, and its projected charge density gives a bit-thread flow whose maximal flux reproduces the black hole entropy. The same duality maps D1-D5-P to D0-D4-F1. In this case, a radial D1 source becomes a D2 source wrapped on the dual circle. Contracting the resulting spatial bivector with the normalized closed one-form on that circle gives a conserved effective radial flow whose maximal flux again reproduces the entropy. Although the local metrics, dilatons, source currents, and flow norms change, the dilaton-weighted transverse area density and the integrated maximal flux are invariant. These results provide a nontrivial T-duality test of the correspondence and extend it from string currents to wrapped-brane currents that reduce to string-like flows.

</details>


### [133] [Dynamical Schwinger Production of Dipole--Antidipole Pairs in a Fractonic Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24415)
*Celio R. Muniz,R. N. Costa Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在一维偶极守恒Bose-Hubbard链中施加周期二次势（等效时变二阶电场），从单位填充的Mott背景出发，研究场诱导的激发出产。单次关联跳跃直接成核$|030\rangle$构型（能量$3U$），而由$2U$下移的流形包含空间分辨的偶极-反偶极构型。精确对角化表明有限跃迁使该低流形变为色散能带，其有限链边缘显著低于$3U$；但奇次谐波谱权重按$(J/U)^2$微扰压低。二阶处理$|1111\rangle\leftrightarrow|0220\rangle$通道给出弱场共振位移，经精确时间演化验证。扩展链动力学显示偶极-反偶极分离及向高能多体扇区的再分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多操纵预先存在的偶极或分形子（fracton）激发，而其从Mott态（无预制激发）中的产生机制尚不清楚。本文旨在探究在偶极守恒约束下，通过时变二阶电场从单位填充Mott背景产生激发的机制，并寻找多光子动力学Schwinger产生在凝聚态体系中的类比。

Method: 对一维偶极守恒Bose-Hubbard链施加周期二次势（时变二阶电场）。通过精确对角化分析激发流形的色散结构，采用微扰二阶处理分析$|1111\rangle\leftrightarrow|0220\rangle$通道（含自能对角移位），并用精确时间演化验证共振位移与驱动动力学。

Result: 单次关联跳跃成核$|030\rangle$（$3U$）；$2U$下移流形含偶极-反偶极构型，有限跃迁使其变成色散带，边缘低于$3U$。奇次谐波谱权重按$(J/U)^2$压低，源于初末本征态虚混合。二阶处理预测弱场共振位移，获精确演化确认。扩展链出现偶极-反偶极分离及向高能多体扇区的再分布。

Conclusion: 该机制提供了多光子动力学Schwinger产生的凝聚态类比：精确偶极守恒将可移动的相反电荷替换为可移动的相反偶极，而孤立的分形子电荷保持不动。

Abstract: We investigate field-induced excitation production from the unit-filled Mott background of a one-dimensional dipole-conserving Bose--Hubbard chain driven by a periodic quadratic potential, realizing a time-dependent rank-two electric field. Unlike protocols that manipulate pre-existing dipolar or fractonic excitations, we address their production from an initially Mott-like state without prepared dipolar or fractonic excitations. A single correlated hop directly nucleates the compact $|030\rangle$ configuration at the atomic energy $3U$, while a lower manifold derived from $2U$ contains spatially resolved dipole--antidipole configurations. Exact diagonalization shows that finite hopping turns this lower manifold into a dispersive band whose finite-chain edge lies substantially below the compact $3U$ scale. Despite this lower energetic edge, its normalized odd-harmonic spectral weight is perturbatively suppressed as $(J/U)^2$, reflecting virtual hybridization of the initial and final eigenstates. A consistent second-order treatment of the $|1111\rangle\leftrightarrow|0220\rangle$ channel includes diagonal self-energy shifts and predicts a weak-field resonance displacement confirmed by exact time evolution. Extended-chain dynamics shows genuine dipole--antidipole separation together with redistribution into higher-energy many-body sectors. The resulting mechanism provides a condensed-matter analogue of multiphoton dynamical Schwinger production in which exact dipole conservation replaces mobile opposite charges by mobile opposite dipoles, while isolated fractonic charges remain immobile.

</details>


### [134] [Unistochastic reduction of multi-matrix coherent-state kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24483)
*Shannon Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视多矩阵BPS关联子中出现的有限N相干态重叠积分，将其表示为单随机集上Haar推前测度的Laplace变换，并给出N=3的紧致Bessel积分表示及一般N的矩展开与采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限N相干态重叠积分在多矩阵BPS关联子中具有重要地位，但其显式结构尚未被充分理解。作者希望通过与单随机集及Haar推前测度建立联系，为这类积分提供新的几何与概率解释，并简化其计算。

Method: 将积分I_N(C)=∫_{U(N)}dμ_H(U) exp(∑_{ij}C_{ij}|U_{ij}|^2)解释为单随机集上Haar推前测度的Laplace变换；利用行、列随机性约束将源变量从N^2个约化为(N-1)^2个；对N=3，借助已知的单随机体上的Haar诱导测度得到三维Bessel积分表示；对一般N，使用Weingarten演算给出精确低阶矩展开，并设计直接的Haar推前采样算法。

Result: 证明了I_N(C)是单随机集上Haar推前测度的Laplace变换；源变量数由N^2减至(N-1)^2；对N=3获得紧凑的三维Bessel积分表示；对一般N给出精确的低阶矩展开以及可操作的Haar推前采样方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将有限N相干态重叠积分统一为单随机集上概率测度的Laplace变换，提供了新的几何视角与实用计算工具，既可用于解析矩计算，也可用于数值采样，对多矩阵BPS关联子的研究具有推进意义。

Abstract: We revisit the finite-$N$ coherent-state overlap integrals appearing in multi-matrix BPS correlators. For commuting coherent-state parameters, these integrals take the form $I_N(C)=\int_{U(N)}dμ_H(U)\, \exp\!\left(\sum_{ij}C_{ij}|U_{ij}|^2\right)$. We show that $I_N(C)$ is naturally the Laplace transform of the Haar-pushforward measure on the unistochastic set. Row and column stochasticity reduce the source variables from $N^2$ to $(N-1)^2$. For $N=3$, using the known Haar-induced measure on the unistochastic body, we obtain a compact three-dimensional Bessel-integral representation. For general finite $N$, the same formulation gives an exact low-order moment expansion via Weingarten calculus and a direct Haar-pushforward sampling program.

</details>


### [135] [Braneworld tidal charge through the classical double copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24521)
*Juan C. La Cruz,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Randall-Sundrum II模型中膜上旋转黑洞几何的经典双拷贝，发现单拷贝与零拷贝的正则源完全正比于潮汐电荷β，而质量贡献在正则区无源；潮汐电荷与Kerr-Newman电磁电荷平方存在形式对应，且电磁场存在时低能Kerr-Schild几何及其双拷贝仅通过组合Q²+β依赖两种贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典双拷贝框架下，探讨额外维度（bulk）对膜上有效几何的影响如何通过双拷贝结构体现，以及潮汐电荷与电磁电荷在双拷贝层面是否可区分，从而理解不同物理起源的贡献在有效几何双拷贝中能否变得不可分辨。

Method: 针对Randall-Sundrum II情景中膜上旋转黑洞几何，利用其Kerr-Schild表示（平坦背景），引入编码五维bulk影响的潮汐电荷β，构造对应的单拷贝和零拷贝，并与Kerr-Newman解进行对比；进一步在膜上加入电磁场，分析低能Kerr-Schild几何及其双拷贝中Q²与β的组合依赖。

Result: 单拷贝和零拷贝的正则源完全正比于潮汐电荷β，质量相关贡献在正则区无源；与Kerr-Newman对比显示，在诱导几何及其双拷贝层面，潮汐电荷与电磁电荷平方存在形式对应，尽管二者物理起源不同且β可取正负；当膜上包含电磁场时，两种贡献仅通过组合Q²+β进入低能Kerr-Schild几何及其双拷贝。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在有效几何的经典双拷贝中，物理上不同的膜与bulk贡献可能变得不可区分，凸显了双拷贝对有效几何中不同来源参数的合并特性。

Abstract: We investigate the classical double copy of the rotating black-hole geometry induced on the brane in the Randall-Sundrum II scenario. The influence of the five-dimensional bulk is encoded in the effective four-dimensional geometry through a tidal charge, while the metric admits a Kerr-Schild representation over a flat background. We construct the corresponding single and zeroth copies and find that their regular sources are entirely proportional to the tidal charge, whereas the mass-dependent contribution is source-free in the regular region. Comparison with Kerr-Newman reveals a formal correspondence between the tidal charge and the squared electromagnetic charge at the level of the induced geometry and its double copies, despite their different physical origins and the fact that the tidal charge may take either sign. When an electromagnetic field is included on the brane, both contributions enter the low-energy Kerr-Schild geometry and its double copies only through the combination $Q^2+β$. These results illustrate how physically distinct brane and bulk contributions can become indistinguishable in the classical double copy of an effective geometry.

</details>


### [136] [On the (In)equivalence of de Sitter and Holographic Entanglement Entropies in Lovelock Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24557)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究高曲率引力及其膜世界实现中de Sitter熵与全息纠缠熵的关系，发现二者在RS II模型的静态渐近de Sitter膜世界时空上一致，但在稳态时空中一般不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清高曲率引力中引力熵与全息熵之间的关系，并探究Lovelock理论及其膜世界实现中全息对应的更广泛图景。

Method: 采用引力作用量的正则表述与欧几里得引力路径积分，推导包含高曲率表面项贡献的de Sitter熵，并与由相应熵泛函得到的全息纠缠熵进行比较；先分析Gauss-Bonnet引力，再推广至一般Lovelock引力。

Result: 在Gauss-Bonnet引力中，两种熵在RS II模型的静态渐近de Sitter膜世界时空上一致，而在稳态时空中一般不同，差异源于与等时面相关的外曲率贡献——该贡献在静态时消失、稳态时非零；分析推广至一般Lovelock引力后，静态情形下的一致性依然成立；同样的静态/稳态区分也适用于膜世界黑洞。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了高曲率引力中引力熵与全息熵的关系，并为Lovelock理论及其膜世界实现中的全息对应提供了更广泛的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in higher-curvature gravity and its braneworld realization. Using the canonical formulation of the gravitational action and the Euclidean gravitational path integral, we derive the de Sitter entropy, including contributions from higher-curvature surface terms, and compare it with the holographic entanglement entropy obtained from the corresponding entropy functional.
  We first consider Gauss-Bonnet gravity and show that the two entropies coincide for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes on the RS II model, while they generally differ for stationary spacetimes. The mismatch in the stationary case arises from an extrinsic-curvature contribution associated with the constant-time surface, which vanishes for static configurations but is generally nonzero for stationary geometries.
  We then extend the analysis to general Lovelock gravity and show that the agreement between the de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy persists for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes in the RS II model beyond the Gauss-Bonnet case. We also comment that the same distinction between static and stationary configurations applies to braneworld black holes: the black hole entropy agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy in the static case, while the two generally differ for stationary braneworld black holes.
  Our results clarify the relation between gravitational and holographic entropies in higher-curvature gravity and provide a broader perspective on holographic correspondence in Lovelock theories and their braneworld realizations.

</details>


### [137] [The Gaiotto-Kim Trace Formula and Line Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24582)
*Sungjoon Kim,Spencer Stubbs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究将4d半BPS线缺陷插入Gaiotto-Kim迹公式，得到到3d Wilson环的同态并保持融合代数；给出确定3d TQFT简单对象的算法，并用超对称局域化刻画模S、T矩阵，应用于带高次单值性的(A1,G) Argyres-Douglas理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaiotto-Kim迹公式计算的3d TQFT是4d N=2超共形场论约化而来，其简单对象与模数据（S、T矩阵）的确定是理解该TQFT结构的关键；4d半BPS线缺陷的插入可提供与3d Wilson环的对应关系，从而系统刻画这些数据。

Method: 将4d半BPS线缺陷插入Gaiotto-Kim迹公式，使其映射为3d Wilson环并保持融合代数；提出基于UV 3d N=2阿贝尔Chern-Simons-物质理论中Wilson环来确定3d TQFT简单对象的系统算法；利用超对称局域化刻画模S、T矩阵。

Result: 建立了4d线缺陷到3d Wilson环的同态，保持融合代数；给出了确定3d TQFT简单对象的算法；通过超对称局域化得到模S、T矩阵；并将算法应用于带高次单值性的(A1,G) Argyres-Douglas理论所对应的多种3d TQFT。

Conclusion: 该工作为从4d N=2超共形场论约化得到的3d TQFT提供了确定简单对象和模数据的系统框架，并通过(A1,G) Argyres-Douglas理论的高次单值性情形进行了验证。

Abstract: We study the insertion of 4d half-BPS line defects into the trace formula proposed by Gaiotto and Kim, which computes the ellipsoid partition function of the 3d topological quantum field theory (TQFT) arising from a 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theory. This yields a homomorphism to 3d Wilson loops, preserving the fusion algebra of the 4d defects. We also present a systematic algorithm for determining the simple objects of a 3d TQFT in terms of Wilson loops in the UV 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian Chern-Simons-matter theory. Utilizing supersymmetric localization, we characterize the modular $S$- and $T$-matrices of the TQFT. We apply our algorithm to various 3d TQFTs arising from the trace formula with higher-power monodromy of the $(A_1,G)$ Argyres-Douglas theories.

</details>


### [138] [ABJM to BMN in a Double Scale Limit: Indices and Bubbling Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24649)
*Zhengyuan Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在双标度极限下，ABJM理论的单极子电荷扇区与BMN矩阵模型建立精确对应，并通过引力与超对称指标两条路径予以证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究ABJM理论在双标度极限（N,k→∞, N/k²→ν）下固定单极子电荷扇区的结构，旨在揭示其与BMN矩阵模型的内在联系，为理解AdS/CFT对应中可积性与矩阵模型提供新视角。

Method: 引力侧：取Donos–Simón半BPS几何的Hopf商的双标度极限，分析盘面通量向无限导电平面的转变及有限盘面重现Lin–Maldacena静电问题；场论侧：从有限N、k的ABJM局域化公式出发，证明超共形指标在横向逸度中逐系数分解为中性贡献与秩q BMN矩阵模型的精炼Witten指标。

Result: 双标度极限下，Penrose几何保留有限半径的紧致零圆；单极子电荷q扇区携带q单位纵向动量，自然对应秩q BMN矩阵模型，ν固定其无量纲耦合。量化通量直接映射标记BMN真空的配分数据；中性贡献恰为零单极子电荷极限的指标，除以该普适因子可分离出求和所有超对称真空的BMN指标。

Conclusion: ABJM理论在双标度极限下的固定单极子电荷扇区与秩q BMN矩阵模型精确等价，该关系在引力与超对称指标两侧均得到验证，且通过除以普适中性因子可孤立提取BMN指标。

Abstract: We study a double scale limit of $U(N)_k\times U(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory, with $N,k\to\infty$ and $N/k^2\toν\in(0,\infty)$, at fixed positive total monopole charge $q$. In this limit, the Penrose geometry retains a compact null circle of finite radius, and the sector of monopole charge $q$ carries $q$ units of longitudinal momentum. It is therefore naturally associated with the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, while $ν$ fixes its dimensionless coupling. We establish this relation from both gravity and the supersymmetric index. On the gravity side, we take the double scale limit of Hopf quotients of the Donos--Simón half-BPS geometries. The disk carrying the growing background flux becomes an infinite conducting plane, while the remaining finite disks reproduce the Lin--Maldacena electrostatic problem. Their quantized fluxes map directly to the partition data labeling BMN vacua. On the field-theory side, starting from the finite-$N$, finite-$k$ ABJM localization formula, we prove that, at fixed positive monopole charge $q$, the ABJM superconformal index factorizes coefficientwise in the transverse fugacities into a universal neutral contribution and the refined Witten index of the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, together with the longitudinal momentum weight. The neutral contribution is precisely the limiting zero-monopole-charge index. Dividing by this universal factor therefore isolates the BMN index summed over all its supersymmetric vacua.

</details>


### [139] [Finite-Time Doppler Covariance Spectroscopy in the AdS/CFT correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24806)
*Feiyi Liu,Shiyang Chen,Yang Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个全息框架下的有限时长协方差方案，在旋转BTZ黑洞时空中利用边界控制相对运动产生的速度共振，从完整正半定协方差矩阵中重构成对响应，并以归一化对比度估计内禀浴手性与BTZ旋转参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS/CFT中手性/旋转信息的提取需要一种不依赖控制诱导运动学、能够分离内禀浴手性的有限时间协方差表征方法，以定量估计旋转BTZ黑洞参数。

Method: 在旋转BTZ黑洞时空下建立精确有限时长协方差框架：利用两边界控制相对运动将紧致边界柱上的和频匹配转化为按标量场角动量索引的离散速度共振；用双设置正交循环消除差频扇区，从完整正半定协方差矩阵重构共振成对贡献；对孤立角模，在相反共振速度处采样边界谱，通过归一化对比度消除反射对称控制因子与状态无关算符归一化，得到内禀浴手性的有限线宽估计；并用实投影泄漏界控制邻模污染。

Result: 对平滑浴谱，居中偶对称窄化窗口引起的偏差从线宽二次阶开始，保护超出高斯采样；长脉冲计算用精确BTZ谱验证相位循环重构和二次线宽标度；浴加权计算用精确截断转移振幅确立向基线的收敛；默认控制下前两个非零角模可从完整角动量求和提取并以亚百分比确定性偏差重构BTZ旋转参数。

Conclusion: 该框架将控制诱导的共振运动学与内禀浴手性分离，把有限时间协方差数据转化为旋转BTZ黑洞的定量估计，为手性分辨的AdS/CFT协方差测量提供路径。

Abstract: From a holographic perspective, we formulate an exact finite-duration covariance framework that resolves chirality in the AdS/CFT correspondence. In a rotating BTZ black hole spacetime, relative motion between two boundary controls converts sum-frequency matching on the compact boundary cylinder into discrete velocity resonances indexed by the angular momentum of the scalar field. Because the complete real-control covariance also contains difference-frequency sectors, a two-setting quadrature cycle cancels them and reconstructs the resonant pair contribution from complete positive-semidefinite covariance matrices. For an isolated angular mode, reconstructed pair responses at opposite resonance velocities sample the boundary spectrum at opposite angular momentum and a common control-selected frequency. Their normalized contrast cancels reflection-symmetric control factors and the state-independent operator normalization, yielding a finite-linewidth estimator of intrinsic bath chirality. For a smooth bath spectrum, the bias from common centered even narrowing windows begins at quadratic order in linewidth, extending the protection beyond Gaussian sampling. An explicit real-projection leakage bound controls contamination from neighboring modes. Long-pulse calculations with the exact BTZ spectrum verify phase-cycle reconstruction and quadratic linewidth scaling. Bath-weighted calculations with exact truncated transfer amplitudes establish convergence to this baseline. Under the default controls, the first two nonzero angular modes support extraction from the complete angular sum and reconstruct the BTZ rotation parameter with subpercent deterministic bias. The framework thus separates control-induced resonance kinematics from intrinsic bath chirality and converts finite-time covariance data into a quantitative estimate of the rotating BTZ black hole.

</details>


### [140] [The Generalized Uncertainty Principle. Massless QED Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24817)
*Ezequiel Valero,Héctor Gisbert,Victor Ilisie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无质量GUP形变量子电动力学中，计算一圈、形变参数一阶的紫外发散与抵消项结构，发现重整化只需一个独立物理抵消项（轴矢-轴矢四费米相互作用），并给出诱导Wilson系数的对数跑动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义不确定性原理（GUP）的有效场论解释本身无法判断其树级算符内容在重整化下是否封闭，需要检验形变理论的自洽性。

Method: 在无质量GUP形变量子电动力学中，取一圈阶和形变参数一阶，计算相关Green函数的紫外发散，确定抵消项结构，并在消除冗余算符后分析物理抵消项。

Result: 消除冗余算符后，除树级理论对应的抵消项外，仅需一个独立物理抵消项：轴矢-轴矢四费米相互作用；由此导出了诱导Wilson系数的领头对数跑动，并讨论其与Einstein-Cartan引力中接触相互作用的算符层面对应。

Conclusion: 该GUP形变在一圈、形变参数一阶下允许一个最小辐射完成，其诱导的轴矢-轴矢四费米算符与Einstein-Cartan引力中的接触相互作用存在对应关系。

Abstract: The effective-field-theory interpretation of generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) deformations does not by itself establish whether their tree-level operator content is closed under renormalization. We investigate this question in massless GUP-deformed quantum electrodynamics at one loop and to first order in the deformation parameter. We compute the ultraviolet divergences of the relevant Green functions and determine the counterterm structure. Our central result is that, after eliminating redundant operators, renormalization requires only one independent physical counterterm beyond those associated with the tree-level theory: an axial-axial four-fermion interaction. The original deformation therefore admits a minimal radiative completion at this order. We derive the leading logarithmic running of the induced Wilson coefficient and discuss its operator-level correspondence with the contact interaction arising in Einstein-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [141] [Gauss--Bonnet running and the de Sitter saddle of quadratic gravity inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24912)
*Ruolin Liu,Niayesh Afshordi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准单圈重整化下，包含通常被忽略的Gauss–Bonnet系数，可恢复二次引力的de Sitter态：Euler项跑动平衡$R^2$项的标度依赖，形成唯一耦合比下的de Sitter解，其scalaron势为极平坦山顶加上逆线性暴胀平台，预言$r\gtrsim0.008$、$0.973\lesssim n_s\lesssim0.978$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前由二次引力曲率平方耦合的量子跑动驱动的暴胀能解释CMB观测偏好的高标量倾斜，但其出发点是Euclidean四球面上$R^2$耦合跑动的极大值。该极大值只在一个重整化方案中存在，且纳入Euler迹反常后不再稳定，因此需要寻找不依赖特定方案、更稳健的de Sitter态来源。

Method: 在标准单圈跑动中保留通常被忽略的Gauss–Bonnet系数，考察Euler项跑动对$R^2$项标度依赖的平衡。寻找同时满足引力约束与紧致Euclidean作用量平稳性的唯一耦合比下的de Sitter解，并计算其scalaron势、标量倾斜$n_s$、张标比$r$以及方案独立性。

Result: 发现唯一耦合比下存在平稳de Sitter解，其scalaron势为极平坦山顶（$m^2/H^2\simeq-5\times10^{-10}$）连接逆线性暴胀平台。最后$N_*\simeq50$–$60$个e-folds方案独立，$n_s\simeq1-4/(3N_*)$，$r$由增强跑动的物质场数量决定。当前张量上界排除纯引力并给出最小物质含量：约$4\times10^6$个共形耦合标量或$3\times10^5$个矢量，比动量诱导方案所需少2.6倍；单圈控制到暴胀结束给出约高十倍的上限。该窗口内预言$r\gtrsim0.008$、$0.973\lesssim n_s\lesssim0.978$。

Conclusion: 保留Gauss–Bonnet系数的标准单圈跑动可稳健地恢复方案独立的de Sitter暴胀态，解决原极大值方案依赖与不稳定性问题。模型预言落在未来CMB偏振巡天可及范围内，同时给出物质含量的最小与最大窗口约束。

Abstract: Inflation driven purely by the quantum running of the curvature-squared couplings of quadratic gravity was shown to accommodate the high scalar tilt favored by recent cosmic microwave background observations, starting from a Euclidean four-sphere at a maximum of the running $R^2$ coupling. That maximum exists only in one renormalization scheme, and is not stationary once the Euler trace anomaly is included. We show that the standard one-loop running, with the usually neglected Gauss--Bonnet coefficient retained, recovers the missing de Sitter state: the Euler running balances the scale dependence of the $R^2$ term at a unique coupling ratio, giving a de Sitter solution that is stationary for the gravitational constraint and for the compact Euclidean action alike, and whose scalaron potential is an extremely flat hilltop ($m^2/H^2\simeq-5\times10^{-10}$) joined to an inverse-linear inflationary plateau. The last $N_*\simeq50$--$60$ $e$-folds are scheme independent, with scalar tilt $n_s\simeq1-4/(3N_*)$, while the tensor-to-scalar ratio, $r$, is set by the number of matter fields that enhance the running. The current tensor bound excludes pure gravity and sets a minimum matter content, some $4\times10^6$ conformally coupled scalars or $3\times10^5$ vectors---$2.6$ times fewer than the momentum-induced scheme requires---while one-loop control to the end of inflation sets a maximum about ten times higher. Across that window the model predicts $r\gtrsim0.008$ with $0.973\lesssim n_s\lesssim0.978$, within reach of upcoming CMB polarization surveys.

</details>


### [142] [Chiral Carroll string near Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24939)
*Kedar S. Kolekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从相对论性闭玻色弦作用量出发，按光速 $c$ 的幂次做 Carroll 展开，得到手征 Carroll 弦在弯曲 string-Carroll 靶时空中的作用量，并将其应用于接近 4 维 Schwarzschild 黑洞的经典闭弦，研究其近视界动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弦在黑洞视界附近的极端相对论/非相对论极限行为，利用 Carroll 极限（$c\to 0$）刻画视界处的因果结构，探索手征 Carroll 弦作为近视界弦动力学的有效描述。

Method: 对相对论性闭玻色弦作用量按光速 $c$ 的幂次进行 Carroll 展开，导出弯曲 string-Carroll 靶时空中手征 Carroll 弦的作用量；随后将该框架应用于趋近 4 维 Schwarzschild 黑洞的经典闭弦，以近视界 string-Carroll 展开几何作为靶时空求解其动力学。

Result: 手征 Carroll 弦在视界上只允许左行或右行的涨落模式，同时在 Rindler 时空中表现出非平凡运动，且该运动依赖于这些视界涨落。

Conclusion: Carroll 展开为近视界弦动力学提供了有效描述：视界涨落呈手征性（单向传播），而 Rindler 区域的运动由这些手征视界涨落所决定，揭示了黑洞视界附近弦运动的独特结构。

Abstract: We obtain the action for the chiral Carroll string moving in a curved string-Carroll target spacetime from a Carroll expansion, in powers of the speed of light $c$, of the action for the relativistic closed bosonic string. Applying this setup to a classical closed bosonic string approaching the $4$-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole, we study the dynamics of the string when it behaves as a chiral Carroll string with the near-horizon string-Carroll expanded geometry as the target spacetime. We find that the chiral Carroll string can have either left-moving or right-moving fluctuations on the horizon, while exhibiting a non-trivial motion in the Rindler spacetime which depends on these horizon fluctuations.

</details>


### [143] [Towards All-Order Locality in the Higher-Spin Holomorphic Zero-Form Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24956)
*D. A. Valerev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究从Vasiliev方程得到的全纯零形式扇区中局域相互作用顶点的构造，导出了保证全纯零形式顶点存在的必要充分条件，并具体计算了自旋局域的全纯η²零形式扇区顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自旋理论中局域顶点的系统构造是关键问题。此前工作（arXiv:2208.02004）仅以较宽松形式提出了零形式顶点的要求，且只建立了旋量自旋局域性，尚未实现时空自旋局域性及具体顶点计算。

Method: 聚焦于Vasiliev方程全纯零形式扇区，在旋量自旋局域性基础上，对全纯零形式顶点施加更严格的附加要求（该要求可保证时空自旋局域性），推导全纯一形式扇区顶点需满足的必要充分条件，以确保满足该要求的全纯零形式顶点存在，并将其应用于计算全纯η²零形式扇区顶点。

Result: 得到了全纯一形式扇区顶点存在的必要充分条件；成功计算出自旋局域的全纯η²零形式扇区顶点，超越了此前仅建立旋量自旋局域性的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为高自旋理论中局域顶点的系统全阶构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work addresses the construction of local interaction vertices in the holomorphic zero-form sector obtained from Vasiliev equations. We focus on a requirement on zero-form vertices, previously proposed in a less restrictive form in arXiv:2208.02004, which, together with spinor spin-locality, guarantees space-time spin-locality. Necessary and sufficient conditions on the holomorphic one-form sector vertices are derived that ensure the existence of holomorphic zero-form vertices satisfying this requirement. As a concrete demonstration, the proposed scheme is applied to compute the spin-local holomorphic $η^2$ zero-form sector vertex, a result that goes beyond previous work where only spinor spin-locality was established. These findings lay the groundwork for a systematic all-order construction of local vertices in higher-spin theory.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [A codimension formula for intrinsic perturbation of $A$-hypergeometric series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22414)
*Ryunosuke Nakano*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究齐次A-超几何系统在一般方向上的典范形式解空间，给出其关于内蕴扰动所得典范级数张成空间的有限维余核表示与余维数计算公式，并将结论推广至全纯解；对格秩2、5q列的齐次A-超几何系统，证明该张成空间余维数至少为⌈3q²/4⌉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究齐次A-超几何系统解空间中，由内蕴扰动生成的典范级数所张成的子空间的结构与余维数，进而量化这些典范级数对解空间的"覆盖"程度。

Method: 以一般方向下齐次A-超几何系统的典范形式解空间为对象，考虑所有出现在该空间中的指数与所有有序负支集族所对应的内蕴扰动典范级数，用其张成空间的商空间构造有限维余核表示，并计算该张成空间的余维数；进而通过共有的非奇异域将表示与余维数结果转移到全纯解上。

Result: 给出解空间对典范级数张成空间之商的有限维余核表示，并计算其余维数；证明该表示与余维数可转移到公共非奇异域上的全纯解。对任意整数q≥1，针对格秩2、5q列的齐次A-超几何系统，证明内蕴扰动所得典范级数的张成空间在典范形式解空间中的余维数至少为⌈3q²/4⌉。

Conclusion: 内蕴扰动生成的典范级数张成空间在典范形式解空间中具有可计算的有限余维数，且该余维数下界随q呈二次增长（至少⌈3q²/4⌉），说明这些典范级数仅覆盖解空间的一部分。

Abstract: We study the canonical formal solution space of a homogeneous $A$-hypergeometric system in a generic direction. We present as a finite-dimensional cokernel the quotient of that space by the span of the canonical series obtained by intrinsic perturbation, taken over all exponents occurring in that space and all ordered negative support families, and we compute the codimension of that span. We show that the presentation and the codimension transfer to the holomorphic solutions on a common nonsingular domain. We apply these results, for every integer $q\geq 1$, to a homogeneous $A$-hypergeometric system of lattice rank 2 with $5q$ columns and show that the span of the canonical series obtained by intrinsic perturbation has codimension at least $\lceil 3q^2/4\rceil$ in the canonical formal solution space.

</details>


### [145] [Local monodromy, Behrend function and Hilbert scheme of points on $\mathbb{C}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22469)
*Yunfeng Jiang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出 $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ 上 Behrend 函数值的一个判定准则，基于局部单值性与 Denef–Loeser 喷流空间的 Lefschetz 迹公式，并据此给出该 Behrend 函数非常值的 Milnor 纤维-单值性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Behrend 函数在 $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ 上的取值是否恒定是一个自然问题，Jelisiejew–Kool–Schmiermann 已证其非常值。本文旨在从 Milnor 纤维与单值性这一更几何的角度，为此现象提供新的判定准则和证明途径。

Method: 利用局部单值性与 Denef–Loeser 关于喷流空间的 Lefschetz 迹公式，建立 $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ 上 Behrend 函数值的判定准则；当单值的二、三、四次幂分别 unipotent 时，将其分别化归为 Hessian 奇偶公式、三次补公式与四次公式。

Result: 得到了 Behrend 函数值的判定准则，并在单值幂为 unipotent 的情形下分别给出 Hessian 奇偶公式、三次补公式和四次公式；由此给出了 $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ 上 Behrend 函数非常值的 Milnor 纤维与单值性证明。

Conclusion: 该工作以 Milnor 纤维和单值性为基础，重新证明了 Jelisiejew–Kool–Schmiermann 关于 $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ 上 Behrend 函数非常值的结论，并提供了相应的显式公式框架。

Abstract: We prove a criteria for the Behrend function values on the Hilbert scheme of $n$-points on $\C^3$ from local monodromy and the Denef--Loeser's Lefschetz trace formula of jet spaces. If the powers two, three, and four of the monodromy is unipotent, it reduces respectively to a Hessian parity formula, a cubic-complement formula, and a quartic formula. This gives a Milnor fiber and monodromy based proof of nonconstancy of the Behrend function on $\Hilb^n(\C^3)$ after Jelisiejew-Kool-Schmiermann.

</details>


### [146] [Maximal singular curves over finite fields from elliptic and hyperelliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22557)
*Cesar Hilario,Rodrigo Salomão,Renato Vidal Martins*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何嵌入的线性投影构造了有限域上显式的极大奇异曲线族，并确定了所得曲线的亏格（gonality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大奇异曲线是达到Aubry-Perret界的曲线，该界是Hasse-Weil-Serre界在奇异曲线情形下的自然推广；作者希望获得有限域上显式的极大奇异曲线族并研究其性质。

Method: 从有限域F_q（q为奇数）上的光滑椭圆曲线或超椭圆曲线C̃出发，利用几何嵌入的线性投影，并结合超椭圆Gorenstein曲线的Stöur嵌入以及三角Gorenstein曲线的Rosa-Stöur理论，生成具有非分裂节点或尖点的奇异曲线C；该方法应用于Tafazolian及Tafazolian-Torres光滑极大曲线。

Result: 构造出有限域上显式的极大奇异曲线族，这些曲线具有非分裂节点或尖点，并达到Aubry-Perret界；同时确定了所有所得曲线的gonality。

Conclusion: 几何嵌入线性投影结合Gorenstein曲线嵌入理论是构造极大奇异曲线的有效途径，所构造曲线族为极大奇异曲线提供了显式例子，其gonality也已完全确定。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of maximal singular curves over finite fields. A maximal singular curve is a curve that attains the Aubry-Perret bound, which is the natural extension of the Hasse-Weil-Serre bound on the maximum number of rational points on a smooth curve over a finite field.Starting from a smooth elliptic or hyper elliptic curve $\tilde{C}$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q}$ ($q$ odd), we generate singular curves $C$ with non-split nodes or cusps. In our approach we use linear projections of geometric embeddings, and we apply Stöur's embedding of hyperelliptic Gorenstein curves and the Rosa-Stöur theory of trigonal Gorenstein curves. The construction is applied to the Tafazolian and Tafazolian-Torres smooth maximal curves. Finally, we also determine the gonality of all obtained curves.

</details>


### [147] [Special valuations and automorphisms of affine log Calabi--Yau varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22737)
*Yuchen Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 仿射对数Calabi–Yau簇的特殊骨架与紧化选择无关且在自同构下不变；有限生成赋值特殊当且仅当关于自然偏序为极大。对Markov三次曲面补的三维仿射对数CY，描述三个Vieta对合在特殊骨架上的作用并与双曲平面上的(∞,∞,∞)-三角反射群关联；证明任意有限三角剖分下特殊骨架在某个单纯形上非局部闭，经锥构造给出对作者与Xu猜想的一个反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有定义依赖对数CY–Fano紧化，需明确特殊骨架是否内蕴（不依赖紧化并在Aut(U)下不变），并刻画特殊赋值的序论特征；同时需研究具体三维例子以检验相关猜想。

Method: 利用对偶复形与骨架构造，证明特殊/有限生成赋值定义的骨架与紧化选择无关；用U上正则函数诱导的自然偏序刻画特殊赋值（极大性）；研究A^3中Markov三次曲面补的仿射对数CY三维体，分析三个Vieta对合在特殊骨架上的作用并与(∞,∞,∞)-三角反射群对应；对任意有限三角剖分考察特殊骨架的局部闭性，并用锥构造给出反例。

Result: 特殊骨架与对数CY–Fano紧化选择无关，因而Aut(U)-不变；有限生成赋值特殊的充要条件是关于自然偏序极大；在Markov三次曲面补的三维情形，Vieta对合作用与双曲平面(∞,∞,∞)-三角反射群相关；任意有限三角剖分下，特殊骨架在某单纯形上非局部闭；经锥构造得到对作者与Xu猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 特殊骨架是U的内蕴不变量，特殊赋值可由自然偏序极大性刻画；具体三维例子揭示了与反射群的关联并给出非局部闭现象，从而否定相关猜想。

Abstract: Let $U$ be an affine log Calabi--Yau variety. Although special and finitely generated valuations are defined using a log CY-Fano compactification of $U$, we prove that the corresponding skeleta in the dual complex are independent of this choice, and hence invariant under $\mathrm{Aut}(U)$. We also show that a finitely generated valuation is special if and only if it is maximal with respect to a natural partial order induced by regular functions on $U$.
  We then consider the affine log Calabi--Yau threefold obtained as the complement of the Markov cubic surface in $\mathbb{A}^3$. We describe the action of the three Vieta involutions on the special skeleton and relate it to the $(\infty,\infty,\infty)$-triangle reflection group on the hyperbolic plane. We also show that, for every finite triangulation of the dual complex, the special skeleton fails to be locally closed on some simplex. Via the cone construction, this provides a counterexample to a conjecture of the author and Xu.

</details>


### [148] [On the Rank of Normal Functions on Pointed Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22901)
*Lorenzo Fassina*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究指向曲线模空间上一类自然正规函数，证明其秩在阿贝尔微分对应层外达到最大，在该层上恰好下降1，由此刻画双分歧轨迹的局部几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解指向曲线模空间上一类自然正规函数的秩行为，并借助秩的退化规律来揭示相关双分歧轨迹的局部几何结构。

Method: 在指向曲线的模空间上考察一类自然正规函数，逐点计算其秩：在对应的阿贝尔微分层之外证明秩取最大值，而在该层之上证明秩恰好下降一。

Result: 该族正规函数的秩在阿贝尔微分对应层外是最大的，且沿该层恰好下降1。

Conclusion: 由上述秩的精确退化结果，可刻画相应双分歧轨迹的局部几何。

Abstract: We study a natural class of normal functions on the moduli space of pointed curves and determine their rank at every point. We prove that the rank is maximal outside the corresponding stratum of Abelian differentials, while along this stratum it drops by exactly one. As a consequence, we describe the local geometry of the associated double ramification locus.

</details>


### [149] [Local cohomology with Schubert support on compact Hermitian symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22960)
*Michael Perlman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致Hermitian对称空间中Schubert簇支撑的局部上同调层，给出其作为混合Hodge模的D-单合成因子与权重滤过的统一根论构造，并翻译为组合规则，应用于对称行列式与Pfaffian簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧致Hermitian对称空间中Schubert簇上的局部上同调层携带混合Hodge模结构，但其合成因子与权重滤过缺乏跨Lie型的统一显式描述，尤其缺少Lagrangian、正交Grassmannian及例外Hermitian对的组合公式。

Method: 采用根论方法，跨Lie型统一构造D-单合成因子与权重滤过；对Lagrangian与正交Grassmannian，将公式翻译为涉及严格划分与shifted Dyck图案的组合规则；并对所有Schubert簇计算Hodge有理同调与局部上同调亏格。

Result: 得到跨Lie型的显式根论构造；在Lagrangian与正交Grassmannian上给出严格划分与shifted Dyck图案的组合规则；推广Raicu–Weyman工作，描述对称行列式与Pfaffian簇支撑的局部上同调权重滤过；给出每个Schubert簇的Hodge有理同调公式与局部上同调亏格；并列出两个例外Hermitian对中所有Schubert簇的局部上同调权重滤过表。

Conclusion: 该工作为紧致Hermitian对称空间中局部上同调层提供了统一、显式且可组合计算的混合Hodge模描述，扩展了经典Grassmannian上的相应公式，并覆盖例外Lie型。

Abstract: We consider local cohomology sheaves on compact Hermitian symmetric spaces supported in Schubert varieties, which carry natural structures as mixed Hodge modules. Our main result gives an explicit root-theoretic construction, uniform across Lie types, for the D-simple composition factors and weight filtration on these modules. For Lagrangian and orthogonal Grassmannians, we translate these formulas into combinatorial rules involving strict partitions and shifted Dyck patterns, analogous to existing formulas on ordinary Grassmannians. As an application, we expand upon work of Raicu--Weyman to describe the weight filtration on local cohomology supported in symmetric determinantal and Pfaffian varieties. We also give formulas for the Hodge rational homology level and local cohomological defect of every Schubert variety in a compact Hermitian symmetric space. Finally, we tabulate the local cohomology weight filtrations for all Schubert varieties in the two exceptional Hermitian pairs.

</details>


### [150] [On the gap between the integral and rational Gongyo indices of toric Fano varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22997)
*Hiroshi Sato*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究光滑环面Fano簇上有理Gongyo指标与整Gongyo指标之差（gap），给出依赖维数的下界猜想，并在伪对称情形证明该下界及等号分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维数不超过7的光滑环面Fano簇的精确计算暗示了一个依赖维数的、关于Gongyo指标gap的尖锐下界，作者希望证明并刻画该下界。

Method: 对由反规范极小模型纲领对射影空间在环面不变点处爆破所得的光滑环面Fano模型计算两个指标；对伪对称光滑环面Fano簇证明所提下界并确定等号情形；应用提升维数的构造从奇数维弱Fano簇（gap为零）得到具有正gap的无穷族。

Result: 证明了伪对称光滑环面Fano簇的依赖维数下界并分类等号情形；构造出具有正gap的无穷族光滑环面Fano簇；确定了标准模型中通过迭代该构造获得Fano簇所需的最小维数增量，并计算了极小提升的两个指标；证明每个正有理数都可作为Gongyo指标gap出现，即使在伪对称类中亦然。

Conclusion: Gongyo指标gap受维数制约且下界在伪对称类中可达；gap可取任意正有理数值，说明其取值集合在有理数中稠密/无界。
