Abstract: Exact calculations for smooth toric Fano varieties in dimensions at most seven suggest a sharp dimension-dependent lower bound for the positive difference between the rational and integral Gongyo indices. We compute both indices for the smooth toric Fano models obtained from blow-ups of projective space at torus-invariant points by the anticanonical minimal model program. For pseudo-symmetric smooth toric Fano varieties, we prove the proposed lower bound and determine all equality cases. We also apply a dimension-raising construction to obtain an explicit infinite family of smooth toric Fano varieties with positive gap from odd-dimensional weak Fano varieties with zero gap. For the standard models considered, we determine the smallest dimension increase needed to obtain a Fano variety by iterating this construction and compute both indices of the minimal lifts. Finally, every positive rational number occurs as a Gongyo-index gap, even within the pseudo-symmetric class.

</details>


### [151] [A polynomial formula for the Albanese map of the Cartwright-Steger surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23124)
*Zoltán Szabó*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 给出了Cartwright--Steger曲面的Albanese纤维化（亏格19的Lefschetz纤维化）的显式公式：在Borisov--Yeung射影模型中由三个13次整系数齐次多项式给出，目标为椭圆曲线Y²Z=X³−3888Z³，并确定三个结点的精确坐标及其像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartwright--Steger曲面的Albanese纤维化已知是亏格19、底为环面的Lefschetz纤维化，但缺乏显式描述；本文旨在给出该纤维化的具体显式公式及相关几何数据。

Method: 在Borisov--Yeung射影模型中，用三个13次整系数齐次多项式表示该映射；借助识别定理将验证归结为曲面上一个多项式恒等式；利用系数界，通过模若干素数的计算在Q上验证该恒等式；并计算三个结点的精确坐标及其在椭圆曲线上的像。

Result: 得到Albanese纤维化的显式公式：目标为椭圆曲线Y²Z=X³−3888Z³，映射由三个13次整系数齐次多项式给出；确定了三个结点的精确坐标及其在椭圆曲线上的像。

Conclusion: 该工作为Cartwright--Steger曲面的Albanese纤维化提供了完全显式的描述，并展示了识别定理与模素数验证相结合的有效性。

Abstract: The Albanese fibration of the Cartwright--Steger surface is a genus-$19$ Lefschetz fibration over a torus. We give an explicit formula for it: in the projective model of Borisov and Yeung, the map is represented by three homogeneous polynomials of degree $13$ with integer coefficients, and its target is the elliptic curve $Y^2Z=X^3-3888Z^3$. A recognition theorem shows that it suffices to verify one polynomial identity on the surface. A coefficient bound allows us to establish the identity over $Q$ from computations modulo primes. We also determine exact coordinates for the three nodes and their images on the elliptic curve.

</details>


### [152] [Restricted monodromy containing transpositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23238)
*Julianne Barnhart,Taylor Brysiewicz,Michael Burr,Thomas Yahl*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将分支覆盖的monodromy群与其限制的monodromy群相联系，证明受限monodromy群中出现对换会显著约束原分支覆盖的monodromy群，并据此完善稀疏迹检验算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏迹检验算法用于数值判定稀疏多项式系统解集的完备性，但其正确性依赖于对monodromy群结构的理解，而分支覆盖与其限制之间的monodromy群关系尚不明确。

Method: 建立分支覆盖的monodromy群与各限制（restriction）的monodromy群之间的联系；证明若某个受限monodromy群含有对换（transposition），则原分支覆盖的monodromy群受到强约束；并将迹检验的概念推广到分支覆盖框架下。

Result: 证明了对换存在性对原monodromy群的强约束性定理；给出了分支覆盖意义下迹检验的推广；完成了稀疏迹检验算法的理论补全。

Conclusion: 分支覆盖与其限制的monodromy群之间的对换约束为稀疏迹检验提供了严格理论基础，使该数值完备性验证算法在理论上得以完善。

Abstract: We relate the monodromy group of a branched cover to the monodromy groups of its restrictions. We show that the presence of a transposition in a restricted monodromy group significantly constrains the monodromy group of the original branched cover. Using this result, we complete the sparse trace test algorithm, which numerically verifies whether a set of solutions to a sparse polynomial system is complete. Along the way, we develop a generalization of the notion of a trace test in the setting of branched covers.

</details>


### [153] [Semiampleness on Jacobian elliptic surfaces of Kodaira dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23306)
*Antonio Laface,Sichen Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究半稳定 Jacobian 椭圆曲面在 $N(\pi)\ge 2\chi+4$ 时成为 Mori dream surface 的存在性问题，给出 nef isotropic divisor 半丰沛性、偶数纤维情形下的判定准则，并构造反例说明奇数纤维情形可失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laface 等人已证明当 $\delta(\pi)\le\chi$ 时零截面与可约纤维分支生成 $\overline{\mathrm{NE}}(X)$，且 $N(\pi)\le 2\chi+3$ 时 $X$ 为 Mori dream surface。本文关注边界之外，即 $N(\pi)\ge 2\chi+4$ 时 Mori dream surface 的存在性问题。

Method: 设 $\pi: X\to\mathbb P^1$ 为 $\mathbb C$ 上半稳定 Jacobian 椭圆曲面，$\chi=\chi(\mathcal O_X)\ge3$，假设 Mordell-Weil 群有限且至少有一个可约纤维，可约纤维类型为 $I_{n_1},\dots,I_{n_s}$，并令 $\delta(\pi)=\sum_{i=1}^s \lfloor n_i^2/4\rfloor/n_i$。在 $\delta(\pi)\le\chi$ 下，分析 nef isotropic divisor 的半丰沛性；在各 $n_i$ 为偶数时建立 $X$ 为 Mori dream surface 的判别准则；当某 $n_i$ 为奇数时，构造具体 Jacobian 椭圆曲面反例。

Result: 证明每个 nef isotropic divisor 均为 semiample（当各 $n_i$ 为偶数时）；得到准则：若各 $n_i$ 为偶数，则当 (i) 所有 $n_i=2$ 或 (ii) $N(\pi)\le 2\chi+4$ 时 $X$ 为 Mori dream surface；若某个 $n_j=4$，则 $X$ 为 Mori dream surface 当且仅当 $N(\pi)\le 2\chi+4$。此外，当某个 $n_i$ 为奇数时，构造出满足 $\delta(\pi)=\chi=3$、Mordell-Weil 群平凡、奇异纤维构型为 $I_4+3I_3+23I_1$ 的 Jacobian 椭圆曲面 $Y\to\mathbb P^1$，其八个非竖直 isotropic extremal rays 均非 semiample，故 $Y$ 不是 Mori dream surface。

Conclusion: 在 $N(\pi)\ge 2\chi+4$ 范围内，Mori dream surface 的存在性依赖于可约纤维的奇偶性：各 $n_i$ 为偶数时给出充分必要条件，而存在 $n_i$ 为奇数时可由显式反例导致非 Mori dream surface。

Abstract: Let $π: X\to \mathbb P^1$ be a semistable Jacobian elliptic surface over $\mathbb C$, and set $χ=χ(\mathcal O_X)\ge3$, so that $κ(X)=1$. Assume that the Mordell-Weil group of $π$ is finite and that $π$ has at least one reducible fiber, the reducible fibers being of types $I_{n_1},\cdots, I_{n_s}$. Recently, Laface et al. proved that the zero section and the components of the reducible fibers generate $\overline{\mathrm NE}(X)$ if and only if $$δ(π):=\sum_{i=1}^s\frac{\lfloor n_i^2/4\rfloor}{n_i}\leχ.$$ In particular, the Mori cone is rational polyhedral in this range. They also proved that $N(π)=\sum_{i=1}^s n_i\le 2χ+3$ implies that $X$ is a Mori dream surface. In this paper, we study the existence problem of Mori dream surfaces provided that $N(π)\ge 2χ+4$. Suppose $δ(π)\le χ$. We first show that every nef isotropic divisor on $X$ is semiample, and whenever each $n_i$ is even. Furthermore, when every $n_i$ is even, we obtain criteria for $X$ to be a Mori dream surface: $X$ is a Mori dream surface provided that either (i) all $n_i=2$, or (ii) $N(π)\le 2χ+4$; if some $n_j=4$, then $X$ is a Mori dream surface if and only if $N(π)\le 2χ+4$. However, once some $n_i$ is odd, we construct a Jacobian elliptic surface $π: Y\to\mathbb P^1$ with $δ(π)=χ=3$, trivial Mordell-Weil group, and singular-fiber configuration $I_4+3I_3+23I_1$ for which none of the eight non-vertical isotropic extremal rays is semiample. In particular, $Y$ is not a Mori dream surface.

</details>


### [154] [Mordell--Weil groups of quasi-elliptic surfaces in characteristic 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23430)
*Toshiyuki Katsura*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在特征 $p=3$ 下，建立了 $\mathbf{P}^1$ 上拟椭圆曲面全局截面的初等阿贝尔群挠秩与某些超椭圆曲线 $p$-秩之间的具体关系，并确定了某类拟椭圆曲面 Mordell–Weil 群的挠秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I. Dolgachev 此前观察到拟椭圆曲面全局截面初等阿贝尔群的挠秩与超椭圆曲线 $p$-秩之间存在神秘联系，本文旨在解释这一联系。

Method: 在特征 $p=3$ 的设定下，通过比较 $\mathbf{P}^1$ 上拟椭圆曲面的全局截面所成初等阿贝尔群的挠秩与相关超椭圆曲线的 $p$-秩，建立二者之间的具体（显式）关系。

Result: 给出了挠秩与 $p$-秩之间的具体对应关系，从而解释了 Dolgachev 观察到的现象，并据此确定了一类拟椭圆曲面 Mordell–Weil 群的挠秩。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了两个看似无关不变量之间的内在联系，并为计算特定拟椭圆曲面的 Mordell–Weil 群挠秩提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In characteristic $p =3$, we establish a concrete relationship between the torsion rank of the elementary abelian group of global sections of quasi-elliptic surfaces over the projective line ${\mathbf P}^1$ and the $p$-rank of certain hyperelliptic curves, thereby explaining a mysterious relationship between these two invariants previously observed by I. Dolgachev. As an application, we determine the torsion ranks of the Mordell--Weil groups of a class of quasi-elliptic surfaces.

</details>


### [155] [Bimeromorphic invariance of $\partial\bar\partial$-property and blow-up formulae I: counterexamples and derived category approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23469)
*Wei Liu,Sheng Rao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 通过Honda的twistor空间构造、三维流形的∂∂̄-引理双有理不变性与嵌入消解，构造出满足∂∂̄-引理的三维紧复流形，其中含有一个使该引理失效的紧复曲面，否证了最小可能维数下的遗传性，并给出Alessandrini修正问题在四维及以上各维的反例；同时建立广义Bott–Chern复形的导出blow-up公式及Dolbeault blow-up公式，引入相对∂∂̄-性质并给出blow-up判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究∂∂̄-引理在子流形上的遗传性，以及Alessandrini修正问题；并希望在消解（blow-up）下推广Bott–Chern复形与Dolbeault上同调的相关公式。

Method: 利用N. Honda的twistor空间构造、三维流形∂∂̄-引理的双有理不变性与嵌入消解；建立带系数的广义Bott–Chern复形的导出blow-up公式；引入相对∂∂̄-性质并用Frölicher谱序列与Hodge滤链刻画。

Result: 构造出满足∂∂̄-引理的三维紧复流形，其中含有一个使其失效的紧复曲面，给出最小环境维数下的反例；在四维及以上各复维数给出Alessandrini修正问题的反例；得到与系数上同调比较映射相容的导出blow-up公式及有界导出系数的Dolbeault blow-up公式；给出blow-up判别准则；反例揭示经典公式在凝聚系数与奇异环境空间下的局限以及朴素Bott–Chern Künneth公式的失效。

Conclusion: ∂∂̄-引理在子流形上不具有遗传性，其反例可在最小可能维数（环境维数3、子流形维数2）实现，并推广为Alessandrini修正问题的反例；相对∂∂̄-性质与blow-up公式为相关上同调理论提供了新工具，同时表明经典公式在更一般情形下需修正。

Abstract: Using N. Honda's construction of twistor spaces, bimeromorphic invarince of $\partial\bar\partial$-lemma for threefolds and embedded resolution, we find a smooth compact complex threefold satisfying the $\partial\bar\partial$-lemma that contains a smooth compact surface for which the lemma fails. This disproves heredity in the smallest possible ambient dimension and yields counterexamples to L. Alessandrini's modification question in every complex dimension at least four. We establish a derived blow-up formula for generalized Bott--Chern complexes with coefficients, compatible with the comparison maps to coefficient cohomology. We also obtain a Dolbeault blow-up formula with bounded derived coefficients. For compact complex manifolds, we introduce a relative $\partial\bar\partial$-property, characterize it using the Frölicher spectral sequence and Hodge filtrations, and prove a blow-up criterion. Finally, counterexamples show the limitations of the classical formulae for coherent coefficients and singular ambient spaces, and the failure of the naive Bott--Chern Künneth formula.

</details>


### [156] [Nondeformability of Moishezon manifolds with nonnegative Kodaira dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23538)
*Mu-Lin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究具有连通纤维的全纯淹没，若非零纤维均同构于固定的光滑Moishezon流形S且κ(S)≥0，则中心纤维X_0亦同构于S。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨紧复流形族中纤维的形变刚性，特别是中心纤维在非零纤维全部同构时能否保持同构性质，将小平–Spencer形变理论中的刚性结果推广到Moishezon流形情形。

Method: 设π:X→D为具有连通纤维的真全纯淹没，且对每个t≠0，X_t双全纯于固定光滑Moishezon流形S，并假设S的Kodaira维数κ(S)≥0，通过形变理论与Moishezon流形上的奇点消解、极小模型等工具分析中心纤维X_0。

Result: 证明当κ(S)≥0时，中心纤维X_0与S双全纯等价，即X_0≃S。

Conclusion: 对Kodaira维数非负的光滑Moishezon流形，纤维全同构的形变族具有刚性：中心纤维不会发生退化，仍同构于一般纤维S。

Abstract: Let $π:\X\to\D$ be a proper holomorphic submersion with connected fibers, and suppose that $X_t$ is biholomorphic to a fixed smooth Moishezon manifold $S$ for every $t\ne0$. If $κ(S)\ge0$, we prove that $X_0\simeq S$.

</details>


### [157] [Freeness of Pluricanonical Linear System on Smooth Minimal n-folds of General Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23637)
*Hanye Gu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究一般型极小射影簇的多典范自由性问题，证明了 $\left|\frac{n^2+n+2}{2}K_X\right|$ 无基点，并给出 Fujita 自由猜想在 nef 且 big 除子情形下的上界；同时证明了增广基 locus 之外的基点和估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fujita 自由猜想关于多典范映射的基点自由性仅有猜测上界，而在一般型极小簇及 nef 且 big 除子的推广情形中，需要有效的界与结构刻画。本文旨在给出这类情形下具体可行的上界，并控制基点相对于增广基 locus 的位置。

Method: 对 $n$ 维复射影光滑极小一般型簇 $X$，结合一般型极小模型理论、nef 与 big 除子的性质以及增广基 locus 的分析，估计多典范系统的基点；通过构造与乘子理想/渐近不变量相关的论证得到无基点界与基点包含关系。

Result: 证明 $\left|\frac{n^2+n+2}{2}K_X\right|$ 无基点；对 $n$ 维光滑射影簇 $X$ 与 nef 且 big Cartier 除子 $B$，当整数 $m\ge m_n\sim n^2/\log n$ 时，$\operatorname{Bs}|K_X+mB|\subseteq \mathbf{B}_+(B)$，其中 $\mathbf{B}_+(B)$ 为增广基 locus。

Conclusion: 论文在一般型极小簇情形给出了多典范无基点的显式上界，并将结论推广到 nef 且 big 除子的情形，刻画了基点的位置，为 Fujita 自由猜想相关上界提供了新的结果。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth minimal projective variety of general type of dimension $n$ over $\mathbb C$. We prove that $|\frac{n^2+n+2}{2}K_X|$ is base point free. This gives a bound for the pluricanonical case of Fujita Freeness Conjecture with merely nef and big divisor. Let $X$ be a smooth projective variety of dimension $n$ and $B$ be a nef and big Cartier divisor. We also prove that $\operatorname{Bs}|K_X+mB|\subseteq \mathbf{B}_+(B)$ for every integer $m\geq m_n\sim n^2/\log n$, where $\mathbf{B}_+(B)$ is the augmented base locus.

</details>


### [158] [Frobenius splitting for Fano threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23639)
*Hiromu Tanaka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明特征p>5时光滑Fano三维簇均quasi-$\mathcal{F}$-分裂，且p>11时为$\mathcal{F}$-分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将正特征下Fano簇的$\mathcal{F}$-分裂性质研究从曲面推广到三维情形，拓展特征阈值结果。

Method: 利用正特征代数几何与$\mathcal{F}$-分裂理论，对光滑Fano三维簇分类并结合分裂判据进行论证。

Result: 每个特征p>5的光滑Fano三维簇是quasi-$\mathcal{F}$-分裂的；当p>11时是$\mathcal{F}$-分裂的。

Conclusion: 光滑Fano三维簇的$\mathcal{F}$-分裂性随特征增大成立，给出明确的特征阈值(p>5与p>11)。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth Fano threefold of characteristic p>5 is quasi-F-split. Moreover, if p>11, then it is F-split.

</details>


### [159] [Polynomial Volume Bounds and Effective Birationality for Log Calabi-Yau Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23763)
*Pinxian Bie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为带 ε-对数典范 log Calabi–Yau 边界的复射影曲面上的整系数 nef and big Weil 除子给出了统一的体积多项式下界：体积 ≥ 绝对正常数 × ε^9/(1+log(1/ε))，无需 bigness 假设，也不对 B 的系数作任何限制；同时导出阶为 ε^{-11/2}√(1+log(1/ε)) 的有效双有理性，适用于所有充分大的倍数，包括伴随系统与整系数伪有效加法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小模型纲领与有效性问题的框架下，曲面情形中带有 ε-对数典范 log Calabi–Yau 边界的体积下界与有效双有理性长期缺乏统一估计；已有结果往往需要额外假设（如 bigness 或系数限制）。本文旨在移除这些限制，建立仅依赖 ε 的统一多项式下界与有效双有理性阶。

Method: 研究对象为复射影曲面 (X, B)，其中 (X, B) 具有 ε-对数典范的 log Calabi–Yau 边界。作者对整系数 nef and big Weil 除子 D 的体积 vol(D) 进行下界估计，并进一步控制相应线性系统的有效双有理性（即充分大倍数映射的稳定性阶）。

Result: 体积下界为 vol(D) ≥ C·ε^9/(1+log(1/ε))（C 为绝对正常数）；有效双有理性的阶为 ε^{-11/2}√(1+log(1/ε))，且适用于所有充分大的倍数，涵盖伴随系统及整系数伪有效加法；结论不需要 bigness 假设，也不对边界系数施加限制。

Conclusion: 本文给出了曲面 ε-对数典范 log Calabi–Yau 情形下体积与有效双有理性的统一多项式估计，弱化了既有文献的假设条件，为相关有效性猜想在曲面情形提供了更强的显式结果。

Abstract: We provide a uniform polynomial lower bound for the volume of an integral nef and big Weil divisor on a complex projective surface admitting an $\varepsilon$-log canonical log Calabi--Yau boundary. More precisely, the volume is bounded below by an absolute positive constant times $\varepsilon^9/(1+\log(1/\varepsilon))$. No bigness or assumptions on the coefficients of $B$ are required. We also derive effective birationality of order $\varepsilon^{-11/2}\sqrt{1+\log(1/\varepsilon)}$ for all sufficiently large multiples, including adjoint systems and integral pseudo-effective additions.

</details>


### [160] [Fundamental Lemmas in Universal Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24047)
*Yi Hu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对通用爆破（universal blowups）中的若干基本引理进行了抽象化陈述与证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于通用爆破的讨论中，基础性引理缺乏统一的抽象表述与证明，本文旨在填补这一基础性空缺。

Method: 对通用爆破相关的基本引理进行抽象化（abstract）并给出证明。

Result: 建立并证明了通用爆破中的若干基本引理。

Conclusion: 这些基本引理为后续通用爆破的理论研究提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: We abstract and prove some fundamental lemmas in universal blowups

</details>


### [161] [A local cohomology obstruction to small Cohen--Macaulay modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24142)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个局部上同调障碍，用于判定曲面截面环的完备局部环上小Cohen–Macaulay模的不存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小Cohen–Macaulay模在截面环完备局部环上的存在性障碍，并给出可量化的判定方法。

Method: 结合自反扩张的初等变换、Harder–Narasimhan斜率、Bogomolov不等式以及双截面Koszul估计，并利用固定源核估计将上同调转移到去尖谱上。

Result: 当c(Y,H)>0时，任意非零有限自反模M满足dim_C H^2_m(M)≥c(Y,H)·rk M；在六线Hirzebruch–Kummer族的六次成员上，H^2=1080、χ(O_Y)=1926，得到界99·rk M。

Conclusion: 给出了显式的完全交模型，并论证该例中不存在非零有限极大Cohen–Macaulay模。

Abstract: We propose a local-cohomology obstruction to the existence of small Cohen--Macaulay modules over completed section rings of surfaces. Let $Y$ be a smooth connected complex projective surface, and let $H$ be an ample globally generated divisor with $K_Y\equiv4H$ numerically. Put $c(Y,H)=15H^2/8-χ(\OO_Y)$. The main claim is that, when $c(Y,H)>0$, every nonzero finite reflexive module $M$ over the completed vertex local ring of $\bigoplus_{n\geq0}H^0(Y,\OO_Y(nH))$ satisfies $\dim_\C H^2_\mm(M)\geq c(Y,H)\rk M$. The argument combines elementary transformations of reflexive extensions, Harder--Narasimhan slopes, Bogomolov's inequality, and a two-section Koszul estimate. A fixed-source kernel estimate transfers the resulting cohomology to the punctured spectrum without requiring a grading on $M$. For the degree-six member of the six-line Hirzebruch--Kummer family, $H^2=1080$ and $χ(\OO_Y)=1926$, so the bound is $99\rk M$. We give an explicit complete-intersection model for this application and spell out the argument leading to the claimed nonexistence of nonzero finite maximal Cohen--Macaulay modules.

</details>


### [162] [Trifocal Tensors in Three-View Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24314)
*Yiran Xu,Changqing Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立"共形张量代数"，用良定义的多线性算子替代零散矩阵运算，对三视图做对称协变下标处理，并给出三焦张量的精确秩刻画、外积表示、对极点提取及基于自动微分框架的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三焦张量虽是三视图几何的核心对象，但其3×3×3的三阶形式在标准文献中主要以矩阵方式零散操作，缺乏统一、坐标无关的代数框架，导致约束推导不统一且缺少结构性的退化判据。

Method: 以协变下标T_{ijk}对称处理三视图，用多线性算子构建共形张量代数；将点、线、混合对应约束统一重导为单一代数中的一次收缩；推导各mode-k展开T[k]（3×9）的秩性质、外积表示T=A^T×b_4 − B^T×_2 a_4及双收缩迹对极点提取；并通过PyTorch实现24参数双线性参数化进行自动微分精化实验。

Result: 非退化情形下所有mode-k展开T[k]∈R^{3×9}满足rank(T[k])=3，任一展开秩亏可判定相机配置退化，给出近零代价退化告警与估计张量验证准则；获得外积表示与对极点直接提取公式；PyTorch实验中24参数双线性参数化作为结构先验，消除了非结构化27元素自动微分精化的精度漂移。

Conclusion: 共形张量代数以坐标无关的多线性算子统一了三焦张量的约束推导与操作，可直接对接PyTorch/TensorFlow等自动微分框架，其秩刻画兼具退化检测与张量验证价值，结构先验参数化显著提升估计稳定性。

Abstract: Matrices and tensors are ubiquitous throughout computer vision. The trifocal tensor $\caT$ is a $3\times 3\times 3$ tensor that plays a vital role in three-view geometry. However, this tensor, though displayed in a third-order form, is manipulated and operated primarily in a matrix style in standard literature. This article establishes the conformal tensor algebra by replacing ad-hoc matrix collections with well-defined multilinear operators. We treat all three views symmetrically by covariant subscript indexing ($T_{ijk}$). To make precise what is new beyond notation: the classical point, line, and mixed correspondence constraints are here re-derived, but \emph{uniformly}, each as a single contraction in one shared algebra; the results that are genuinely new include the exact rank characterization of the trifocal tensor --- all mode-$k$ unfoldings $\caT[k]$ in $\RR^{3 \times 9}$ satisfy $\rank(\caT[k])=3$ for non-degenerate setups, and rank deficiency of any unfolding certifies degeneracy of the camera configuration, which yields a near-zero-cost degeneracy alarm and a validation criterion for estimated tensors; the outer-product representation $\caT = A^{\top}\times \bfb_{4} - B^{\top}\times_{2} \bfa_{4}$; and direct epipole extraction via double contractive traces. The coordinate-free multilinear operators seamlessly map to tensor auto-differentiation frameworks (e.g., PyTorch, TensorFlow); a concrete PyTorch experiment demonstrates that the induced $24$-parameter bilinear parametrization acts as a structural prior that eliminates the accuracy drift exhibited by an unstructured $27$-entry autodiff refinement.

</details>


### [163] [Finiteness theorems for pseudo-coherent complexes on algebraic stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24420)
*Jack Hall,Oliver Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对温和代数栈的proper态射，推出函子保持相对S的伪凝聚复形，解决了SGA6中Illusie的猜想，并给出了非温和情形的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的粘合与有限性定理（如Kiehl关于proper态射下伪凝聚性的结果）主要针对概形。对于代数栈，尤其是具有非有限稳定化子的非温和情形，相关理论尚不完善。Illusie在SGA6中提出的猜想正是希望将这一有限性结果推广到更一般的代数栈框架中。

Method: 作者利用导出代数几何（derived algebraic geometry）的工具，在导出设置下研究代数栈上的拟凝聚层，并在此基础上扩展了一些基础性结果。

Result: 证明了$R f_*$将相对$S$伪凝聚的复形映为相对$S$的伪凝聚复形；在概形情形下解决了Illusie的猜想；同时在非温和情形（如无限稳定化子）下也获得了相关的新结果；此外给出了Kiehl经典结论的新证明，并将拟凝聚层的一些基础结果推广至导出情形。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了proper态射下伪凝聚性的保持性，既解决了SGA6的猜想，又为非温和代数栈提供了新工具和结果，展示了导出代数几何在栈上同调有限性问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Let $f\colon X \to Y$ be a proper and tame morphism of algebraic stacks, where $X$ and $Y$ are locally of finite type over an algebraic stack $S$. We prove that $R f_*$ sends complexes that are pseudo-coherent relative to $S$ to pseudo-coherent complexes relative to $S$. In the scheme case, this resolves a conjecture of Illusie from SGA6. We also prove related and new results in the non-tame setting (e.g., infinite stabilizers). Our methods use derived algebraic geometry and also give new proofs of classical statements for schemes due to Kiehl. Along the way, we extend some foundational results for quasi-coherent sheaves on algebraic stacks to the derived setting.

</details>


### [164] [2-step ideals and commuting matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24491)
*Klemen Šivic*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过Hilbert概型与交换矩阵簇的对应关系，定义了对应2-step理想的交换矩阵簇，并估计其维数，从而给出n=3情形下2-step理想维数估计的新证明，同时弱化了原论文中的部分假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-step理想是近期为构造Hilb^d(A^n)的新不可约分支、特别是Hilb^d(A^3)中大维数轨迹而引入的。原论文的维数估计依赖若干假设，作者希望借助交换矩阵的语言重新处理，以简化或放宽这些条件。

Method: 利用Hilbert概型与交换矩阵簇（varieties of commuting matrices）之间的对应关系，定义出与2-step理想相对应的交换矩阵簇轨迹，并通过估计这些轨迹的维数来推导2-step理想轨迹的维数。

Result: 给出了n=3时2-step理想轨迹维数估计的新证明，并且在该情形下能够去掉原论文维数估计中的一些假设。

Conclusion: 交换矩阵簇的视角为研究2-step理想及其Hilbert概型分支提供了有效工具，在n=3时得到更一般（假设更少）的维数估计，也说明该方法有潜力推广至更高维或其他类似理想类。

Abstract: 2-step ideals are ideals $I$ of the polynomial ring that satisfy $\mathfrak{m}^{k+2}\subsetneq I\subsetneq \mathfrak{m}^k$ where $\mathfrak{m}$ is the maximal ideal generated by variables. This class of ideals was recently introduced in [F. Giovenzana, L. Giovenzana, M. Graffeo, P. Lella: New components of Hilbert schemes of points and 2-step ideals, 2025, arxiv: 2507.02789] with the aim to obtain new irreducible components of $\mathrm{Hilb}^d(\mathbb{A}^n)$ and in particular to obtain new loci in $\mathrm{Hilb}^d(\mathbb{A}^3)$ of large dimension. In this paper we use the correspondence between Hilbert schemes and varieties of commuting matrices to define loci of commuting matrices that correspond to 2-step ideals. Then we estimate the dimensions of the obtained loci to get new proofs for the estimates of dimensions of loci of 2-step ideals in the case $n=3$. In this case we are also able to omit some assumptions in the dimension estimates in the above mentioned paper.

</details>


### [165] [The Generalized Lax Conjecture for Strictly Hyperbolic Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24542)
*Mario Kummer,Tim Netzer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 人工智能系统为"每个严格双曲多项式的双曲锥都是谱面体锥"这一命题找到了证明，从而在一般情形下确立了广义Lax猜想的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Lax猜想是凸代数几何与优化理论中的核心问题，长期未能解决。此前只在低维或特殊情形有结果，一般情形的严格双曲多项式所对应的双曲锥是否具有谱面体实现（即表示为线性矩阵不等式的可行集）始终悬而未决。

Method: 采用人工智能系统自动搜索并构造证明，输出的是一个显式证明而非数值验证或部分结果，针对对象为严格双曲多项式及其双曲锥的谱面体实现问题。

Result: 找到了该命题的证明：每个严格双曲多项式的双曲锥均为谱面体锥。由此在generic（一般/生成性）情形下建立了广义Lax猜想。完整猜想仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该结果部分推进了广义Lax猜想，确认了generic情形下的正确性；完全的一般性证明仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We report a proof found by an artificial intelligence system for the following statement: The hyperbolicity cone of every strictly hyperbolic polynomial is spectrahedral. This establishes the generalized Lax conjecture for the generic case, while the full conjecture remains open.

</details>


### [166] [Untwisting the twisted character variety via a stacky curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24584)
*Anna Borri*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明光滑射影曲线 X 的 twisted character variety 等价于通过添加 orbifold 点得到的 stacky 曲线 X 的 character variety 的一个连通分支，从而将非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应解释为 X 上 λ-联络的 Hodge 模空间几何，并用 Shende 的单纯方案方法计算 tautological 类的权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: twisted character variety 与非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应是高维代数几何与几何表示论的核心对象，但其几何结构与上同调性质计算困难。作者希望借助 stacky 曲线与 orbifold 点的观点，将 twisted 情形转化为已理解的 Hodge 模空间几何，从而获得可计算的上同调权重信息。

Method: 在光滑射影曲线 X 上添加一个 orbifold 点构造 stacky 曲线 X；证明 X 的 twisted character variety 与 X 的 character variety 的一个连通分支等价；由此将次数 d 的非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应表述为 X 上 λ-联络的 Hodge 模空间的几何；沿用 Shende 的方法，用三角剖分得到的单纯方案 Δ_X 替换 X 以进行上同调计算。

Result: 建立了 twisted character variety 与 stacky 曲线 character variety 分支之间的等价；给出了非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应在 λ-联络 Hodge 模空间层面的几何解释；利用单纯方案替换计算出了 twisted character variety 上同调中 tautological 类的权。

Conclusion: 通过 orbifold 点与 stacky 曲线的等价视角，可以在 Hodge 模空间框架下统一理解 twisted 非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应，并借助 Shende 的三角剖分方法有效计算 tautological 类的上同调权重。

Abstract: Given a smooth projective curve $X$ over $\mathbb{C}$, we show that the twisted character variety of $X$ is equivalent to a component of the character variety of a stacky curve $\mathcal{X}$, constructed by adding an orbifold point to $X$. This allows to interpret the non abelian Hodge correspondence in degree $d$ over $X$ as a statement about the geometry of the Hodge moduli space of $λ$-connections on $\mathcal{X}$. Following Shende's approach (see arXiv:1411.4975), we exploit this interpretation to compute the weights of the tautological classes on the cohomology of the twisted character variety, by replacing $\mathcal{X}$ with a simplicial scheme $Δ_{\mathcal{X}}$ obtained from a triangulation.

</details>


### [167] [Conormal Rank of Subvarieties of Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24694)
*Elizabeth Pratt*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入Grassmannian子簇的"余法秩"新统计量，并证明Chow–Lam形式及其推广具有低余法秩，且低余法秩超曲面均为此类形式，推广了Gelfand–Kapranov–Zelevinsky的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grassmannian子簇的余切空间可自然等同于线性同态空间，作者希望利用这一结构刻画子簇的性质，并推广Gelfand–Kapranov–Zelevinsky关于超曲面的经典结果。

Method: 将Grassmannian子簇的余切空间等同于线性同态空间，据此定义子簇的余法秩统计量；通过该统计量分析Chow–Lam形式及其自然推广，并计算Schubert簇、环面轨道闭包、positroid簇等具体簇族的余法秩。

Result: Chow–Lam形式及其自然推广具有低余法秩；余法秩充分低的超曲面均为此类形式；计算了Schubert簇、环面轨道闭包和positroid簇等簇族的余法秩。

Conclusion: 余法秩为研究Grassmannian子簇提供了新的有效工具，统一并推广了Chow–Lam形式及Gelfand–Kapranov–Zelevinsky的相关结果。

Abstract: The cotangent space to any subvariety of a Grassmannian is naturally identified with a space of linear homomorphisms. We use this to define a new statistic on a subvariety of a Grassmannian, called \emph{conormal rank}. We show that Chow--Lam forms and their natural generalizations have low conormal rank, and moreover that hypersurfaces of sufficiently low conormal rank are all of this form, generalizing a result of Gelfand--Kapranov--Zelevinsky. We also compute the conormal rank of many families of varieties, including Schubert varieties, torus orbit closures, and positroid varieties.

</details>


### [168] [On Free and Nearly Free Conjecture of Rational Unibranched Projective Plane Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24810)
*Xiping Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了约化不可约复平面曲线补的欧拉示性数不小于其自由性亏格，并据此验证了Dimca-Sticlaru猜想：单分支有理平面曲线必为自由或近自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平面曲线奇点与其自由性（freeness）之间的关系，试图建立拓扑不变量（补集欧拉示性数）与代数不变量（自由性亏格）之间的不等式，从而刻画自由与近自由曲线的判定条件，并解决Dimca-Sticlaru关于单分支有理曲线分类的猜想。

Method: 设 C⊂P^2 为约化不可约复平面曲线，U为其补集。引入自由性亏格 ν(C)（来自 Dim24 的定义），通过分析奇点及单分支性条件，证明不等式 χ(U)≥ν(C)，并在此框架下推导有理单分支情形的自由性结论。

Result: 对任意约化不可约复平面曲线 C，其补集满足 χ(U)≥ν(C)。当 C 为单分支奇点的有理曲线时，由该不等式可推出其必为自由曲线或近自由曲线。

Conclusion: 该不等式将补集欧拉示性数与自由性亏格相联系，为曲线自由性提供了拓扑判据，并证实了 Dimca-Sticlaru 关于单分支有理平面曲线要么自由要么近自由的猜想。

Abstract: Let $C\subset \PP^2$ be a reduced irreducible complex plane curve with complement $U$. In this paper we prove that $χ(U)\geq ν(C)$, where $ν(C)$ is the freeness defect of $C$ defined in \cite{Dim24}.
  When $C$ is a rational curve with unibranched singularities, this inequality proves the conjecture of Dimca and Sticlaru, that a rational unibranched plane curve is either free or nearly free.

</details>


### [169] [Magic positivity of Snapper polynomials for matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24917)
*Shiyue Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究线丛张量幂的Snapper多项式的正性，通过magic正性与实根性视角，对拟阵的toric簇上引入zonotopal类并证明其Snapper多项式magic正；同时在 \overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n} 上构造类似线丛，证明相关h*-多项式实根，且Snapper多项式magic正当且仅当n≤7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Snapper多项式系数的正性蕴含线丛的多种正性概念，因此刻画其正性具有重要几何与代数意义；作者希望借助magic正性和实根性这一组合框架，系统研究Snapper多项式的正性。

Method: 引入无环拟阵toric簇向量丛Grothendieck K-环中的zonotopal类；将Snapper多项式实现为拟阵沿某些线的Dilworth截断的加权独立多项式；在 \overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n} 上引入类似线丛；提出拟阵的饱和与弱饱和K-类，用其dragon Hall–Rado多拟阵给出magic正性的充分和必要条件。

Result: zonotopal类的Snapper多项式magic正，系数为正，h*-多项式实根；可实化情形下该多项式是嵌入射影线乘积中wonderful簇的多重分次Hilbert多项式； \overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n} 上类似线丛的Snapper多项式magic正；第一Chern类互异的ψ-类余切丛线丛虽非zonotopal，其h*-多项式实根，且Snapper多项式magic正当且仅当n≤7。

Conclusion: 通过zonotopal类、饱和/弱饱和K-类及dragon Hall–Rado多拟阵，建立了Snapper多项式magic正性的组合判据，揭示了拟阵与 \overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n} 上线丛正性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In 1959, Snapper showed that the Euler characteristic of the tensor powers of a line bundle on a normal projective scheme is a polynomial, later named the \emph{Snapper polynomial}. Positivity of coefficients of Snapper polynomials implies various notions of positivity of line bundles, which we study through the lens of magic positivity and real-rootedness. We introduce zonotopal classes in the Grothendieck $K$-ring of vector bundles of the toric variety for any loopless matroid, and prove that their Snapper polynomials are magic positive. Our proof realizes such a Snapper polynomial as a weighted independence polynomial of the Dilworth truncation along certain lines of the matroid. As a consequence, their coefficients are positive, and their $h^{\ast}$-polynomials are real-rooted. In the realizable case, this polynomial is the multigraded Hilbert polynomial of the wonderful variety embedded in a product of projective lines.
  We introduce analogous line bundles on the Deligne--Mumford--Knudsen moduli space $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ and prove that their Snapper polynomials are magic positive. For cotangent line bundles whose first Chern classes are distinct $ψ$-classes, which are not zonotopal, we nonetheless prove that their $h^{\ast}$-polynomials are real-rooted, whereas their Snapper polynomials are magic positive if and only if $n\leqslant7$. More generally, we introduce saturated and weakly saturated $K$-classes of matroids, which furnish a sufficient and a necessary condition for magic positivity of Snapper polynomials in terms of their dragon Hall--Rado polymatroids.

</details>


### [170] [Boundedness of geometrically integral $ε$-klt del Pezzo surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24968)
*Shikha Bhutani,Sudipta Das*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Borisov-Alexeev-Borisov猜想在任意域上几何整ε-klt del Pezzo曲面剩余正特征情形的有界性，通过给出不规则性的统一上界，补全了所有正特征下的有界性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BAB猜想的有界性问题在正特征情形仍有缺口。Bernasconi与Martin已建立特征2、3、5之外的有界性，并将其余情形归结为不规则性（irregularity）的统一上界问题，该上界尚未被证明，构成本文要填补的关键空白。

Method: 针对任意域上几何整的ε-klt del Pezzo曲面，证明其不规则性满足统一上界，从而处理特征2、3、5的剩余情形，并将其与Bernasconi-Martin的结果拼接完成整体论证。

Result: 证明了不规则性的统一上界，解决了BAB猜想在几何整ε-klt del Pezzo曲面情形下剩余的正特征情形。

Conclusion: 在所有正特征下，几何整ε-klt del Pezzo曲面的有界性定理被完全建立，BAB猜想的相应情形得以完成。

Abstract: We prove the remaining positive characteristic cases of the Borisov-Alexeev-Borisov conjecture for geometrically integral $\varepsilon$-klt del Pezzo surfaces over arbitrary fields. Bernasconi and Martin established boundedness outside characteristics $2$, $3$, and $5$, and reduced the remaining cases to a uniform bound for the irregularity. We prove this bound, thereby completing the boundedness theorem in all positive characteristics.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Theory of Gravity as Theory of Local Inertial Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22354)
*Bartłomiej Bąk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究基于时空对称联络的仿射引力理论（以完整黎曼张量为基本对象），建立变分表述，并首次在一般情形下证明从仿射图景到度规图景的转换，将非度规性诠释为度规图景中的物质场（呼应外尔对电磁学的诠释）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力理论以度规为核心，但作者认为更基本的对象应为仿射联络，从而将引力自然地理解为牛顿第一定律的局部版本；同时已有的"非度规性即物质场"结论仅对特定拉格朗日量类别成立，需推广至一般情形。

Method: 以时空对称联络（局部惯性系场）作为基本变量，构建完整黎曼张量仿射理论的变分表述（基于作者与导师合作文章的结果），并以若干示例支撑框架，进而实施仿射图景到度规图景的转换程序。

Result: 证明仿射图景中的非度规性可解释为度规图景中的物质场，且该结论此前仅在特定拉格朗日量类别下成立；本文首次在完全一般的情形下给出从完整黎曼张量理论到常规度规理论的转换，并用先前示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 完整黎曼张量的仿射理论构成引力的扩展理论，引力可作为牛顿第一定律的局部版本自然涌现；非度规性对应物质场的诠释与外尔对电磁学的诠释一致，仿射—度规转换的一般性结果为该框架提供了核心支撑。

Abstract: It is proved that the affine theory of the full Riemann tensor constitutes the extended theory of gravity. The fundamental object here is a spacetime symmetric connection. Its physical interpretation is that of a field of local inertial frames. In this approach, gravity arises, in a natural way, as a local version of Newton's First Law. A variational formulation of the theory is presented, based on the results introduced in the article co-authored by the present author and one of the supervisors. The general framework is supported by several examples. A central result of the dissertation is the transition from the affine picture to the metric picture. A simplified version of this procedure -- valid for a specific class of Lagrangians. It was proved there that the non-metricity in the affine picture can be interpreted as a matter field in the metric picture. This is precisely Hermann Weyl's interpretation of electromagnetism. Here, the transformation from the full Riemann tensor theory to the conventional metric theory is examined in full generality for the first time and illustrated using the previously introduced examples.

</details>


### [172] [Sourced Gravitational Radiation in Parity-Violating Tetrad Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22396)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究四参数挠率引力理论中线性引力模、远场源响应及二阶能量守恒，给出8个线性偏振的来源-振幅关系与模分辨能流，并指出矢量模哈密顿量非正定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索包含宇称偶挠率不变量与宇称奇耦合的四参数挠率引力理论的线性动力学与能量性质。

Method: 在闵可夫斯基背景线性化并做标量-矢量-张量分解；计算动能本征值、传播速度与通量归一化；推导紧凑正则源响应及源到振幅关系；证明二阶诺特流等于二阶Møller流并分解模能量动量流。

Result: 一般非退化宇称破坏区有2个张量、2个标量和4个矢量自由度；张量与标量模以光速传播，两支矢量分支速度依赖耦合；迹恒等式表明一般分支矢量哈密顿量非正定，即使动能本征值为正、速度为实数。

Conclusion: 矢量贡献因无界哈密顿量仅为形式上有符号的响应；静止极限复现Shirafuji-Nashed 1997结果，参数扫描显示矢量模劈裂与动能简并。

Abstract: We study linear gravitational modes, sourced far-zone fields, and quadratic energy balance in a four-parameter tetrad theory of gravity. The theory contains three parity-even torsion invariants and a parity-odd coupling $v^μa_μ$, where $v_μ$ and $a_μ$ are the vector and axial torsion components. Linearizing around Minkowski spacetime and applying a scalar--vector--tensor decomposition, we find two tensor, two scalar, and four vector degrees of freedom in the generic nondegenerate parity-violating region. The tensor and scalar modes propagate at the speed of light, while the two vector branches have coupling-dependent propagation speeds. We derive their kinetic eigenvalues, characteristic speeds, and flux normalizations. An exact trace identity shows that the vector Hamiltonian is not positive definite in the generic branch, even when the kinetic eigenvalues are positive and the propagation speeds are real. We also derive the response to a general compact canonical source and obtain explicit source-to-amplitude relations for all eight linear polarizations. The field $V_μ=\partial^νB_{νμ}$ is used to illustrate intrinsic-spin multipoles. {We further prove that the quadratic Noether current equals the second-order Møller current for the chosen first-derivative tetrad Lagrangian and derive the corresponding mode-resolved energy and momentum fluxes.} The vector contribution remains a formal signed response because of the unbounded Hamiltonian. The stationary limit reproduces the results of Ref.~\cite{ShirafujiNashed1997}, and a representative parameter scan illustrates vector-mode splitting and kinetic degeneracy.

</details>


### [173] [Charged particles and fields in bumblebee gravity: Periodic orbits and superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22482)
*Marco A. A. de Paula,Renan B. Magalhães,V. B. Bezerra,A. A. Araújo Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究bumblebee引力中带电黑洞背景下带电粒子与标量场的动力学，重点分析Lorentz破缺参数l对轨道、吸收截面和超辐射的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Lorentz破缺参数l在带电bumblebee黑洞时空中所起的作用，并检验其相对Reissner-Nordström情形的偏离。

Method: 回顾带电bumblebee黑洞解及其几何性质；求解带电粒子的圆轨道与束缚轨道；采用数值方法计算任意频率下的总吸收截面，并考虑非渐近平坦时空修正的渐近动量和通量；给出低频与高频解析近似。

Result: 最内稳定圆轨道随l增大而增大；低频截面出现区分两支渐近分支的过渡速度并推广RN结果；数值与解析近似在相应极限一致；参数空间分为无界吸收、有界吸收和有界超辐射三区，超辐射发生于ω<ω_c；超辐射放大随l增大而减弱，l<0时超辐射散射强于RN。

Conclusion: 增大l虽降低势垒，但因截面公式中的几何因子1/(1+l)而使总吸收截面减小；Lorentz破缺对黑洞吸收与超辐射性质具有显著影响。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles and fields in the background of a charged black hole in bumblebee gravity, aiming to better understand the role played by the Lorentz-violating parameter $l$. We first review the derivation of the charged bumblebee black hole and investigate its main physical and geometrical properties. We then obtain the circular and bound trajectories of charged particles and find that the innermost stable circular orbit increases with $l$. By using a numerical approach, we compute the total absorption cross section for arbitrary values of the scalar wave frequency $ω$. Since the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we construct the total absorption cross section by taking into account the modified asymptotic momentum and flux. Analytical approximations are derived in the low- and high-frequency regimes. The low-frequency expression displays a transition velocity that separates two distinct asymptotic branches and generalizes the corresponding Reissner-Nordström result. Our numerical results for the cross section agree with the analytical approximations in their corresponding limits, and the absorption parameter space can be divided into three regions: Unbounded absorption, bounded absorption, and bounded superradiance. In particular, superradiance occurs whenever $ω<ω_{c}$, where $ω_{c}$ is the threshold for superradiance. Moreover, the superradiance amplification decreases as we increase $l$, and charged bumblebee black holes with $l < 0$ lead to greater superradiant scattering than in the Reissner-Nordström case. We also note that although increasing $l$ lowers the potential barrier, it reduces the total absorption cross section because of the geometrical factor $1/(1+l)$ present in the cross section formula.

</details>


### [174] [Flexible numerical characteristic gluing to extremal Reissner-Nordström black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22518)
*Junwoo Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文用数值方法研究带电标量场引力坍缩形成极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞的特征胶合构造:改进无质量标量场的黑洞尺寸下界,并给出有质量标量场质量电荷比上界(为1)为锐界的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作利用特征胶合方法构造了违反黑洞力学第三定律的时空。本文旨在进一步定量刻画该胶合构造,特别是考察带电标量场坍缩形成极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞时所允许的参数范围。

Method: 采用数值方法研究特征胶合构造:(1)对无质量标量场,改进胶合可行所对应的最终黑洞尺寸下界;(2)对有质量标量场,在质量电荷比接近但低于1的情况下构造胶合解。

Result: 对无质量标量场,显著提高了胶合可行的最终黑洞尺寸下界;对有质量标量场,成功构造出质量电荷比略低于1的胶合解,给出了原上界为锐界的强有力证据。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞形成中质量电荷比上界(等于1)是锐的,并推进了对违反第三定律时空胶合构造的定量理解。

Abstract: Recent work has used the method of characteristic gluing to construct spacetimes violating the third law of black hole mechanics. This paper uses numerical methods to investigate two aspects of the gluing construction of solutions describing the gravitational collapse of a charged scalar field to form an extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. First, for a massless scalar field, we improve significantly the lower bounds on the size of the final black hole for which gluing is possible. Second, for a massive scalar field, it is known that the formation of an extremal Reissner--Nordström black hole is impossible if the mass-to-charge ratio of the scalar field exceeds unity. We present strong evidence that this bound is sharp by constructing gluing solutions with a scalar field mass-to-charge ratio just below unity.

</details>


### [175] [Black Holes as Frequency-Dependent Filters of Stochastic Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22644)
*Sefi Katznelson,Aidan Minger,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文澄清黑洞对稳态引力波背景的响应是实频散射问题而非额外准简正模源，并以频率-角度-偏振相关的"滤波器"框架量化其效应。结果显示对真实黑洞群体（含全部暗物质的星子质量原初黑洞）光深可忽略，可观测性更依赖近邻、对齐、快旋或瞬变照明系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清一个常见概念混淆：受扰黑洞会产生ringdown（复频准简正模），而对稳态引力波背景的响应本质是实频散射，并不会产生额外观测谱线。作者希望将这一"标准区分"定量化，并评估黑洞群体对稳态背景的实际谱畸变是否可观测。

Method: 将黑洞建模为依赖频率、角度与偏振的滤波器（transfer function）。对Schwarzschild黑洞计算弹性散射与视界吸收的贡献；对各项同性稳态背景证明弹性散射无净单极信号，唯有视界吸收产生群体层面的谱畸变。随后把单黑洞结果提升为宇宙学群体的角度-偏振输运核（transport kernel），得到光学深度。并将分析推广到Kerr黑洞，考察超辐射导致的共转模式放大。

Result: 对Schwarzschild黑洞：弹性散射不产生净单极信号，视界吸收是唯一群体层面谱畸变，给出其吸收与相位延迟特征，并将稳态响应与有限波包激发的因果ringdown联系起来。群体的角度-偏振输运核给出的光深对现实黑洞群体可忽略，包括占全部暗物质的星子质量原初黑洞。对Kerr黑洞：超辐射可在特定共转模式产生真实放大，但各向同性入射与随机自旋取向强烈稀释漫射信号。

Conclusion: 稳态引力波背景经黑洞传播的累积宇宙学效应在可观测层面可忽略，因此探测黑洞对背景的印记更可能来自罕见、近邻、自旋与视线对齐、快速旋转或瞬变照明的系统，而非宇宙学尺度上的累积传播。

Abstract: Black holes ring when perturbed, whereas their response to a stationary gravitational-wave background is a real-frequency scattering problem, not a source of additional quasi-normal-mode lines. We make this standard distinction quantitative by treating a black hole as a frequency-, angle-, and polarization-dependent filter. For an isotropic stationary background around Schwarzschild holes, elastic scattering produces no net monopole signal, so horizon absorption is the only population-level spectral distortion. We calculate this transfer function for Schwarzschild holes in detail, identify its absorptive and phase-delay signatures, and connect the stationary response to the causal ringdown excited by a finite wave packet. We then promote the single-hole result to an angular and polarization transport kernel for a cosmological population. The resulting optical depth is negligible for realistic black-hole populations, including asteroid-mass primordial black holes comprising all dark matter. We then extend the analysis to Kerr holes, for which superradiance allows genuine amplification in selected co-rotating modes, but isotropic incidence and random spin orientations strongly dilute the diffuse signal. Observable effects are therefore more likely in rare, nearby, aligned, rapidly spinning, or transiently illuminated systems than through cumulative cosmological propagation.

</details>


### [176] [An exact Kerr-like rotating Morris--Thorne wormhole: throat, ergoregion and equatorial shadow slice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22823)
*Mark Sukaiti,Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 构建了零红移Morris-Thorne虫洞的Kerr类旋转推广，证明喉部为类时曲面而非视界，并确定其存在条件与能层起始位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将静态零红移Morris-Thorne虫洞推广至旋转（站轴、稳态）情形，探索Kerr类旋转对虫洞几何、喉部结构、因果性及能层的影响。

Method: 采用Teo型稳态轴对称ansatz；对随零角动量观测者共动的各向异性源求解Einstein方程，得K(r)=r^2+a^2并将参考系拖曳化为径向积分；对b(r)=r_0^2/r用不完全椭圆积分计算并用ADM角动量归一化；用准局部方法定义喉部，构造带符号喉部适配坐标，并推导光学赤道捕获区间。

Result: 时空渐近平坦但ADM质量为零，故a=J_ADM/M_ADM不适用，a与J_ADM独立，得到(r_0,a,J_ADM)族；喉半径r_th=√(r_0^2−a^2)，实性界|a|≤r_0，正规范围0≤|a|<r_0；喉部为族S_ℓ中唯一零平均曲率且面积二阶变分正的闭合面；带符号坐标显示两个渐近平坦端、排除闭合类时曲线、喉部为类时；确定能层起始位置，并给出光学赤道捕获区间。

Conclusion: 该旋转虫洞喉部为类时而非视界，因果性良好（无闭合类时曲线），且有两个渐近平坦端；这些结论不依赖于J_ADM=ar_0的选取。所支持的应力张量为有效唯象源，不主张微观物质拉氏量或真实状态方程；因Hamilton-Jacobi方程不可分离，仅给出光学赤道捕获结果。

Abstract: We construct an exact Kerr-like rotating extension of the zero-redshift Morris--Thorne wormhole in a Teo-type stationary, axisymmetric ansatz. The Einstein equations for an anisotropic source comoving with zero angular momentum observers fix $\mathcal{K}(r)=r^2+a^2$ and reduce frame dragging to a radial quadrature. For $b(r)=r_0^2/r$, this is evaluated using incomplete elliptic integrals and normalised by the ADM angular momentum. The spacetime is asymptotically flat with zero ADM mass, so $a=J_{\rm ADM}/M_{\rm ADM}$ does not apply. The oblateness length $a$ and angular momentum $J_{\rm ADM}$ remain independent, yielding a family $(r_0,a,J_{\rm ADM})$. The choice $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$ selects only a one-parameter slice for numerical ergoregion and equatorial-capture illustrations; it is not a field-equation constraint. We find $r_{\rm th}=\sqrt{r_0^2-a^2}$, with reality bound $|a|\leq r_0$ and regular range $0\leq |a|<r_0$. In the canonical stationary foliation, the throat is defined quasi-locally as the unique closed surface $S_0$ in the family $S_\ell$ with zero mean curvature and positive area second variation. Moreover, $r=r_{\rm th}$ and $\ell=0$ are coordinate representations. A signed throat-adapted coordinate reveals two asymptotically flat ends, excludes closed timelike curves, and establishes that the throat is timelike, not a horizon. These conclusions hold independently of $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$. The supporting stress tensor, reconstructed from the Einstein tensor, is an effective phenomenological source. We do not claim any microscopic matter Lagrangian or realistic equation of state. We also determine the ergoregion onset. Since the full Hamilton--Jacobi equation is nonseparable, we limit ourselves to derive the optical result for a one-dimensional equatorial capture interval.

</details>


### [177] [Covariant kinetic theory of photons coupled to massive particles in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22837)
*Long Cui,Yi-fan Cai,Xin Hao,Bin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了广义弯曲时空中光子与有质量粒子耦合演化的协变玻尔兹曼方程，构造了发射-吸收、康普顿散射及有质量粒子散射的碰撞积分，通过H定理导出吸收与发射速率关系及平衡态细致平衡条件，得到光子气体的协变热力学关系，并以Kerr时空中吸积盘热辐射为例进行了说明，进一步推广至包含零质量与有质量粒子的多组分过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力场中的非平衡辐射过程需要从第一性原理出发的理论描述，而现有框架对弯曲时空中光子与有质量粒子耦合的非平衡演化处理不足，缺乏协变的碰撞积分与热力学关系。

Method: 发展协变玻尔兹曼方程以描述弯曲时空中光子与有质量粒子的耦合演化；构造光子发射-吸收、康普顿散射及有质量粒子间散射的碰撞积分并验证守恒律；利用H定理确定吸收与发射跃迁速率的关系；在平衡态下由熵产为零导出细致平衡条件，进而得到光子气体的协变热力学关系；并将碰撞积分推广至任意多组分（零质量与有质量粒子）过程。

Result: 证明了碰撞积分满足守恒律；H定理要求吸收与发射跃迁速率满足特定关系，在非相对论极限下退化为爱因斯坦关系；平衡态熵产为零给出细致平衡条件，并由此获得光子气体的协变热力学关系；以Kerr时空吸积盘热辐射为示例，分别讨论了全局平衡与局部热力学平衡下的开普勒盘情形。

Conclusion: 该协变玻尔兹曼框架从第一性原理出发统一处理强引力场中的非平衡辐射过程，并可推广至任意多组分零质量与有质量粒子过程。

Abstract: This paper develops the covariant Boltzmann equation governing the coupled evolution of photons and massive particles in general curved spacetimes. We construct the collision integrals for photon emission-absorption, Compton scattering, and scattering between massive particles, and show the conservation laws. Requiring the H-theorem to hold determines the relation between the absorption and emission transition rates, which reduces to the Einstein relation in the non-relativistic limit. At equilibrium the entropy production vanishes, yielding the detailed balance condition, from which we obtain the covariant thermodynamic relations of the photon gas. We illustrate these results with the thermal radiation from an accretion disk in Kerr spacetime, first in global equilibrium and then in a Keplerian disk in local thermodynamic equilibrium. We further extend the collision integrals to arbitrary multi-species processes involving both null and massive particles. The formulation treats non-equilibrium radiative processes in strong gravitational fields from first principles.

</details>


### [178] [Charged Klein-Gordon modes on an Ellis wormhole: magnetic confinement and conditional Heun solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22860)
*Abdullah Guvendi,Omar Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究(2+1)维Ellis可穿越虫洞在外加磁场中的带电Klein-Gordon模式，该磁场通过辅助Euclidean嵌入空间构造。分离变量后径向方程等价于一维Schrödinger算符，可化为合流Heun类方程，并分析了磁约束谱与多项式解的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弯曲时空（虫洞）背景与外部规范场耦合下带电标量场的谱结构，特别是磁场对径向模式的约束效应及其可解性。

Method: 采用Euclidean嵌入空间定义均匀外磁场，通过拉回环境势得到正则方位角规范场；分离Klein-Gordon方程，将径向问题转化为等价的一维Schrödinger算符；利用合流Heun方程及多项式截断条件进行分析。

Result: 非零磁耦合下二次渐近项在完整虫洞上产生径向约束；径向方程属于合流Heun类；多项式截断仅在由同时终止条件决定的受限参数子流形上存在，故不构成一般约束谱；给出了相应的相对论能量并分析了最低多项式扇区，同时区分了形式连续ℓ可解性与角周期条件要求的整数方位分离常数。

Conclusion: 磁场诱导的径向约束使虫洞上的Klein-Gordon模式呈现离散化趋势，但精确多项式解仅出现于特殊参数子流形，一般谱需用合流Heun函数描述；形式可解性与物理角周期性需加以区分。

Abstract: We study charged Klein-Gordon modes on a $(2+1)$-dimensional Ellis traversable wormhole in an external magnetic field defined as uniform in an auxiliary Euclidean embedding space. The spatial geometry is intrinsically catenoidal, while the embedding is used only to construct the external gauge connection. Pullback of the ambient potential yields a regular azimuthal gauge field whose associated intrinsic magnetic scalar reverses sign across the throat. Separation of the Klein-Gordon equation leads to a radial problem that is unitarily equivalent to a one-dimensional Schrödinger operator with a regular effective potential. For nonzero magnetic coupling, the quadratic asymptotic term produces radial confinement on the complete wormhole. The radial equation can be reduced to the confluent-Heun class. Polynomial truncation is possible only on constrained parameter submanifolds determined by simultaneous termination conditions and therefore does not represent the generic confined spectrum. We derive the corresponding relativistic energies and analyze the lowest polynomial sector, while distinguishing the formal continuous-$\ell$ solvability correlations from the integer values of the azimuthal separation constant required by the standard angular periodicity condition.

</details>


### [179] [Probing black holes surrounded by Dust field in Quantum Fluctuation Modified Gravity with twin-peak QPOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22874)
*Kamola Shermatova,Qodir Badalov,Abubakir Shermatov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Bekzod Rahmatov,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Dilafruz Xujanova,Farhod Nuraliev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在量子涨落修正引力下研究含尘埃场静态球对称黑洞，用双峰QPO数据经MCMC约束参数：K∈(0,0.5)、α∈(0.3,0.6)（2σ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子涨落修正引力中引入尘埃场后的黑洞时空性质，并借助准周期振荡观测对模型参数给出观测约束。

Method: 求解含尘埃场的静态球对称黑洞解，分析视界结构与有效势、ISCO、边缘束缚轨道、边缘圆轨道及Keplerian与径向本轮频率；据此构建相对论进动、翘曲盘、本轮共振三种双峰QPO模型及3:2、4:3、5:4共振半径；对两颗恒星级与两颗中等质量源的QPO数据做MCMC拟合。

Result: K与α作用相反：增大K使内外视界趋近合并为极端视界，增大α趋向Schwarzschild极限；轨道半径随K内移、随α外移；三种模型共振半径呈同样趋势；MCMC给出2σ约束K∈(0,0.5)、α∈(0.3,0.6)。

Conclusion: 量子涨落参数与尘埃场强度参数对黑洞时空及QPO特征具相反调控作用，且多源多模型的观测约束一致集中于较小参数区间，支持该修正引力框架的可检验性。

Abstract: We study a static, spherically symmetric black hole solution surrounded by a dust field within quantum fluctuation modified gravity, in which the quantum-fluctuation parameter $α$ and the dust-field strength parameter $K$. The horizon structure reveals the two parameters acting in opposite directions: strengthening $K$ draws the inner and outer horizons together until they merge into a single extremal horizon, while a larger $α$ restores the Schwarzschild limit; the same opposing trend extends to the effective potential and the characteristic circular-orbit radii -- the innermost stable circular orbit, the marginally bound orbit, and the marginal circular orbit -- which shift inward with $K$ and outward with $α$, and to the Keplerian and radial epicyclic frequencies of massive test particles. From these frequencies we build three twin-peak quasi-periodic oscillation models -- relativistic precession, warped-disk, and epicyclic-resonance -- along with their $3{:}2$, $4{:}3$, and $5{:}4$ resonance radii, all following the same $K$-$α$ trend. Testing all three models against twin-peak QPO data from two stellar-mass (GRO~J1655$-$40, XTE~J1550$-$564) and two intermediate-mass (M82~X$-$1, NGC~1313~X$-$1) sources through a Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis places best-fit constraints, at the $2σ$ level, of $K\in(0,0.5)$ and $α\in(0.3,0.6)$ across all sources and models.

</details>


### [180] [Time and causality in quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22890)
*Charis Anastopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从更广义视角审视量子引力中的时间问题，指出时间困难并非仅源于引力量子化，而是已潜藏于经典力学、相对论、量子论与量子场论之中；文章以因果次序、时间延续与"现在"三个面向为线索，评述各大量子引力方案对时间问题的回应，并强调该问题在弱引力、近平坦时空的量子描述中即已出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上"时间问题"常被视为量子理论与广义相对论结合的产物，本文认为这一框架过于狭窄。作者希望说明：在与量子引力相关的既有理论（经典力学、相对论、量子论、量子场论）中，因果结构、时钟、可观测量与测量之间就已存在张力，因此有必要在更广的概念背景下重新定位时间问题，并据此审视各量子引力方案。

Method: 采用概念分析与方案比较的方法：首先梳理时间在经典力学、相对论、量子理论与量子场论中进入理论的方式，区分因果次序、时间延续、"现在"三个主要面向；随后系统考察主流量子引力进路对时间问题的处理，包括正则量子化与Wheeler-DeWitt量子化、路径积分与历史表述、微扰及背景依赖进路、因果集、扭量理论；并按各方案是否保留外部时间、是否因量子化而使时间成为问题、是否将因果或时间结构纳入基础来分类比较；最后结合量子基础与相对论量子信息的问题，并分析线性化引力与弱引力量子系统。

Result: 分析表明，时间相关的概念困难并不只在引力被量子化时才出现，而是普遍存在于构建量子引力所依据的各理论之中。各量子引力方案可依据其对待时间的方式加以区分：或保留外部时间，或经由量子化使时间成为待解难题，或直接把因果/时间结构作为理论基础。

Conclusion: 时间问题并不局限于普朗克尺度物理。在线性化引力与弱引力量子系统中，为近平坦几何下的时空提供具有预测能力且具有操作意义的量子描述时，时间问题便已经出现，因此应将其视为更广泛的量子基础与相对论量子信息问题，而非单纯的量子引力技术难题。

Abstract: The problem of time in quantum gravity is often presented as a consequence of applying quantum theory to general relativity. These notes adopt a broader perspective. We first examine how time enters classical mechanics, relativity, quantum theory, and quantum field theory, distinguishing three principal aspects: causal order, temporal duration, and the present. This analysis shows that the conceptual difficulties associated with time do not arise only when gravity is quantised. Tensions between causal structure, clocks, observables, and measurement are already present in the theories from which quantum gravity is constructed. We then survey responses to the problem of time across the principal approaches to quantum gravity. These include canonical and Wheeler-DeWitt quantisation, path-integral and histories formulations, perturbative and background-dependent approaches, causal sets, and twistor theory. The programmes are compared according to whether they retain an external time, render time problematic through quantisation, or incorporate causal or temporal structure into their foundations. Particular emphasis is placed on questions arising from quantum foundations and relativistic quantum information. We conclude by examining linearised gravity and weak-gravity quantum systems. This regime shows that the problem of time is not confined to Planck-scale physics, but arises already in attempts to provide a predictive and operationally meaningful quantum description of spacetime near flat geometry.

</details>


### [181] [Modified Gravity Framework for the Woods-Saxon Inflation: Theoretical Foundations and Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22933)
*Feyzollah Younesizadeh,Davoud Kamani,Younes Younesizadeh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在扩展引力理论中引入非最小耦合 $f(\phi)T$ 并采用 Woods-Saxon 势，得到与最新观测数据吻合的暴胀模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构建一个修改引力框架下的暴胀情景，该框架通过膨胀子 $\phi$ 与能量-动量张量迹 $T$ 的非最小耦合扩展 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量，并期望在暴胀后宇宙中自然恢复标准广义相对论。

Method: 方法上采用 $f(\phi)T$ 框架并应用 Woods-Saxon 势，分析慢滚动力学。对于特定耦合函数选择，得到光速声速 $c_s^2=1$。

Result: 在模型参数 $a \lesssim 1$ 与适中的耦合 $\beta=0.1$ 下，得到标量谱指数 $n_s \approx 0.959$–$0.966$ 和张量-标量比 $r<0.01$，与最新的 Planck 和 BICEP/Keck 关于 $(n_s, r)$ 约束的数据吻合良好。并展示了 $f(\phi)T$ 耦合对模型在 CMB 两点统计下的观测可行性至关重要。

Conclusion: 结论：本工作在所考虑的暴胀观测量范围内，建立了一个可行的暴胀情景。

Abstract: We investigate an inflationary scenario within a modified gravity theory that extends the Einstein-Hilbert action through a non-minimal coupling between the inflaton $φ$ and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor $T$. This $f(φ)T$ framework is designed to naturally recover the standard general relativity in the post-inflationary universe. Within this theory, we apply the Woods-Saxon potential. The slow-roll dynamics yields a luminal sound speed $c_s^2=1$ for our specific choice of the coupling function. For the model parameters $a \lesssim 1$ and the modest coupling $β=0.1$, we obtain a scalar spectral index $n_s\approx0.959$--$0.966$ and a tensor-to-scalar ratio $r<0.01$, which reveal a good agreement with the latest Planck and BICEP/Keck data on the $(n_s, r)$ constraints. Crucially, we shall demonstrate that the $f(φ)T$ coupling is essential for the observational viability of the model with respect to the CMB two-point statistics. This work establishes a viable inflationary scenario within the scope of the inflationary observables which are considered here.

</details>


### [182] [Quasinormal modes in the field of a dyon-like dilatonic black hole corresponding to $A_1 + A_1$ Toda chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22999)
*U. S. Kayumov,S. V. Bolokhov,V. D. Ivashchuk,A. N. Malybayev,G. S. Nurbakova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了非极端膨胀双荷黑洞背景下无质量测试标量场的准正规模，在eikonal极限下推导并分析了准正规模谱，并进行了高阶WKB数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索膨胀双荷黑洞这一含两个标量场和两个2-形式的四维引力模型解中的标量场准正规模，检验其在特定参数极限下是否回归已知黑洞（Schwarzschild与Reissner-Nordström）的准正规模谱。

Method: 考虑四维引力模型中的非极端膨胀双荷黑洞解，由满足 $\vec{\lambda}_1=\vec{\lambda}_2=\vec{\lambda}$ 且 $\vec{\lambda}^2=1/2$ 的两个膨胀耦合向量控制；在eikonal极限下推导无质量标量测试场的准正规模，并对两个低阶模式采用高阶WKB方法进行数值求解。

Result: 准正规模由解参数 $\mu>0$、$P_1>0$、$P_2>0$ 决定；在 $P_1=P_2=0$ 与 $P_1=P_2=P>0$ 两个极限下，结果分别精确退化为Schwarzschild与Reissner-Nordström黑洞的已知（eikonal）准正规模谱；同时给出了不同参数取值下两个低阶模式的高阶WKB数值频率。

Conclusion: 膨胀双荷黑洞的eikonal准正规模谱在相应极限下与Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞一致，验证了该解与已知黑洞谱的连续性，并提供了高阶WKB数值结果。

Abstract: We explore the quasinormal modes of a massless test scalar field in the gravitational background of a non-extremal dilatonic dyonic black hole. The dyon-like black hole solution is considered within a four-dimensional gravitational model that includes two scalar fields and two 2-forms. It is governed by two dilatonic coupling vectors $\vecλ_s$ (with $s=1,2$) that satisfy $\vecλ_1 = \vecλ_2 = \vecλ$ and $\vecλ^2 = 1/2$. We derive and examine the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar test field in the eikonal limit. These modes are governed by the solution parameters $μ> 0$, $P_1 > 0$, and $P_2 > 0$. In the two limiting scenarios -- namely $P_1 = P_2 = 0$ and $P_1 = P_2 = P > 0$ -- our results reduce exactly to the known (eikonal) quasinormal spectra for the Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes, respectively. Numerical higher-order WKB analysis of quasinormal frequencies for two lower levels (for various values of parameters) is also presented.

</details>


### [183] [Energy-momentum-squared gravity: charged quark star solutions with unified interacting matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23014)
*Ayan Banerjee,Bobur Turimov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Faisal Javed,Gulnoza Palvanova,Sulton Usanov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在能量-动量平方引力(EMSG)框架下，采用含微扰QCD修正、色超导和有限奇异夸克质量效应的统一相互作用夸克物质状态方程，研究带电夸克星的平衡结构。结果表明电荷显著增大最大质量和半径，强负耦合下可达约3太阳质量、致密度约0.34，与GW190814次天体质量范围相符，且与多个脉冲星及NICER观测约束一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修改引力(EMSG)与电场电荷共同作用下夸克星的结构，检验带电致密天体能否解释观测中质量偏大的候选天体（如GW190814次天体），并评估其与现行天体物理约束的相容性。

Method: 采用统一相互作用夸克物质状态方程，纳入微扰QCD修正、色超导及有限奇异夸克质量效应；在广泛EMSG耦合强度和相互作用参数范围内求解修改的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff(TOV)方程，计算带电夸克星的平衡结构、最大质量、半径、致密度、中心密度并检验因果性。

Result: 引入电荷相比中性构型显著提高最大质量和半径；在强负耦合和强相互作用下恒星模型质量接近3太阳质量，致密度约0.34，落入GW190814次天体的推断质量区间；电荷使最大质量处的中心密度降低约10%–15%，同时完全满足因果性要求；与PSR J1614−2230、J0348+0432、J0740+6620、J0952−0607及近期NICER半径测量的比较表明，带电EMSG构型在较宽参数空间内与当前天体物理约束一致。

Conclusion: 适度的电荷结合非线性物质-几何耦合可持续存在比广义相对论预言更重的夸克星，凸显带电致密天体在修改引力与多信使天体物理中潜在的观测相关性。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium structure of electrically charged quark stars within the framework of energy-momentum-squared gravity (EMSG), adopting a unified interacting quark-matter equation of state that incorporates perturbative QCD corrections, color superconductivity, and finite-strange-quark-mass effects. By solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations over a broad range of EMSG coupling strengths and interaction parameters, we show that the inclusion of electric charge substantially increases both the maximum mass and radius compared with neutral configurations. For strong negative couplings and pronounced interaction strengths, the resulting stellar models reach masses close to $3,M_{\odot}$ with compactness ratios of order $0.34$, placing them within the mass range inferred for the secondary component of GW190814, whose nature remains observationally undetermined. The presence of charge reduces the central density at maximum mass by approximately $10$--$15\%$ while maintaining full compliance with causality requirements. Furthermore, comparisons with observational constraints from PSR J1614$-$2230, J0348$+$0432, J0740$+$6620, J0952$-$0607, and recent NICER radius measurements indicate that charged EMSG configurations remain consistent with current astrophysical bounds over a wide parameter space. These results demonstrate that a moderate electric charge, when combined with nonlinear matter-geometry coupling, can support quark stars that are significantly more massive than those predicted in general relativity, underscoring the potential observational relevance of charged compact objects in modified gravity and multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [184] [Null Geodesics in the Reissner-Nordström Black Hole Spacetime Pierced by a Cosmic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23025)
*Tianxu Huo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了被宇宙弦穿过的Reissner-Nordström黑洞时空中的零测地线，给出三类零测地线的解析解，并通过数值模拟分析电荷Q与宇宙弦参数α对光轨迹和偏折角的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reissner-Nordström黑洞与宇宙弦的组合时空具有重要的物理意义，但该时空中的零测地线结构及其受电荷和宇宙弦参数影响的方式尚需系统研究。

Method: 求解零测地线运动方程，根据|Q|/M比值将零测地线分类，获得各类别的解析解；基于解析解数值模拟光轨迹；在赤道面推导偏折角表达式。

Result: 零测地线可分为三大类，分类完全由|Q|/M决定；宇宙弦参数α增强三类零测地线的环绕行为；偏折角随电荷Q增大而减小，随宇宙弦参数α增大而增大。

Conclusion: 该时空的零测地线结构由电荷质量比决定，宇宙弦参数显著影响光传播的环绕与偏折特性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate null geodesics in the spacetime of a Reissner-Nordström black hole pierced by a cosmic string. Analysis of the equations of motion reveals that null geodesics in this conical spacetime can be classified into three main categories, with the classification entirely determined by the ratio $|Q|/M$ (where $Q$ and $M$ denote the black hole charge and mass, respectively). Analytical solutions to the null geodesic equations are obtained for each category. Based on these solutions, we numerically simulate light trajectories and analyze in detail the effects of the black hole charge $Q$ and the cosmic string parameter $α$. It is found that the cosmic string parameter $α$ enhances the winding behavior of null geodesics across all three main categories. Finally, we consider light deflection in the equatorial plane and derive an expression for the deflection angle, revealing that the charge $Q$ decreases the deflection angle, whereas the cosmic string parameter $α$ increases it.

</details>


### [185] [LQG-inspired black hole solutions in (2+1)-dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23044)
*Zijian Shi,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文首次构建了三种独立的 LQG 启发的 (2+1) 维黑洞框架，发现加入量子引力修正后可同时得到渐近平坦、AdS 和 dS 三类解，从而消除了经典 (2+1) 维与 (3+1) 维解之间的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典 (2+1) 维广义相对论中，黑洞解仅存在于 anti-de Sitter (AdS) 情形，即 BTZ 黑洞，这与 (3+1) 维存在多种渐近几何的黑洞形成鲜明不对称。作者希望考察引入有效量子引力效应后，这一不对称是否仍存在。

Method: 构建三种独立的 LQG 启发框架：$\bar\mu$-scheme 和乐修正 (holonomy corrections)、量子 Oppenheimer-Snyder 坍缩以及一个协变有效模型，并将其应用于 (2+1) 维黑洞的求解。

Result: 三种方法给出相同的定性结果：在包含 LQG 启发修正后，均出现三类不同解，分别对应渐近平坦、anti-de Sitter 和 de Sitter 几何。

Conclusion: 将有效量子引力效应纳入后，经典 (2+1) 维与 (3+1) 维解之间的不对称性被消除，表明 (2+1) 维量子引力修正下黑洞解的丰富程度可与 (3+1) 维情形相媲美。

Abstract: It is well known that in (2+1)-dimensional general relativity, black hole solutions exist only in the anti-de Sitter case--the so-called Banados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole. In this letter, we construct, for the first time, three independent LQG-inspired frameworks for (2+1)-dimensional black holes: the $\barμ$-scheme holonomy corrections, the quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder collapse, and a covariant effective model. Remarkably, all three approaches yield the same qualitative picture: when LQG-inspired corrections are included, we find three distinct types of solutions corresponding to asymptotically flat, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter geometries. Our results demonstrate that the asymmetry between classical (2+1)-dimensional and (3+1)-dimensional solutions is eliminated upon incorporating effective quantum gravity effects.

</details>


### [186] [Charged particles and QPO constraints in tidal charge black hole magnetosphere sourced by a current loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23187)
*Ozodbek Abdurakhmonov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Inomjon Ibragimov,Sherzod Djumanov,Nuraliev Farhod,lkhomjon Makhmudov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究Randall-Sundrum膜世界潮汐荷黑洞与ISCO处电流环偶极磁场中带电粒子动力学，推导ISCO量与三基频，构建五种双峰QPO模型并用MCMC拟合六个黑洞源，给出潮汐荷与磁耦合参数上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Randall-Sundrum膜世界模型中的潮汐荷参数与电磁耦合效应能否通过黑洞X射线准周期振荡（QPO）观测加以约束，特别是考察静态非旋转背景下偶极电流环磁场是否能产生非零节点进动频率。

Method: 在潮汐荷黑洞弯曲时空中求解Maxwell方程得到ISCO处电流环的解析方位磁四势；基于Hamilton-Jacobi方程推导环内外带电粒子有效势，分析ISCO半径、角动量、能量、轨道频率对潮汐荷b与磁耦合ω的依赖；计算三个基本频率；据此构建RP、WD及ER2-ER4五种双峰QPO模型，对GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564、GRS 1915+105、H 1743+322、M82 X-1、Sgr A*六个源的观测频率进行MCMC参数约束。

Result: 偶极环场在静态非旋转背景下恢复了非零节点进动频率。以ER4共振模型为代表，MCMC拟合给出中等且不可忽略的潮汐荷b≈0.3-1.0，磁耦合与零一致，|ω|≲0.05，从而对潮汐荷与磁耦合强度给出上限。

Conclusion: 利用天体物理双峰QPO数据可有效约束Randall-Sundrum膜世界参数：潮汐荷b偏向0.3-1.0的中等值，磁耦合被限制在很弱范围内，为膜世界模型提供观测检验途径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles in the dipolar electromagnetic field generated by a stationary current loop around a static black hole in the Randall--Sundrum braneworld model. The current loop is located at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of the tidal charged black hole, and the azimuthal electromagnetic four-potential is obtained as an exact analytical solution of the Maxwell equations in the curved background. Starting from the Hamilton--Jacobi equation, we derive the effective potential for charged particle motion in both the interior and exterior regions of the current loop, and analyze the dependence of the ISCO radius, angular momentum, energy, and orbital frequency on the tidal charge parameter $b$ and the magnetic coupling parameter $ω$. We further compute three fundamental frequencies and show that the dipolar loop field revives a nonzero nodal precession frequency even though the background is static and non-rotating. Using these frequencies we construct five twin-peak QPO models (RP, WD, and ER2--ER4) and perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis to constrain the braneworld parameters $(b,ω)$ from the observed twin-peak QPO frequencies of six black hole sources spanning three mass regimes: GRO~J1655--40, XTE~J1550--564, GRS~1915$+$105, H~1743$+$322, M82~X-1, and Sgr~A$^{*}$. Adopting the epicyclic-resonance ER4 model as the representative framework, we find moderate, non-negligible tidal charges $b\simeq0.3$--$1.0$, while the magnetic coupling remains consistent with zero, $|ω|\lesssim0.05$. Our results therefore place upper bounds on the tidal charge and on the magnetic coupling strength from astrophysical QPO data.

</details>


### [187] [Renormalized Dynamical Friction in Black-Hole Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23192)
*Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了紧凑天体在黑洞时空中穿过物质时引力自力(gravitational self-force)的重整化局域运动方程，利用Schwinger-Keldysh围道对环境和度规扰动积分得到因果有效作用量，并给出适用于数值自力计算的重整化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑天体在物质环境中运动的引力自力问题需要一致的局域运动方程与可数值实现的重整化方案，而环境尾流(wake)的引力反作用会引入紫外发散，现有框架尚缺乏针对这一情形完整的重整化处方。

Method: 在Schwinger-Keldysh围道上积掉环境与度规扰动，得到由曲时空费曼图求和给出的因果有效作用量（按质量比和环境密度幂次组织）；领头阶的环境尾流对世界线引力反作用，其图含对数紫外发散，重整化领头耗散性流体-粒子耦合；用局域大动量展开分离发散（此时曲时空推迟格林函数退化为平坦切空间形式），并将奇异性翻译到Barack-Ori模式求和方案，解析给出理想流体环境的穿孔(puncture)和正则化参数；对无碰撞尘埃，通过与黑洞穿行介质的精确近区计算匹配确定有限Wilson系数。

Result: 得到领头阶对数紫外发散的重整化对象（耗散性流体-粒子耦合）；给出理想流体环境下Barack-Ori模式求和的解析穿孔与正则化参数；模式减除可定点发散部分但不能确定有限的Wilson系数；对无碰撞尘埃，通过匹配精确近区计算确定了该有限系数，从而形成完整重整化处方。

Conclusion: 该工作为黑洞时空中紧凑天体在物质环境里运动的引力自力提供了完整的重整化处方，可直接用于数值自力计算。由于模式减除无法确定有限Wilson系数，需借助与精确近区计算的匹配来补全，这表明完整方案需要结合局域重整化与全局匹配。

Abstract: We derive the renormalized local equation of motion for the gravitational self-force on a compact object moving through matter in a black-hole spacetime. We integrate out the environment and metric perturbations on a Schwinger--Keldysh contour, obtaining a causal effective action given by a sum of curved-spacetime Feynman diagrams organized by powers of the mass ratio and environmental density. At leading order, an environmental wake gravitationally backreacts on the worldline. The corresponding diagram contains a logarithmic ultraviolet divergence that renormalizes the leading dissipative fluid--particle coupling. We isolate the divergence with a local large-momentum expansion, in which the curved-spacetime retarded Green's function reduces to its flat tangent-space form. We translate the resulting singularity into a Barack--Ori mode-sum scheme, deriving an analytic puncture and regularization parameters for an ideal-fluid environment. Mode subtraction fixes the divergent part but not the finite Wilson coefficient. For collisionless dust, we determine this coefficient by matching to an exact near-zone calculation for a black hole moving through the medium. This gives a complete renormalization prescription suitable for numerical self-force calculations.

</details>


### [188] [Instability of naked singularities of perfect fluid under $C^{1,α}$ gravitational perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23357)
*Junbin Li,Tingting Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明Einstein–Euler系统球对称自相似坍缩中出现的裸奇点在引力扰动下不稳定：初始共形度规的小$C^{1,\alpha}$（无对称性）扰动会导致陷获面形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称自相似坍缩中会产生裸奇点，但其在非对称引力扰动下的稳定性尚不清楚；本文旨在检验这些裸奇点是否稳健，从而判断其物理相关性。

Method: 对爱因斯坦–欧拉系统的球对称自相似坍缩解施加无任何对称性的小$C^{1,\alpha}$初始共形度规扰动，并利用声速严格小于光速这一性质进行扰动分析。

Result: 小$C^{1,\alpha}$扰动导致陷获面形成；关键在于声速严格慢于光速，使引力辐射能在流体产生任何奇点之前充分汇聚并形成陷获面。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦–欧拉系统中球对称自相似坍缩的裸奇点在一般引力扰动下是不稳定的，因此可能并不具有物理意义。

Abstract: We study the instability of the naked singularities arising in the spherically symmetric self-similar collapsing of the Einstein--Euler system under gravitational perturbations. We show that small $C^{1,α}$ perturbations (without any symmetries) of the initial conformal metric lead to trapped surface formation. One key point is that the speed of sound is strictly slower the the speed of light, so the gravitational radiation can become sufficiently concentrated to form a trapped surface before the fluid develops any singularity.

</details>


### [189] [Optimal all-angle reconstruction of the Hellings-Downs curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23400)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用全脉冲星对协方差联合重建Hellings-Downs角相关曲线的方法，在每个角分bin保留自由幅度，获得全角最佳线性无偏估计量，显著降低bin标准差，提升引力波背景测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列通过不同脉冲星计时残差间的空间相关性探测纳赫兹引力波，对广义相对论下各向同性、非偏振随机背景，系综平均相关应服从Hellings-Downs曲线，测量该角模式可检验信号的引力波起源。传统逐bin重建在每个角分bin内独立优化权重，未利用bin间相关性信息，因而估计精度受限，需要更优的联合重建方法来提高测量精度。

Method: 采用全脉冲星对协方差联合重建HD曲线，在每个bin保留自由幅度，构建全角最佳线性无偏估计量（BLUE），使每个bin值及任意bin线性组合的方差最小化。将该方法应用于公开的NANOGrav 15年数据产品，并对类似SKAO的未来PTA进行预测评估。

Result: 在NANOGrav 15年数据上，方法将bin标准差最多降低12.9%，中位降低9.5%；对类似SKAO的PTA，预测降低幅度达36.4%，中位为29.6%。

Conclusion: 基于全脉冲星对协方差的联合重建方法优于传统逐bin独立重建，能显著降低角相关曲线各bin的不确定度，从而实现对引力波背景更精确的测量，对未来SKAO等PTA尤其有效。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) detect nanohertz gravitational waves through spatial correlations between the timing residuals of different pulsars. For an isotropic, unpolarized stochastic background in general relativity, the ensemble-mean correlation follows the Hellings--Downs (HD) curve; measuring this angular pattern tests the gravitational-wave origin of the signal. Standard bin-by-bin reconstructions optimize the weights within each angular bin separately. We reconstruct the curve jointly using the full pulsar-pair covariance, retaining a free amplitude in every bin. The resulting all-angle best linear unbiased estimator minimizes the variance of every bin value and every linear combination of bins. Applied to the public NANOGrav 15 yr data products, our method reduces the bin standard deviations by up to 12.9\%, with a median reduction of 9.5\%. For a future Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO)-like PTA, the predicted reduction reaches 36.4\%, with a median of 29.6\%, enabling significantly more precise measurements of the gravitational-wave background.

</details>


### [190] [Grey-body factor from correspondence with quasinormal mode for gravitational perturbation of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23434)
*Hyewon Han,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究高维Reissner-Nordström黑洞准正规模（QNM）与灰体因子（GBF）之间的对应关系，系统计算了标量、矢量、张量微扰（含标量与矢量的$(+)$、$(-)$型）的QNM与GBF，检验对应关系的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QNM与GBF之间基于WKB推导的对应关系在高维带电黑洞、不同微扰类型与参数下是否成立，明确其适用范围与偏差来源。

Method: 对高维Reissner-Nordström黑洞的引力微扰，采用连分数法计算QNM；根据Frobenius级数的奇点结构，必要时采用through-midpoints积分法；并将由对应关系得到的GBF与数值积分结果进行比较，评估各时空维度$D$与电荷下的精度。

Result: 对应关系对标量$(+)$、矢量$(+)$、矢量$(-)$和张量微扰给出良好近似；但标量$(-)$型在$D>6$低至中等电荷处偏差显著，高电荷时恢复合理精度，尽管有效势出现与WKB推导假设不符的双峰结构。其他类型随$D$增大对应关系变差，而标量$(-)$型在$D=8$反而优于$D=7$；矢量$(-)$微扰在近极端黑洞中表现良好，即使$D>6$时势呈双峰。

Conclusion: QNM与GBF对应关系的适用范围比WKB近似假设所预期的更广，但存在类型与维度依赖的偏差，尤其标量$(-)$型在特定参数区需谨慎使用。

Abstract: We investigate the correspondence between quasinormal modes (QNMs) and grey-body factors (GBFs) of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black holes. Considering gravitational perturbations of these black holes, we systematically compute the QNMs and GBFs for scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, including the $(+)$ and $(-)$ types of scalar and vector perturbations, to examine the validity of the correspondence. The QNMs are computed using the continued fraction method, and the integration-through-midpoints method is applied when required by the singularity structure of the Frobenius series for each perturbation type and set of black-hole parameters. We evaluate the accuracy of the correspondence for each spacetime dimension $D$ and black-hole charge by comparing the GBFs obtained from the correspondence with those obtained by numerical integration. The correspondence provides a good approximation to the GBFs for the scalar($+$), vector($+$), vector($-$), and tensor perturbations. However, for the scalar($-$) type, the correspondence exhibits pronounced deviations at low to intermediate charges in $D > 6$. At higher charges, it recovers reasonable accuracy despite the effective potential exhibiting a double-peak structure inconsistent with the assumptions underlying the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB)-based derivation of the correspondence. In contrast to the other types, for which the correspondence deteriorates as $D$ increases, the scalar($-$) type is more accurate in $D=8$ than in $D=7$. Furthermore, the correspondence performs well for the vector($-$) perturbation of near-extreme black holes, even though the potential develops a double-peak structure in $D>6$. These results indicate that the correspondence is applicable over a broader range than expected from the assumptions of the WKB approximation.

</details>


### [191] [ACT-compatible Inflation in Scalar Field Coupled $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23511)
*Shin'ichi Nojiri,S. D. Odintsov,V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 引力框架下构建满足 ACT 暴胀约束的模型，并讨论暴胀后重加热阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 引力（等价于 $f(Q,B)$ 或 $f(Q,C)$）能否给出与 ACT 观测约束相容的暴胀模型，并探讨其与标量场耦合 $f(R)$ 引力的对应关系及自由度问题。

Method: 利用与 $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 理论相容的重构技术，提出两种满足约束的哈勃率 $H$ 函数形式，并在 $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 引力及 $f(R)$ 标量场耦合引力中构造实现这些 $H$ 行为的模型，同时分析重加热阶段。

Result: 成功构造出两种满足 ACT 暴胀约束的哈勃率 $H$ 演化形式，并在两类引力框架下给出相应实现模型；$f(R)$ 标量场耦合情形可改写为 Einstein 引力加两个标量场。

Conclusion: $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 引力可作为实现 ACT 相容暴胀的可行框架，但 $f(Q)$ 引力动力学自由度数目尚未确定，导致 $f(Q,\tilde R)$ 情形存在非平凡困难。

Abstract: In this work, we construct models compatible with ACT constraints on inflation in the framework of $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity, with $f(R)$ gravity coupled to a scalar field. The $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory is equivalent to $f(Q, B)$ gravity models or $f(Q, C)$ gravity where $B$ or $C$ is the difference between $Q$ and the scalar curvature $\tilde R$ in Einstein's gravity, so, $B=Q-\tilde R$ or $C=\tilde R - Q$. Using appropriate reconstruction techniques compatible with $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory, we propose two functional behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ that satisfy the constraints, and we construct models that realize these behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ using $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity and $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field. We also discuss the reheating stage after inflation. Although the scenario of $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field is well-known, since the model can be rewritten in the form of Einstein's gravity coupled with two scalar fields, the non-trivial problem occurs in the $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity case, because the number of dynamical degrees of freedom in $f(Q)$ gravity is not settled.

</details>


### [192] [Bi-scalar-tensor theory: a spatially covariant gravity approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23704)
*Jia-Jun Chen,Xian Gao,Tsutomu Kobayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造无鬼多场高阶导数标量-张量理论的新方法：从空间协变引力出发，耦合额外标量场，通过哈密顿约束分析得到消除不想要标量模的简并条件与一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多场高阶导数标量-张量理论常伴随鬼（ghost）自由度，即不希望的额外模，需要系统方法构造无鬼理论。

Method: 从空间协变引力出发并耦合额外标量场，得到可理解为双标量-张量理论（其中一个标量取幺正规范）的空间协变标量场理论；在度规速度通过外曲率、额外标量通过最高二阶时间导数的广泛类别中，进行哈密顿约束分析，推导简并条件与附加一致性条件，并研究双场disformal变换及其可逆条件。

Result: 该理论一般传播五个物理自由度（两个张量模和三个标量模），其中一个标量模为额外标量引入的不想要模；当简并条件和一致性条件同时满足时，不想要模被消除，理论传播四个自由度。结果理论分为两类：三次约束闭合不产生新约束，以及生成四次约束。还给出了双场disformal变换的可逆条件及其幺正规范表达。

Conclusion: 该构造为构建含高阶导数的双场及多场标量-张量理论提供了系统方法，可实现无鬼自由度传播。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for constructing ghost-free multi-field higher-derivative scalar-tensor theories. The idea is to start from spatially covariant gravity and couple it to an additional scalar field. The resulting spatially covariant scalar field theory can be understood as a bi-scalar-tensor theory in the unitary gauge of one scalar. Within a broad class in which metric velocities enter through the extrinsic curvature and the additional scalar enters through temporal derivatives up to second order, we perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis. We show that the theory generally propagates five physical degrees of freedom (DOFs), i.e., two tensor modes and three scalar modes. One of the scalar modes is the unwanted mode associated with the additional scalar. We then derive a degeneracy condition and an additional consistency condition. When both conditions hold, the unwanted mode is eliminated and the theory propagates four degrees of freedom. The resulting theories fall into two cases, depending on whether the preservation of the tertiary constraint closes without a new constraint or generates a quaternary constraint. We also study the two-field disformal transformation, derive its invertibility condition, and express it in the unitary gauge. Our construction provides a systematic approach to constructing bi- and multi-scalar-tensor theories with higher derivatives.

</details>


### [193] [Black holes in depleted Dehnen dark matter halos with physical inner edges and strong-field observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23750)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Soroush Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 构建了被耗竭Dehnen暗物质晕包围的静态球对称黑洞时空，推导度规函数、红移及参数约束，分析光子球与阴影等强场观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究暗物质晕对黑洞时空几何与强场观测特征的影响，摒弃动态吸积过程，转而刻画黑洞吸收内边缘物质后形成的最终平衡构型。

Method: 构建静态球对称黑洞-暗物质晕解析模型：将规定内边缘以内的初始晕质量并入中心黑洞，在着装视界与残余晕之间留下真空间隙；推导一般度规函数与红移相位，给出γ=0,1,2,5/2的显式解；利用主导能量条件、全局可容许性及稳定圆轨道条件约束参数；计算光子球与阴影。

Result: 视界半径依赖于Dehnen尖点指数γ与被吸收晕质量，去晕时精确回到Schwarzschild解；主导能量条件给出普适局域下界κ≥5/4，全局可容许性还依赖尖点指数与晕致密度；若在晕边缘施加稳定圆轨道，则稳定Einstein团簇解释要求κ≥3；阐明了尖点与耗竭尺度对强场观测量的影响，已有解析黑洞-晕几何及Schwarzschild、弱晕极限均为特例。

Conclusion: 该通用构造统一并推广了此前黑洞-暗物质晕解析时空，为通过强场观测量（光子球、阴影）探测暗物质晕的尖点结构与耗竭尺度提供了参数化框架。

Abstract: We construct a static and spherically symmetric black hole (BH) spacetime surrounded by a depleted Dehnen dark matter (DM) halo. The model describes a final equilibrium configuration rather than dynamical accretion process: the initial halo mass inside a prescribed inner edge is incorporated into the central BH, leaving a vacuum gap between the dressed horizon and the surviving halo. Consequently, the horizon radius depends on the Dehnen cusp index $γ$ and the absorbed halo mass, while the Schwarzschild result is recovered exactly when the halo is removed. We derive general expressions for the metric functions and redshift phase and obtain explicit solutions for $γ=0,1,2,$ and $5/2$. The dominant energy condition imposes the universal local bound $κ\geq5/4$, whereas global admissibility depends additionally on the cusp index and halo compactness. A literal stable Einstein-cluster interpretation requires the stronger condition $κ\geq3$ when stable circular motion is imposed at the halo edge. We also analyze the photon sphere, shadow, and admissible parameter space, demonstrating how the halo cusp and depletion scale affect strong-field observables.
