Previously studied analytic BH--halo geometries and the Schwarzschild and weak-halo limits follow as special cases of the general construction.

</details>


### [194] [Equivalence of the wave function of the universe in the Einstein and Jordan frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23785)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Sumanta Chakraborty,Soumitra SenGupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究标量-张量及f(R)引力在量子宇宙学微超空间中爱因斯坦框架与约当框架的等价性，发现等价性取决于算符排序方案，且加入流体时钟通常破坏等价性（辐射流体除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清爱因斯坦框架与约当框架在量子层面是否等价，是引力理论与量子宇宙学中的核心争议问题，以往工作多集中于经典层面或特定模型，缺乏对一般标量-张量及f(R)理论的系统量子分析。

Method: 在齐次各向同性FLRW度规的微超空间中构建量子哈密顿量，采用正则量子化与路径积分两种形式框架，考察不同算符排序方案（含Laplace-Beltrami算符）下的点正则变换协变性，并引入具有任意状态方程参数w的流体时钟进行分析。

Result: 若量子理论在微超空间点正则变换下协变，则两框架等价；对包括Laplace-Beltrami算符在内的一大类算符排序，两框架的宇宙波函数由连接两框架的同一变换相联系，即量子力学等价；加入任意w的流体时钟会破坏该等价性，仅辐射流体（w=1/3）例外。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦框架与约当框架的量子等价性并非普适，而是依赖于算符排序方案和物质内容；结论对任意标量-张量及f(R)理论在形式层面成立，不依赖具体模型，为框架选择提供了量子层面的判据。

Abstract: We investigate the question of equivalence between the Einstein and Jordan frames for generic scalar-tensor and $f(R)$ gravity theories in the context of minisuperspace quantum cosmology. We consider, particularly, the minisuperspace of a homogeneous and isotropic universe with the Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker metric. We show that the equivalence between the frames depends on the operator ordering scheme of the quantum Hamiltonian: if the quantum theory is covariant under point canonical transformations in the minisuperspace, then the equivalence between the two frames holds. We find that for a large class of operator orderings, including the Laplace-Beltrami operator in the minisuperspace, the wave functions of the universe obtained in the two frames are related by the same transformations that connect the two frames, \textit{i.e.}, the frames are quantum mechanically equivalent. Further, we show that adding a fluid clock with an arbitrary equation of state parameter $w$ to the system breaks the equivalence, except for the case of a radiation fluid ($w=1/3$). All our arguments are presented at a formal level in both canonical and path integral approaches, and for any scalar-tensor, $f(R)$ theories without assuming any particular model.

</details>


### [195] [Signatures in the local interstellar medium of electromagnetic radiation produced by neutron star gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23865)
*Preston Jones,Pragati Pradhan,Douglas Singleton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 旅行者号探测器40多年来探测到几kHz的等离子体振荡，但其天体物理来源仍无公认假说。本文探索引力波背景中电磁辐射协同产生这一新理论，考察中子星是否可能为这些低频振荡的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行者号持续探测到几kHz等离子体振荡，且进入局部星际介质后仍持续存在，来源识别困难。中子星是早期假想的候选源之一，但缺乏解释振荡频率的理论机制，因此需要新理论框架来探究其天体物理起源。

Method: 采用近期发展的引力波背景中电磁辐射协同产生（co-production）理论，将其作为低频振荡的可能来源进行分析，并检验中子星是否可作为该过程的天体物理源。

Result: 摘要未给出具体结果，仅提出将协同产生理论应用于解释旅行者号探测到的等离子体振荡，并评估中子星作为源的可能性。

Conclusion: 论文提出以引力波背景中电磁辐射协同产生理论作为旅行者号等离子体振荡的候选解释，并探讨中子星作为天体物理源的可能性，为这一长期未解问题提供新的研究视角。

Abstract: The Voyager spacecraft have been detecting plasma oscillations of a few kHz for over 40 years. However, there is still no widely accepted hypothesis for the astrophysical source of the oscillations. Having entered the local interstellar medium, these detections have persisted, adding to the challenge of identifying the astrophysical source. Neutron stars were one of the earliest hypothesized candidates of the electromagnetic radiation responsible for the detected plasma oscillations, but no theoretical mechanism could be identified to explain the frequency of the oscillations. We explore the recently developed theory of co-production of electromagnetic radiation in gravitational wave backgrounds as a possible source of these low-frequency oscillations and investigate whether neutron stars could be the astrophysical source.

</details>


### [196] [Exact horizon response and charge ensembles of Majumdar--Papapetrou black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23921)
*Jongheon Baek*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了四维Majumdar-Papapetrou时空的静电Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，给出由电容矩阵统一刻度的视界通量关系，并将其推广至D=n+3维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意多中心极端黑洞（Majumdar-Papapetrou）时空中物质场如何分配通量，尤其是各视界电荷的独立可设性与相互耦合结构，是理解多黑洞平衡构型静电响应的核心问题，且已有文献中的Green函数存在电荷守恒与分量通量不一致的争议。

Method: 对最小耦合旁观Maxwell场，构造精确的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射与接地Green函数；用完整图（视界为顶点）的加权Laplacian表示电容矩阵；对比Frolov-Zelnikov多中心Green函数的平移版本与其定电荷对称版本；对双黑洞情形计算自能修正，并推广至D=n+3维。

Result: 得到电容矩阵C_AA=M_A+Σ_{B≠A}M_AM_B/R_AB、C_AB=-M_AM_B/R_AB，即正对角项加加权图的Laplacian，给出共模/差模能量分解；点电荷按调和测度在无穷远与各视界间分流通量；Frolov-Zelnikov对角线补全版保持总视界电荷但会转移视界间电荷，定电荷Green函数含完整电容矩阵的逆；双黑洞给出有限负半定的自能移动与精确自作用力差（无需额外UV减除）；D=n+3维的图权重带普适因子π^{n/2}/Γ(n/2)。

Conclusion: 多中心极端黑洞的静电响应完全由以质量为权重的图Laplacian电容矩阵编码，可同时刻画总电荷与分量通量两种物理约束；该框架可自然扩展至高维，并为自作用力计算提供无UV发散的精确表述。

Abstract: We determine the electrostatic Dirichlet-to-Neumann map of an arbitrary four-dimensional Majumdar--Papapetrou spacetime. For a minimally coupled spectator Maxwell field, the potentials of the disconnected extremal horizons may be prescribed independently, and the resulting flux charges are related by an exact capacitance matrix. If the centers have masses $M_A$ and coordinate separations $R_{AB}$, its entries are $C_{AA}=M_A+\sum_{B\ne A}M_AM_B/R_{AB}$ and $C_{AB}=-M_AM_B/R_{AB}$. The matrix is a positive diagonal term plus the weighted Laplacian of the complete graph of horizons, giving an exact common-mode and differential-mode decomposition of the field energy. The grounded Dirichlet Green function shows that a point charge partitions its flux among infinity and the horizons according to elementary harmonic measures. A direct translation of the multicenter Green function of Frolov and Zelnikov gives the diagonal completion used in their published Eq.~(68), whose component-resolved Gauss flux preserves the total horizon charge but generally transfers charge between horizons. The symmetric Green function that preserves every horizon charge instead contains the inverse of the full capacitance matrix. For a binary, the difference gives a finite, negative-semidefinite shift of the electrostatic self-energy and a corresponding exact self-force difference without an additional ultraviolet subtraction. We finally extend the response matrix, flux partition, and fixed-charge Green function to $D=n+3$ dimensions, where the graph weights acquire the universal factor $π^{n/2}/Γ(n/2)$.

</details>


### [197] [Soliton solution of a gravitating chiral-quark soliton model: dynamical fermion and Dirac-sea in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24159)
*Ryoma Mizutani,Daiki Niiyama,Nobuyuki Sawado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将手征夸克孤子模型（CQSM）推广至球对称弯曲时空并与爱因斯坦引力耦合，自洽求解CQSM与爱因斯坦方程，得到费米子能谱与ADM质量的谱流，发现狄拉克海稀释能量-动量张量效应并导致负ADM质量的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究静态球对称时空下费米子动力学质量受引力效应的影响，探索在引力背景下包含完整狄拉克海的局域化费米子体系（CQSM）的自洽行为。

Method: 在静态球对称弯曲时空中扩展CQSM，将非线手征场耦合的狄拉克费米子与爱因斯坦引力耦合；CQSM源自大N_c QCD低能区、由正规化单费米子圈导出的含完整狄拉克海的强子模型；自洽求解CQSM方程与爱因斯坦方程。

Result: 成功自洽求解CQSM与爱因斯坦方程，获得费米子能量及ADM质量的谱流；狄拉克海稀释能量-动量张量效应，即使存在局域化物质也会出现引力影响的"非活跃点"；动质量较大（M ~ ⟨ψ̄ψ⟩）时狄拉克海效应占主导，导致负ADM质量出现。

Conclusion: 狄拉克海在弯曲时空CQSM中起关键作用，可稀释引力源效应并产生负ADM质量，表明引力与费米子海相互作用带来非平凡的物理后果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Einstein-Dirac system based on a Dirac fermion coupled with a nonlinear chiral field on static spherically symmetric spacetime. Gravitational effects on the dynamical mass of fermions are examined. The chiral-quark soliton model (CQSM) originally was a model of hadron inspired by the low-energy regime of large-$N_\textrm{c}$ QCD, realizing localized fermions with the full inclusion of the Dirac-sea, which is derived from a regularized one-fermion loop. We extend the CQSM in spherically symmetric curved spacetime and coupled with the Einstein gravity. We successfully solve the CQSM and the Einstein equation self-consistently, and obtain the spectral flow of the fermion energies and also of the ADM mass. The Dirac-sea dilutes the effect of the energy-momentum tensor, which induces an inactive point for the impact of gravity even when the localizing matter exists. The Dirac-sea effect becomes dominant for larger dynamical mass $M\sim \langle\barψψ\rangle$, leading to the emergence of the negative ADM mass.

</details>


### [198] [Signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes in semiclassical gravity with trace anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24273)
*Yuxiang Zuo,Shiyang Hu,Dan Li,Chen Deng,Guansheng He,Wenbin Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在半经典引力（A型迹反常）框架下研究旋转黑洞周围Novikov-Thorne吸积盘的热辐射特征，发现耦合参数抑制盘发射，且高自旋时差异显著，可作为区分半经典引力与广义相对论的观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力通过量子反作用为解决黑洞奇点问题提供了有前景的框架，其效应由耦合参数刻画，因此有必要深入探究此类天体周围的吸积性质。

Method: 在含A型迹反常的半经典引力框架下，计算旋转黑洞周围Novikov-Thorne吸积盘的能量通量密度、温度分布与热光度，并跨多参数空间进行分析。

Result: 引入耦合参数会抑制盘发射：能量通量密度和温度的峰值降低，热光度的峰值及对应峰值频率也较广义相对论中的Kerr情形更低；当黑洞具有高自旋时该差异尤为显著。

Conclusion: 耦合参数增大强化中心致密天体的有效引力场强度，使赤道稳定圆轨道外移并提高轨道物质比能，从而降低辐射效率并削弱盘发射；该抑制机制为区分半经典引力与广义相对论、并通过热盘谱约束耦合参数提供了可行的天体物理途径。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity provides a promising framework to address the black hole singularity problem through quantum backreaction characterized by a coupling parameter, making it essential to thoroughly explore the accretion properties around such objects. In this paper, within the framework of semiclassical gravity with type-A trace anomaly, we investigate the thermal radiative signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes across diverse parameter spaces, focusing on the energy flux density, temperature profile, and thermal luminosity. Our findings demonstrate that the introduction of the coupling parameter suppresses disk emission, leading to lower peak values of both energy flux density and temperature, as well as lower peak values and corresponding peak frequencies in thermal luminosity compared to their Kerr counterparts in general relativity. In particular, this discrepancy becomes remarkably pronounced when the black hole possesses a high spin. Mechanistically, increasing the coupling parameter reinforces the effective gravitational field strength of the central compact object, which pushes the equatorial stable circular orbits outward and increases the specific energy of the orbiting matter, thereby reducing the radiative efficiency and weakening the overall disk emission. This suppression mechanism offers a practical astrophysical window to distinguish semiclassical gravity from general relativity and to place observational constraints on the coupling parameter via thermal disk spectra.

</details>


### [199] [A novel phenomenological parametrization of dynamical dark energy via matter-gravity coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24361)
*Hrishikesh Chakrabarty,Daniele Malafarina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于含非最小物质-引力耦合的Markov-Mukhanov作用量，提出了偏离ΛCDM模型的新参数化方案，并构建可拟合DESI"幻影穿越"观测的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΛCDM模型面临DESI等观测对动力学暗能量及"幻影穿越"的暗示，需要能自然产生动力学暗能量并解释此类偏离的理论框架。

Method: 采用Markov-Mukhanov作用量，引入非最小物质-引力耦合，使宇宙学常数Λ与引力耦合G动力学化；据此构建低密度下偏离广义相对论而产生动力学暗能量的简单唯象模型，并利用观测数据约束自由参数。

Result: 给出了模型自由参数受观测数据约束的方法，并在该形式下解释了DESI合作组关于"幻影穿越"的初步观测迹象。

Conclusion: 非最小物质-引力耦合可自然导致动力学暗能量，为解释ΛCDM偏离及幻影穿越提供了一种可行的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a new parametrization for generic departures from the $Λ$CDM model based on the Markov-Mukhanov action with non-minimal matter-gravity coupling. In this approach the cosmological constant $Λ$ and the gravitational coupling $G$ become dynamical with their values determined by the explicit form of the matter-gravity coupling. We use the framework to build a simple phenomenological model where dynamical dark energy is naturally obtained as a consequence of the departure of the theory from classical General Relativity at low densities. We show how the model's free parameters can be constrained from observational data and interpret the tentative observations of `phantom crossing' by the DESI collaboration in view of the proposed formalism.

</details>


### [200] [Joule Thomson expansion of symmergent black holes in extended phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24400)
*Saheb Soroushfar,Sayyed Mehrab Ramezani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在扩展相空间下研究symmergent黑洞的Joule-Thomson膨胀，发现JT系数仅依赖8πP·r₊²，反转曲线与α参数无关，但等焓曲线仍依赖该参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: symmergent黑洞的熵含有修正参数α，会改变焓与热力学体积。为厘清该参数在Joule-Thomson过程中的作用，并建立自洽的热力学框架，作者在扩展相空间中开展系统分析。

Method: 采用扩展相空间形式，将热力学压强作为独立变量引入；使用含参数α的symmergent熵计算焓与热力学体积，推导Joule-Thomson系数、反转曲线、反转压强与温度。

Result: JT系数仅依赖组合8πP·r₊²，对应反转曲线P_i=(π/2)·I_i²，不依赖α；等焓曲线仍参数化依赖于α。得到反转压强与温度的显式表达式，并区分冷却区P>P_i与加热区P<P_i。

Conclusion: symmergent参数影响热力学轨迹但不影响反转边界本身；正压强下有效几何渐近AdS，可作全息解读，JT膨胀对应对偶场论中的等焓过程。

Abstract: We investigate the Joule Thomson expansion of a symmergent black hole within an extended phase-space formalism in which the thermodynamic pressure is introduced as an independent variable. The symmergent entropy contains the parameter alpha-hat, which modifies the enthalpy and thermodynamic volume relative to their standard forms. We show that the Joule Thomson coefficient depends only on the combination 8piP*r_+^2, leading to an inversion curve P_i = (pi/2) * I_i^2 that is independent of alpha-hat. The isenthalpic curves, however, retain a parametric dependence on alpha-hat, indicating that the symmergent sector affects the thermodynamic trajectories but not the inversion boundary itself. The inversion pressure and temperature are derived explicitly, and the cooling regime P > P_i is distinguished from the heating regime P < P_i. Since the effective geometry is asymptotically AdS-like for positive pressure, the results also admit a holographic reading, in which the Joule Thomson expansion corresponds to an isenthalpic process in the dual field theory. The results provide a consistent thermodynamic framework for symmergent black holes and clarify the role of the symmergent parameters in the Joule Thomson process.

</details>


### [201] [Stationary Rotating Geometries Associated with Nonsingular Pulsating Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28850)
*A. D. Souza,F. A. Brito,J. Spinelly*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Gao、Lu、Shen和Faraoni提出的静态外部几何（其内部模型通过坍缩与反弹阶段避免奇点），利用Azreg-Aïnou形式的Newman-Janis算法构建了该几何的旋转外部延拓，得到稳态轴对称旋转时空线元；其视界几何、角速度、表面引力和霍金温度在量子/膜世界修正可忽略（l→0）时退化为Kerr与Schwarzschild极限。作者进一步分析了固定转动状态空间参数下的热响应，识别Davies型奇点，并建立了扩展的热力学状态空间关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hawking-Penrose奇点定理表明，在适当假设下广义相对论中的引力坍缩会导致奇点行为。基于此类区域需要超越经典GR的物理预期，受圈量子宇宙学和膜世界场景启发的有效方法引入高密度修正，从而避免奇点。本文旨在为这类无奇点坍缩-反弹模型的静态外部几何构造旋转推广，以研究其旋转时空结构与热力学性质。

Method: 采用Azreg-Aïnou表述下的Newman-Janis算法，对Gao-Lu-Shen-Faraoni静态外部几何进行旋转延拓，构造稳态轴对称旋转时空线元；计算其视界几何、角速度、表面引力与霍金温度；分析固定转动状态空间参数下的热响应，识别Davies型奇点，并推导扩展的热力学状态空间关系。

Result: 得到描述稳态轴对称旋转时空的线元；其视界几何、角速度、表面引力与霍金温度在量子/膜世界修正可忽略（l→0）时恢复Kerr与Schwarzschild极限；在固定转动状态空间参数下识别出Davies型奇点，并建立了扩展的热力学状态空间关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为无奇点坍缩-反弹模型的静态外部几何提供了旋转延拓，所得旋转时空在修正消失时与Kerr和Schwarzschild解一致，并通过Davies型奇点分析与扩展热力学状态空间关系，揭示了此类量子/膜世界修正黑洞的热力学结构。

Abstract: Since the first exact solutions of General Relativity (GR) were obtained, it became clear that the theory predicts a new class of compact objects: black holes. The Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems show that, under appropriate assumptions, gravitational collapse in GR leads to singular behavior. Motivated by the expectation that such regimes require physics beyond classical GR, effective approaches inspired by Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) and braneworld scenarios introduce high-density corrections. In this work we consider the static exterior geometry introduced by Gao, Lu, Shen, and Faraoni, associated with a comoving interior model in which the singularity is avoided through a sequence of collapse and bounce phases. Using the Newman--Janis algorithm in the Azreg-Aïnou formulation, we construct a stationary rotating exterior extension of this geometry. The resulting line element describes a stationary axisymmetric rotating spacetime whose horizon geometry, angular velocity, surface gravity, and Hawking temperature recover the Kerr and Schwarzschild limits when the quantum/braneworld correction becomes negligible ($l\to 0$). We further analyze the thermal response at fixed rotational state-space parameter, identify Davies-type singular points, and formulate an extended thermodynamic state-space relation.

</details>


### [202] [Do gravitational waves assist the genuine tripartite entanglement harvesting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24428)
*Xiang-Yue Yu,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究三个线性排列的Unruh-DeWitt探测器在引力波背景下与无质量标量场局域耦合时的真三体纠缠收获，发现引力波对纠缠收获具有增强或抑制的双重作用，且真三体纠缠在频率和空间域上对引力波扰动更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引力波背景对量子纠缠收获的影响，特别是真三体纠缠是否可作为探测时空扰动的敏感量子探针。

Method: 采用三个线性排列的Unruh-DeWitt探测器，局域耦合于引力波背景中的无质量标量场，分析真三体纠缠的收获行为，并与双体纠缠进行对比。

Result: 引力波对纠缠收获起双重作用（增强或抑制，取决于系统参数）；真三体纠缠对引力波频率呈显著非单调响应（先抑制、后类共振增强、再抑制），高频时趋近Minkowski时空值；对探测器间距也呈非单调依赖，出现双体纠缠中不存在的局域增强峰。

Conclusion: 真三体纠缠在频率和空间域上均比双体纠缠对引力波扰动更敏感，有望成为探测引力波诱导时空扰动的敏感量子探针。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of genuine tripartite entanglement by three linearly arranged Unruh-DeWitt detectors locally coupled to a massless scalar field in a gravitational wave background. We show that gravitational waves play a dual role in entanglement harvesting, either enhancing or suppressing the harvested entanglement depending on the system parameters. In particular, genuine tripartite entanglement exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic response to the gravitational wave frequency, characterized by successive suppression, enhancement resembling resonance, and renewed suppression, while approaching its corresponding value in Minkowski spacetime in the regime of high frequencies. Its dependence on the detector separation is also nonmonotonic, displaying local enhancement peaks that are absent in the corresponding bipartite entanglement behavior. Compared with bipartite entanglement, genuine tripartite entanglement therefore exhibits a higher sensitivity to gravitational wave perturbations in both the frequency and spatial domains. These results suggest that genuine tripartite entanglement may provide a sensitive quantum probe of spacetime perturbations induced by gravitational waves.

</details>


### [203] [Parameter Estimation of Ringdown Quasinormal Modes with Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14277)
*Momoka Iida,Hayato Motohashi,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自编码器的多分量QNM分析框架，将潜空间训练为表征各个模式的物理参数，从而在同一框架内实现波形去噪与参数估计；在分段自旋区间上，对八分量输入波形中最长寿命的两个分量实现良好的域内波形重构与参数恢复，并在选定自旋区间内成功恢复32个参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞并合的铃宕引力波可建模为多种准正规模（QNM）的叠加，其频率与激发因子编码了残余Kerr黑洞的性质。然而，由于模式重叠与噪声干扰，可靠地提取多个QNM分量十分困难，因此需要一种能够在统一框架内同时完成去噪与参数估计的方法。

Method: 构建基于自编码器的多分量QNM分析框架，其潜空间被训练用于表示单个模式的物理参数，从而在同一框架内实现波形去噪与参数估计。采用受控模型波形——即由有限个Kerr QNM之和构成、并使用近期建立的高精度频率与激发因子（包括共振激发附近非平凡的自旋依赖关系）——在划分的自旋区间上评估该方法。

Result: 模型对八分量输入波形中最长寿命的两个分量取得了良好的域内波形重构与参数恢复；当验证自旋远离训练范围时性能下降。在选定的自旋区间内，该框架还能恢复八分量波形的32个参数，整体一致性良好。

Conclusion: 结果证明了针对特定多分量铃宕波形族开展物理信息自编码器推断的可行性，并为进一步使用更接近真实信号的数据进行测试提供了动力。

Abstract: Ringdown gravitational waves from binary black hole mergers can be modeled as superpositions of quasinormal modes (QNMs), whose frequencies and excitation factors encode properties of the remnant Kerr black hole. Reliable extraction of multiple QNM components is challenging because of mode overlap and noise. We develop an autoencoder-based framework for multi-component QNM analysis, in which the latent space is trained to represent the physical parameters of individual modes, enabling waveform denoising and parameter estimation within a common framework. Using controlled model waveforms constructed as finite sums of Kerr QNMs with recently established high-precision frequencies and excitation factors, including their nontrivial spin dependence near resonant excitation, we assess the method across partitioned spin intervals. The model achieves good in-domain waveform reconstruction and parameter recovery for the two longest-lived components of eight-component input waveforms, while its performance degrades when the validation spins lie far outside the training range. In a selected spin interval, the framework also recovers the 32 parameters of an eight-component waveform with good overall agreement. These results demonstrate the feasibility of physics-informed autoencoder-based inference for a prescribed multi-component ringdown waveform family and motivate further tests with progressively more realistic signals.

</details>


### [204] [How Universal Is the Black Hole Zeroth Law?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24648)
*Rajes Ghosh,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 黑洞第零定律在不同引力理论中由场方程的不同视界投影机制保证：一般相对论、f(R)与Lovelock理论依赖混合（零-横向）视界方程，而非Lovelock理论（如广义二次引力）则由零-零分量决定；据此首次在更高曲率引力中给出非微扰的精确证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞第零定律断言稳态Killing视界处于热平衡，但其在广义相对论之外的有效性认识有限。作者旨在揭示该定律的成立是否受统一机制支配，并突破以往依赖围绕广义相对论的微扰展开的局限。

Method: 通过分析不同引力理论中场方程在视界上的投影分量来判定表面重力是否为常数：识别出一般相对论、f(R)引力与Lovelock理论由混合（零-横向）视界方程保证；而在广义二次引力等非Lovelock理论中，由零-零分量场方程决定其成立。以该新判据为工具，进行非微扰的精确论证。

Result: 发现第零定律的成立机制并非普适：一般相对论、f(R)与Lovelock理论源于混合视界方程，而非Lovelock理论由零-零分量主导。在广义二次引力中，零-零分量场方程决定其有效性，并由此首次在更广泛的更高曲率引力中给出第零定律的精确证明，不依赖微扰展开。

Conclusion: 第零定律可作为探测引力底层热力学结构的敏锐探针，并提示可依据保证其成立的机制对修改引力理论进行分类。

Abstract: The black hole zeroth law asserts the thermal equilibrium of a stationary Killing horizon, yet its status beyond general relativity remains limited. We demonstrate that its validity is not governed by a universal mechanism: distinct gravitational theories enforce constancy of the surface gravity through different horizon-projections of their field equations. In general relativity, $f(R)$-gravity, and Lovelock theories, the zeroth law is known to follow from the mixed (null-transverse) horizon equations. By contrast, we show that in non-Lovelock theories the zeroth law is enforced by qualitatively different mechanisms. In generalized quadratic gravity, for instance, the null-null component of the field equations dictates its validity. Exploiting this novel diagnostic, we obtain the first exact proof of the zeroth law in the broader landscape of higher-curvature gravity, without relying on a perturbative expansion around general relativity. Our result establishes the zeroth law as a sharp probe of the underlying thermodynamic structure of gravity, and points toward a classification of modified theories according to the mechanism responsible for its validity.

</details>


### [205] [The third law of black hole thermodynamics: Quantum versus classical considerations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16127)
*Shahar Hod,Tsvi Piran*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨极端带电黑洞在有限时间内形成的可能及其与热力学第三定律(TLT)的兼容性，指出量子真空极化在$|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$区间内通过产生无质量带电粒子对使黑洞放电，从而保证TLT的量子有效性；并进一步猜想在$|eQ|<\hbar/2$区间存在更强的经典约束，阻止极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞的动力学形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kehle和Unger表明无质量带电经典标量场可在有限时间内产生极端黑洞，这与热力学第三定律(TLT)相矛盾，因为极端黑洞温度为零。由于TLT本质上是量子原理，因此需要量子层次的自治分析来检验该矛盾是否真实存在。

Method: 从量子效应出发，指出系统的电场可自发极化真空，产生无质量带电粒子对，进而使黑洞放电。结合Schneider关于小电荷区间经典束缚的分析，采用理论论证与猜想相结合的方法。

Result: 在$|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$区间，真空极化机制保证TLT在量子意义上成立；该机制在$|eQ|<\hbar/2$区间过弱，无法维持TLT。Schneider的经典束缚仅覆盖$|eQ|\leq\hbar/3$区间，并留有更强束缚存在的可能。

Conclusion: 作者猜想该经典束缚可被加强至$|eQ|<\hbar/2$，即在该区间内经典因素即可阻止极端(零温)带电Reissner-Nordström黑洞的动力学形成，从而与量子极化效应的适用范围互补。

Abstract: Kehle and Unger have recently shown that, under suitable conditions, a massless charged classical scalar field can produce an extremal black hole in finite time. This result appears to violate the third law of thermodynamics (TLT), since extremal black holes have zero temperature. However, because the TLT is intrinsically a quantum principle, a self-consistent analysis of this problem must account for quantum effects. In particular, we point out that the electric field of the system may spontaneously polarize the vacuum, thereby creating massless charged pairs that will discharge the black hole. This mechanism guarantees the {\it quantum} validity of the TLT in the regime $|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$ (here ${Q,e}$ denote, respectively, the electric charges of the black hole and the scalar field). This leaves open the question of what happens for lower values of $|eQ|/\hbar$. Recently, Schneider derived a classical bound showing that extremal black holes cannot be formed à la Kehle-Unger in the small-charge regime $|eQ|\leq\hbar/3$. His analysis leaves open the possibility that a stronger bound may exist. We conjecture here that this bound can indeed be strengthened such that classical considerations would prevent extremal (zero-temperature) charged Reissner-Nordström black holes from forming dynamically in the regime $|eQ|<\hbar/2$, where quantum polarization effects are too weak to preserve the validity of the TLT.

</details>


### [206] [Formalizing a unified dynamical system framework for $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24733)
*Saikat Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了f(R)与f(Q)引力的统一动力系统表述，采用三类无量纲变量且无需辅助变量，通过三种等效闭合方式验证并对比了两类引力在宇宙学演化上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hubble归一化框架在刻画f(R)与f(Q)引力时存在局限，且常依赖辅助变量。作者希望建立一个更普适、无辅助变量的统一动力系统形式，以便系统比较不同修正引力理论的宇宙学演化。

Method: 采用三组无量纲变量：(1)运动学宇宙学参数；(2)暗能量状态方程参数w及其N=ln a导数与物质密度丰度Ω_m；(3)描述理论函数形状的理论空间参数{m_i}。系统通过三种等效方式闭合：指定理论、指定宇宙学演化、或指定暗能量状态方程演化，并用显式例子逐一说明。

Result: 在理论闭合中，研究了传统形式失效的Hu-Sawicki f(R)与f(Q)=-2Λ+Q+β√(-Q)模型：f(Q)普遍存在从GR-物质主导时期到晚期de-Sitter时期的异宿轨道，而f(R)对此敏感于初始条件。在宇宙学闭合与状态方程闭合中，比较了运动学等效于ΛCDM(j(z)=1)和动力学等效于ΛCDM(w(z)=-1)的f(R)与f(Q)：两者中GR对f(Q)均为宇宙学过去吸引子，但对f(R)则不是。

Conclusion: 该统一动力系统表述在f(R)与f(Q)引力中有效且无需辅助变量，为比较修正引力理论的宇宙学行为提供了通用框架，并揭示f(Q)与f(R)在GR吸引子性质及轨道稳定性上的本质差异。

Abstract: Going beyond the traditional Hubble-normalized framework, we formalize a unified dynamical system formulation for $f(R)$ and coincident gauge $f(Q)$ gravity using three distinct sets of dimensionless variables: (1) kinematical cosmographic parameters, (2) the dark energy equation of state parameter $w$ (with its $N=\ln a$ derivatives) and matter density abundance $Ω_m$, and (3) theory space parameters $\{m_i\}$ characterizing the shape of the theory function. Our formulation is free of auxiliary variables, closing via three equivalent ways: (1) specifying a theory, (2) requiring a specific cosmological evolution, or (3) requiring a specific dark energy equation of state evolution.
  Each closure strategy is illustrated with explicit examples. The theory closure approach studies a case of Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ and $f(Q)=-2Λ+Q+β\sqrt{-Q}$ models, where traditional formalisms fail. A heteroclinic trajectory from a GR-matter-dominated epoch to a late-time de-Sitter epoch is general for $f(Q)$, but sensitive to initial conditions for $f(R)$. The cosmographic and equation-of-state closure approaches compare $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravities kinematically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($j(z)=1$) and dynamically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($w(z)=-1$). In both, GR acts as a cosmological past attractor for $f(Q)$ gravity, but not for $f(R)$.

</details>


### [207] [Constraining the geometry of rotating black holes with eikonal QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24824)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将校准的eikonal方法与转动黑洞度规相衔接，用贝叶斯推断比较"度规特异"（KRZ全局参数化）与"度规无关"（光环处局部度规量）两类模型，发现均匀先验在局部量上会诱导强非均匀、偏斜先验；对GR注入两模型均与Kerr一致，对偏离GR注入单参数分析失效而多参数分析虽有简并但约束一致，并应用于GW250114环衰减数据仍与Kerr一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模（QNM）是检验广义相对论与环衰减观测的核心手段，但推断黑洞性质或背景时空依赖理论输入：度规特异模型受限于全局参数化，度规无关模型则仅依赖局部量，二者先验映射关系及鲁棒性缺乏系统比较。

Method: 建立校准eikonal方法，将QNM频移与光环附近的局部度规性质联系；用贝叶斯推断对比两类模型：(1)基于全局Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko（KRZ）参数化的度规特异模型；(2)基于光环处度规函数局部值及径向导数的度规无关模型。每类模型分别做单参数与多参数同时分析，并量化先验如何映射到相同的局部量。

Result: KRZ系数上的均匀先验会在局部度规量上诱导强非均匀且偏斜的先验。对与GR一致的注入，两模型均与Kerr一致，并在轨道频率与Lyapunov指数上给出相似后验，尽管诱导先验不同。对偏离GR的注入，单参数分析无法恢复注入的局部偏离，且度规特异情形会偏置全局度规重建。两模型的多参数分析给出相一致的约束，说明具有一定鲁棒性，但引入显著简并及边缘化参数差异，显示更灵活模型的实际局限。应用于GW250114环衰减的近似后验信息时，两模型均与GR的Kerr度规一致。

Conclusion: 度规特异与度规无关方法在GR情形下相互印证并均与Kerr一致；但在偏离GR情形下，单参数分析不可靠，多参数分析虽鲁棒却存在简并与边缘化差异，说明模型灵活性带来实际限制。将该框架用于GW250114环衰减数据，结果同样支持GR的Kerr黑洞。

Abstract: Quasinormal modes of black holes provide a central avenue for confronting general relativity with gravitational-wave observations from the ringdown stage of compact binary coalescence. However, inferring black-hole properties or the background spacetime requires theory-dependent input. In this work, we connect a calibrated eikonal method, which relates quasinormal mode shifts to local metric properties near the light ring, with the underlying rotating black hole metric. Using Bayesian inference, we compare a metric-specific model based on the global Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko parametrization with a metric-agnostic approach based on local values and radial derivatives of the metric functions at the light ring. For each model, we perform both one-parameter and simultaneous multi-parameter analyses and quantify how their priors map onto the same local quantities. We find that uniform priors on the metric-specific coefficients can induce strongly nonuniform and skewed priors on these local metric quantities. For an injection compatible with general relativity, both models remain consistent with Kerr and yield similar posteriors for the orbital frequency and Lyapunov exponent despite their different induced priors. For injections representing deviations from general relativity, one-parameter analyses can fail to recover the injected local deviations and, in the metric-specific case, can bias the global metric reconstruction. The multi-parameter analyses of both models yield consistent constraints, suggesting sufficient robustness. However, they introduce substantial degeneracies and differences in marginalized parameters, demonstrating practical limitations when considering more flexible models. Finally, we apply the framework to approximate posterior information from the ringdown analysis of GW250114 and find that both models are consistent with the Kerr metric in general relativity.

</details>


### [208] [Boson Star Factory: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24913)
*Romain Gervalle,Jorge Filipe Mónico Delgado,Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho,Carlos Herdeiro,Víctor Jaramillo,Eugen Radu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对旋转玻色星与静态偶极玻色星的三种独立数值求解方法（有限差分、有限元、谱方法）进行了系统比较，验证精度并给出基准物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色星是非球形紧致天体中相对简单的对象，但其构造需求解非线性椭圆偏微分方程组，难度较高；以往各研究组各自使用独立数值实现，缺乏对同一物理模型的系统性方法比较。

Method: 针对旋转玻色星和静态偶极玻色星两种最简单的非球形构型，采用三个独立代码：基于有限差分的FIDISOL/CADSOL、基于有限元的FreeFem、基于谱方法的Kadath，并通过多种诊断量评估精度；同时比较同一硬件下的运行时间，并尝试用物理信息神经网络构造旋转Q球解。

Result: 三个求解器在全局可观测量上通常一致到约八位有效数字；给出了各代码在相同硬件上的运行时间对比，以及相关物理量的精确基准值，可作为未来玻色星数值研究的参考数据。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对同一玻色星模型下的多种数值方法进行了系统比较，确认了不同方法间的高度一致性，并提供基准数据；物理信息神经网络构造旋转Q球解进一步验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: Boson stars are self-gravitating solitons of the Einstein-Klein-Gordon equations for a massive complex scalar field, and arguably the simplest horizonless compact objects arising in General Relativity. Beyond spherical symmetry, their construction requires solving a system of nonlinear elliptic partial differential equations, a difficult task per se and that each group has traditionally addressed with its own numerical implementation. A systematic comparison of different methods applied to the same physical model has so far been lacking. In this work, we carry out such a comparison for the two simplest non-spherical configurations: rotating boson stars and static dipolar boson stars. We employ three independent codes, based respectively on finite differences (FIDISOL/CADSOL), finite elements (FreeFem), and spectral methods (Kadath), and assess their accuracy through different diagnostics. The three solvers agree on the global observables to typically eight significant digits. We further compare the runtime of each code on identical hardware and provide precise benchmark values of the relevant physical quantities, intended to serve as reference data for future numerical studies of boson stars. As a further proof of concept, we also present the use of physics-informed neural networks to construct a rotating Q-ball solution.

</details>


### [209] [Observational Features of Nonsingular Rotating Black Holes in the Dark-Energy Dominated Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24926)
*Ramon Torres*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究处于暗能量主导宇宙中的非奇异旋转黑洞,通过相空间零测地线超哈密顿量与Mino型参数计算阴影,提出以阴影面积和垂直直径为探针来约束随曲率演化的宇宙学项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI重子声学振荡结合CMB与Ia型超新星数据暗示暗能量可能随宇宙时间演化;在宇宙学背景下,演化暗能量密度可局部表达为单调曲率不变量的函数,从而启发有效的曲率相关宇宙学项。此外,弯曲时空量子场论与带跑动引力耦合的量子引力方法提供了互补动机。

Method: 在相空间中使用零测地线超哈密顿量和Mino型参数直接建立问题;从零角动量观测者出发进行反向光线追踪以计算黑洞阴影;定义并分析一套涵盖阴影大小、形状与位移的完整标准观测量。

Result: 非奇异旋转黑洞在暗能量主导宇宙中的现象学可与Kerr及Kerr–de Sitter几何有显著差异;阴影面积与垂直直径对跑动宇宙学项给出特别清晰的信号特征。

Conclusion: 联合多个强场观测量及多颗不同质量与自旋黑洞的分析,可为严格约束乃至测量宇宙学项跑动背后的有效正则化尺度、并在统计上区分非奇异黑洞几何与Kerr/Kerr-dS解提供现实可行途径。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, combined with cosmic microwave background and type-Ia supernova data, suggest that dark energy may evolve with cosmic time. Along a cosmological background, an evolving dark-energy density can be expressed locally as a function of a monotonic curvature invariant, motivating an effective curvature-dependent cosmological term. Moreover, quantum field theory in curved spacetime and approaches to quantum gravity with running gravitational couplings provide complementary motivations for exploring such a term.
  Here we study how the phenomenology of nonsingular rotating black holes embedded in a Universe dominated by dark energy can differ substantially from that of the Kerr and Kerr--de Sitter geometries. The problem is directly formulated in phase space using a null geodesic super-Hamiltonian and a Mino-type parameter. The shadows of the black holes are calculated by backward ray tracing from a zero-angular-momentum observer. We define and analyze a comprehensive set of standard shadow observables, characterizing the size, shape, and displacement of the shadow. We show that the area of the shadow and its vertical diameter provide particularly clear signatures of a running cosmological term. In this way, joint analyses of multiple strong-field observables and of several black holes with different masses and spins could provide a realistic route to tightly constraining, or potentially measuring, the effective regularization scale behind the running of the cosmological term and statistically distinguishing the non-singular black hole geometry from Kerr/Kerr-dS solutions.

</details>


### [210] [Analytical estimates of core optics stray light noise and design requirements for ground based gravitational wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25003)
*M. Andrés-Carcasona,M. Evans*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出解析框架，将探测器灵敏度转换为地面引力波干涉仪核心光学区的杂散光需求，涵盖多种散射机制、平面挡板集合及光束收集器捕获的鬼光束需求，并应用于Cosmic Explorer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完整光学布局尚未确定时，需要保守的设计需求来指导杂散光预算与早期设计，现有方法缺乏将灵敏度直接映射为杂散光需求的解析工具。

Method: 推导若干相关散射机制的解析估计，将处理扩展到平面挡板集合，并获得对光束收集器拦截的确定性鬼光束的类似需求；以Cosmic Explorer为例进行应用。

Result: 得到一组紧凑的解析表达式，可用于当前及未来引力波探测器的杂散光预算和早期设计研究，并展示了如何据此推导下一代探测器的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架可在完整光学布局可用之前提供保守的设计需求，是地面引力波干涉仪杂散光预算与早期设计的有效工具。

Abstract: We present an analytical framework to translate detector sensitivity into stray light requirements for the core optics region of ground based gravitational wave interferometers. We derive analytical estimates for several relevant scattering regimes, extend the treatment to collections of planar baffles, and obtain an analogous requirement for deterministic ghost beams intercepted by beam dumps. The framework is intended to provide conservative design requirements before a complete optical layout is available. We apply it to Cosmic Explorer to show how these estimates can be used to derive requirements for next generation detectors. The resulting expressions provide a compact set of tools for stray light budgeting and early design studies in current and future gravitational wave detectors.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

### [211] [A Note on Partially Anti-invariant Submanifolds of Kenmotsu Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22263)
*Muhammad Shuaib,Cenap Ozel,Meraj A. Khan,Yakup Yildirim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究Kenmotsu流形的部分反不变子流形，考察相关分布的 integrability，给出诱导叶状结构全测地的条件，并在结构张量 N、P 平行时探讨分布几何性质；利用曲率张量及常 ψ 截面曲率的 Kenmotsu 空间形式导出多种几何刻画，同时刻画全脐部分反不变子流形并建立不等式及等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Kenmotsu 流形框架下推广部分反不变子流形的研究，弥补此类子流形几何性质、分布可积性与全测地叶状结构方面系统刻画的不足，并为常 ψ 截面曲率的 Kenmotsu 空间形式中的子流形提供量化结果。

Method: 引入 Kenmotsu 流形的部分反不变子流形概念；考察涉及分布的可积性；推导诱导叶状结构为全测地的条件；在结构张量 N 与 P 平行时分析分布几何性质；利用 Kenmotsu 流形曲率张量与常 ψ 截面曲率的 Kenmotsu 空间形式性质进行几何刻画；刻画全脐部分反不变子流形并建立相应不等式，讨论等号成立情形。

Result: 获得了分布可积性条件与诱导叶状结构全测地的充分条件；在 N、P 平行情形下给出分布的几何性质；在常 ψ 截面曲率的 Kenmotsu 空间形式中导出多种几何特征；刻画了全脐部分反不变子流形，并建立了部分反不变子流形的不等式及等号情形。

Conclusion: 该研究系统建立了 Kenmotsu 流形中部分反不变子流形的几何理论框架，将可积性、全测地叶状结构、张量平行性与曲率刻画相结合，并为常 ψ 截面曲率情形下的全脐分类与不等式等号条件提供了完整结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and investigate partially antiinvariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu manifolds. The integrability of the involved distributions is examined, and conditions ensuring that the induced foliations are totally geodesic are obtained. We further explore the geometric properties of these distributions when the associated structure tensors N and P are parallel. By making use of the curvature tensor of Kenmotsu manifolds and the properties of Kenmotsu space forms with constant psi sectional curvature, we derive various geometric characterizations of partially anti-invariant submanifolds. In addition, we characterize totally umbilical partially anti-invariant submanifolds and establish an inequality for partially anti invariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu space forms, including the characterization of the equality case.

</details>


### [212] [Elimination of the parameters in an elliptic parametrization of the Schwarz primitive surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22313)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文围绕Schwarz原始曲面P（D的共轭）的基本拼块参数化展开，分三部分：建立代数工具并精确确定归一化常数ϖ，完成消元并证明sn函数恒等式(★)，并说明该恒等式具有加法可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与companion paper [D]（关于钻石曲面D）从同一起点出发，仅取实部方式不同；需为Schwarz原始曲面P的基本拼块给出显式参数化，并借助代数与椭圆函数工具证明关键恒等式。

Method: 在四段圆弧围成的曲边方形Ω上定义W、θ(ζ)及f、g、𝔥三个函数；取X=(κ Re f, κ Re g, 1/2+κ Im 𝔥)，κ=3/(2K[1/9])，用以参数化P的基本拼块；Part I构建代数工具并确定归一化常数；Part II执行消元；Part III分析(★)的加法可分离性。

Result: 归一化常数被精确识别为 ϖ:=κ^{-1}=K[-3]=½K[3/4]=⅔K[1/9]；Part II完成消元并证明恒等式 (★)：sn(ϖ(x+y),-3)·sn(ϖ(x-y),-3)=⅓sn(3ϖ(z-1/2),1/9)；Part III表明(★)是加法可分离的。

Conclusion: 论文完成了Schwarz原始曲面P基本拼块参数化的代数框架、消元证明及恒等式(★)的加法可分离性分析；与[D]共享同一出发点，概述见arXiv:2609.14206。

Abstract: Let $Ω$ be the curvilinear square bounded by the four circular arcs $|ζ\mp1|=\sqrt{2}$, $|ζ\mp i|=\sqrt{2}$, and let $W=\sqrt{1+14ζ^{4}+ζ^{8}}$. Put \[
  θ(ζ)=\arcsin\frac{2(1+i)ζ}
  {\sqrt{1+4iζ^{2}-ζ^{4}}}, \] \[
  f=\tfrac14\bigl(-iF[θ,\tfrac14]+F[θ,\tfrac34]\bigr),\quad \quad
  g=\tfrac14\bigl(\ \ iF[θ,\tfrac14]+F[θ,\tfrac34]\bigr), \] \[
  \mathfrak h=(2-\sqrt3)\,
  F\bigl[\arcsin\bigl(i(2+\sqrt3)ζ^{2}\bigr),(2-\sqrt3)^{4}\bigr]. \] These are, verbatim, the three functions of our companion paper $\mathrm{[D]}$ on the diamond surface $\mathrm{D}$: the two papers start from the same point, and differ only in which real parts are taken. With $X=(κ\,\operatorname{Re}f,\ κ\,\operatorname{Re}g,\ \tfrac12+κ\,\operatorname{Im}\mathfrak{h})$ and $κ=3/(2K[1/9])$, the map $X$ parametrizes a fundamental patch of Schwarz's primitive surface $\mathrm{P}$, the conjugate of $\mathrm{D}$.
  Part I builds the algebraic apparatus $\ldots$ the normalizing constant is identified exactly: $\varpi:=κ^{-1}=K[-3]=\tfrac12 K[3/4]=\tfrac23 K[1/9]$. Part II carries out the elimination and completes the proof of \[
  (\star)\qquad
  \operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{+}y),-3\bigr)\,\operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{-}y),-3\bigr)
  =\tfrac13\operatorname{sn}\bigl(3\varpi(z-\tfrac12),\tfrac19\bigr). \] Part III shows that $(\star)$ is, after all, additively separable $\ldots$ for an overview of this paper and $\mathrm{[D]}$, please see arXiv:2609.14206.

</details>


### [213] [The suboptimal Penrose inequality for charged initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22418)
*Eunice Ng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在渐进平坦带电初始数据集上建立了涉及质量、面积与电荷的Penrose型不等式，并推广至双曲型及轴对称含角动量情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri最近获得的Penrose型不等式从无电荷情形推广到带电（Einstein-Maxwell）情形。

Method: 针对三维、渐进平坦、无磁场的Einstein-Maxwell方程完整初始数据集进行几何分析，构造并证明包含面积$\mathcal{A}$、电荷$\mathcal{Q}$的定量不等式；对渐进双曲型初始数据集以双曲能量替代ADM质量进行类似论证；在轴对称附加假设下进一步纳入角动量。

Result: 存在小通用常数$\mathcal{C}$，使得$m\geq\mathcal{C}(\sqrt{\mathcal{A}/(16\pi)}+\mathcal{Q}^2\sqrt{\pi/\mathcal{A}})$；双曲型情形有对应结论；轴对称时不等式可加强以包含角动量。

Conclusion: 带电荷的Penrose型不等式成立，涵盖渐近平坦、渐进双曲及轴对称含角动量三种情形，推广了已有的无电荷结果。

Abstract: Given a complete, 3-dimensional, asymptotically flat initial data set for the Einstein-Maxwell equations with vanishing magnetic field, we show that there exists a small universal constant $\mathcal{C}$ such that $m\geq\mathcal{C}(\sqrt{\mathcal{A}/(16π)}+\mathcal{Q}^2\sqrt{π/\mathcal{A}}\,)$. An analogous statement holds for asymptotically hyperboloidal initial data sets with the ADM mass replaced by hyperbolic energy. Under the additional assumption of axisymmetry, the inequality may be further strengthened to include angular momentum. These results extend the Penrose-type inequalities recently obtained by Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri to the charged setting.

</details>


### [214] [The sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds in codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22447)
*Raphael Tsiamis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为欧氏空间中一大类varifold（含任意维数与余维数、具局部有界一阶变差的整varifold）证明了Michael-Simon不等式；在余维2情形下该不等式是sharp的，并蕴含varifold的sharp等周不等式，等号仅在圆盘上成立；证明采用Brendle-Eichmair的最优传输方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Michael-Simon不等式原先主要针对子流形建立，而varifold作为更一般的广义曲面类（允许奇异、不可定向等），需要相应版本的不等式。作者希望把该不等式推广到尽可能一般的varifold类，并在余维2情形下获得最优（sharp）形式及等周推论。

Method: 采用Brendle-Eichmair的最优传输（optimal transportation）方法，对欧氏空间中具局部有界一阶变差、任意维数与余维数的整varifold建立Michael-Simon不等式。

Result: 在所考虑的varifold类上证明了Michael-Simon不等式；在余维2情形下该不等式是sharp的；由它可推出varifold的sharp等周不等式，且等号成立当且仅当对象为圆盘（round disk）。

Conclusion: 该工作将Michael-Simon型不等式推广至一般整varifold，并在余维2情形达到最优，同时给出sharp等周不等式及等号刻画，表明最优传输方法是处理此类几何不等式在varifold框架下的有效工具。

Abstract: We prove a Michael-Simon inequality for a class of varifolds in Euclidean space that includes all integral varifolds with locally bounded first variation of arbitrary dimension and codimension. In codimension $2$, this inequality is sharp and implies the sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds, which attains equality precisely for the round disk. Our proof uses the optimal transportation approach of Brendle-Eichmair.

</details>


### [215] [A Differential-Geometric Framework for Risk-Optimal Asset Reallocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22459)
*Georgios Leventidis,Evangelos Melas*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 论文将投资组合再平衡建模为概率单纯形上的黎曼几何问题，用测地线刻画风险最优调仓路径，并证明引入位置相关风险（如拥挤度）后测地线可弱支配直线与投影梯度路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统再平衡通常沿直线路径调整权重，忽略了调仓过程本身的累积风险，尤其是与持仓位置相关的集中度或拥挤风险。论文旨在为调仓管理提供统一的风险几何框架，寻找风险最小的过渡路径。

Method: 将多头组合表示为概率单纯形上的点，赋予一个融合市场协方差风险与位置相关集中度风险的正定黎曼度量；将调仓轨迹的累积风险定义为黎曼长度，从当前配置到Markowitz目标的最低调仓路径即为测地线；与线性直接再平衡及投影梯度上升进行对比；引入Fisher-Rao集中度项与内生的拥挤度量，并进行数值实验、蒙特卡洛转移与回归分析。

Result: 当仅对市场协方差风险定价时，度量为常数且平坦，测地线恰为直线路径；引入位置相关风险后几何变弯曲，测地线在相同端点下弱支配竞争路径。Fisher-Rao集中度项带来温和但系统性的风险节省；内生拥挤度量产生非凸风险脊，测地线可通过临时分散化绕开。实验表明，收益最大存在于直接路径穿越强拥挤区域的情形。

Conclusion: 该框架给出了调仓管理的一般几何表述，统一了线性路径与梯度路径等特例，并可扩展至更丰富的风险度量与交易成本结构，为风险最优再平衡提供理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a differential-geometric framework for risk-optimal portfolio reallocation. A long-only portfolio is represented as a point of the probability simplex, endowed with a positive-definite Riemannian metric combining market covariance risk with position-dependent concentration risk. The cumulative risk of a rebalancing trajectory is identified with its Riemannian length, so the least-risk transition between a current allocation and a Markowitz target is a geodesic. We compare this route with direct linear rebalancing and projected gradient ascent. When only market covariance risk is priced, the metric is constant and flat, and the geodesic is exactly the straight-line path. Once position-dependent risk is introduced, the geometry becomes curved and the geodesic weakly dominates competing paths with the same endpoints. A Fisher-Rao concentration term produces modest but systematic savings, while an endogenous crowding metric creates non-convex risk ridges that geodesics can bypass through temporary diversification. Numerical experiments, Monte Carlo transitions, and regression analysis show that the largest gains occur when the direct path crosses strongly crowded regions. The framework provides a general geometric formulation of transition management and can accommodate richer risk metrics and transaction-cost structures.

</details>


### [216] [Chain-level decoupling of the physical heterotic $G_2$ deformation complex at the standard embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22565)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在异质弦 $G_2$ 紧化的物理形变复形中，作者在极小标准嵌入处给出了一阶系数复形的链级显式解耦，证明其上同调在所有次数上分解为瞬子与几何两部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质弦 $G_2$ 紧化的物理形变复形中，瞬子形变与几何场及其一阶 $\alpha'$ 修正相互耦合，此前缺少在全次数上显式去除内禀规范与几何耦合的分解结果。

Method: 在极小标准嵌入（规范丛为切丛、规范联络为 Levi-Civita 联络、背景 $G_2$ 结构无挠）下，对一阶系数复形作链级解耦：通过一个斜协变导数（延拓诱导的联络变分）使第一 Atiyah 耦合零伦；再用余微分构造第二个同伦，消去丛扇区到几何扇区的耦合；残余几何曲率项与第一变换的贡献相消。

Result: 上述恒等式在任意链元与每个典范次数上成立，得到互为逆的微分算子链映射；因此上同调在所有次数上分解为瞬子系数上同调与几何系数上同调。论证仅需 $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在全次数上显式去除该物理约化标准嵌入系数复形中内禀规范耦合与几何耦合的分解，提供了该形变复形的链级解耦与上同调分裂。

Abstract: The physical deformation complex of heterotic $G_2$ compactifications couples instanton deformations to geometric fields and their first-order $α'$ corrections. We give an explicit chain-level decoupling of its first-order coefficient complex at the minimal standard embedding, where the gauge bundle is the tangent bundle, the gauge connection is the Levi--Civita connection, and the background $G_2$ structure is torsion-free. The first Atiyah coupling is null-homotopic through a skew covariant derivative extending the induced connection variation. A second homotopy, given by the covariant codifferential of the skew part of an endomorphism-valued cochain, removes the coupling from the bundle sector to the geometric sector. The residual geometric curvature term cancels against the contribution of the first transformation. These identities hold on arbitrary cochains in every canonical degree and yield mutually inverse differential-operator chain maps. Consequently, the cohomology decomposes into instanton and geometric coefficient cohomologies in all degrees. The argument requires only $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$. To our knowledge, no previous explicit all-degree splitting removes the internal gauge and geometric couplings of this physically reduced standard-embedding coefficient complex.

</details>


### [217] [Elastic BB knots are multifold circles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22575)
*Philipp Reiter,Heiko von der Mosel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在广义Fáry-Milnor不等式假设下，证明单分量BB纽结类（桥指标与辫指标相等）中的弹性纽结必为圆环，且覆盖次数等于该指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由单位环的弯曲能量与ropelength的小倍数之和极小化所得的弹性纽结，试图对特定纽结类中的极小化子进行分类。

Method: 假设纽结类C^1边界上成立广义Fáry-Milnor不等式；避免对非光滑ropelength泛函求导；将极小闭辫在实心环面的纤维中收缩，构造具有二次弯曲能量余量和线性阶厚度的比较曲线族，得到正则化极小化子的厚度下界；利用中间尺度变分获得极限曲线对归一化弯曲能量的自由驻定性；由常曲率与自由驻定性将分类归结为线性分布型常微分方程。

Result: 在所述假设下，单分量BB纽结类中的每个弹性纽结都是恰好覆盖bridge index次的圆环。

Conclusion: 该工作通过比较曲线与尺度变分，绕开ropelength泛函的非光滑性，给出了BB纽结类中弹性纽结的完整分类。

Abstract: We study elastic knots obtained by minimizing the bending energy of a unit loop together with a small multiple of ropelength. We assume a generalized Fáry-Milnor inequality on the $C^1$-boundary of a knot class: every $H^2$-limit curve in the $C^1$-closure has total curvature at least $2π$ times the bridge index. Under this assumption we prove that every elastic knot in a one-component BB knot class, that is, a class whose bridge and braid indices coincide, is the round circle covered precisely that many times.
  The proof does not differentiate the nonsmooth ropelength functional. A minimal closed braid is contracted in the fibres of a solid torus, producing a family of comparison curves with quadratic bending-energy excess and thickness of linear order. This yields a lower thickness bound for the regularized minimizers. Variations on an intermediate scale then give free stationarity of the limiting curve for the normalized bending energy. Constant curvature and free stationarity reduce the classification to a linear distributional ordinary differential equation.

</details>


### [218] [Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions on $\mathbb{CP}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22659)
*Yusen Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 的 $1$-凸域上，首次 Dirichlet 特征函数满足定量对数凹性估计 $\nabla^2(-\log u)>\max\{\psi(s),8/5\}g$，故严格对数凹；由此得到水平集的凸性估计及谱隙下界 $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>46/5$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复射影空间 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 中域上首次 Dirichlet 特征函数的对数凹性，推广实空间形式的相应结果并寻求定量刻画。

Method: 对 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 中光滑 $1$-凸域 $\Omega$，利用 Bochner 型恒等式与曲率分析，对首次 Dirichlet 特征函数 $u$ 的 Hessian 矩阵 $\nabla^2(-\log u)$ 作下界估计，结合 $\psi(|\nabla f|^2)=\frac{s}{\sqrt{1+s}}-\log(1+s)$ 给出统一估计。

Result: 得到定量估计 $\nabla^2(-\log u)>\max\{\psi(s),8/5\}g$，由此推出 $u$ 严格对数凹、正则水平集的一致凸性估计，以及基本间隙界 $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>46/5$。

Conclusion: 该结果为复射影空间中特征函数的对数凹性及谱间隙提供了统一的定量框架，可用于研究水平集几何与谱性质。

Abstract: We study the log-concavity property of first Dirichlet eigenfunctions on domains in $\mathbb{CP}^2$. For every smooth $1$-convex domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{CP}^2$, we prove the quantitative estimate \[ \nabla^2(-\log u) > \max\left\{ψ(s),\frac85\right\}g, \text{ where } ψ(|\grad f|^2) = \frac{s}{\sqrt{1+s}}-\log(1+s), \] for its first Dirichlet eigenfunction $u$. In particular, $u$ is strictly log-concave.
  As consequences, we obtain a uniform convexity estimate for the regular level sets of $u$ and the fundamental gap bound $λ_2-λ_1>46/5$.

</details>


### [219] [Pólya--Szegö Inequality on Submanifolds of Riemannian Manifolds with Nonnegative Curvature and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22676)
*Anh Xuan Do,Nguyen Lam,Guozhen Lu,Raoní Ponciano*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在完备非紧、非负截面曲率黎曼流形的子流形上证明了Pólya–Szegö不等式，并由此导出多种Sobolev型不等式及临界情形结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在子流形上建立与欧氏空间类似的对称化（重排）不等式，并刻画几何量（平均曲率、等周量）对不等式常数的影响。

Method: 对定义于子流形Σ上的函数进行Schwarz重排（在R^n上），证明Pólya–Szegö不等式；常数依赖于Σ平均曲率的L^n范数以及由Brendle得到的等周量。

Result: 在小的总平均曲率假设下，导出任意余维子流形上的Sobolev、Log-Sobolev、Hardy与Gagliardo–Nirenberg不等式；在临界Sobolev情形，得到有限体积子流形上的Moser–Trudinger不等式和无限体积子流形上的精确增长不等式。

Conclusion: 在合适假设下，Pólya–Szegö常数等于1，此时各不等式中的临界常数与欧氏空间的最优常数一致。

Abstract: We prove a Pólya--Szegö inequality for functions defined on an $n$-dimensional submanifold $Σ$ of a complete noncompact Riemannian manifold with nonnegative sectional curvature. The associated rearrangement is a Schwarz rearrangement on $\mathbb R^n$, and the constant depends on the $L^n$-norm of the mean curvature of $Σ$ and an isoperimetric quantity obtained by Brendle. As applications, we derive Sobolev, Log-Sobolev, Hardy, and Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequalities on submanifolds of arbitrary codimension under a small total mean curvature assumption. In the critical Sobolev case, we obtain Moser--Trudinger inequalities on finite-volume submanifolds and exact growth inequalities on submanifolds with infinite volume. Under suitable assumptions, the Pólya--Szegö constant equals one; in this case, the critical constants in the inequalities coincide with the sharp Euclidean ones.

</details>


### [220] [Projectively induced Kähler--Einstein surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22832)
*Andrea Loi,Mirel Caibăr*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对可由全纯等距浸入复射影空间实现的紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面进行分类，证明仅有两类：Veronese嵌入的(PP^2, m g_FS)与Segre–Veronese嵌入的(PP^1×PP^1, m(g_FS⊕g_FS))，进而推出此类曲面必为齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的相关工作通常依赖对称性假设或对余维数加以限制。本文旨在去掉这两个限制，在无对称性假设、余维数不受限的一般情形下完成分类，并揭示这类Kähler–Einstein曲面的刚性（齐性）。

Method: 核心新工具是对Fano指数为1的分支进行与余维数无关的排除：结合公共反典范根构造（anticanonical root construction）与Gram–Gauss秩估计，并在次数为5的情形使用等变曲率论证。

Result: 证明此类曲面只有 (PP^2, m g_FS) 与 (PP^1×PP^1, m(g_FS⊕g_FS))（m∈Z_{>0}），分别由Veronese嵌入与Segre–Veronese嵌入实现。作为推论，凡度量由全纯等距浸入有限维复射影空间诱导的连通紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面均为齐性的。

Conclusion: 在无对称性假设、余维数无界的前提下，诱导型紧致Kähler–Einstein曲面被完全分类，只有射影平面与 quadric 曲面两族并具齐性；其中排除Fano指数为1分支的技术对更一般的分类问题可能具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: We classify compact Kähler--Einstein surfaces whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space. No symmetry assumption and no bound on the codimension are imposed. We prove that the only such surfaces are \[ (\PP^2,m g_{\FS}) \quad\text{and}\quad \bigl(\PP^1\times\PP^1,m(g_{\FS}\oplus g_{\FS})\bigr), \qquad m\in\mathbb Z_{>0}, \] realized respectively by the Veronese and Segre--Veronese embeddings.
  The main new ingredient is a codimension-independent exclusion of the entire Fano-index-one branch, combining a common anticanonical root construction with Gram--Gauss rank estimates and, in degree five, an equivariant curvature argument. Consequently, every connected compact Kähler--Einstein surface whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space is homogeneous.

</details>


### [221] [Free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^m$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23172)
*Roberto De Leo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明临界维数 $q_m=m(m+3)/2$ 下环面 $\mathbb{T}^m$ 到 $\mathbb{R}^{q_m}$ 的自由映射存在：通过乘积构造并利用四元数乘法显式构造 cross-free 映射，结合低维情形归纳得到 $Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset$（$m\ge1$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由映射的临界维数为 $q_m=m(m+3)/2$，此前已有低维（$m\le5$）的显式构造，但缺乏一般维数的存在性结果。作者希望证明在所有 $m\ge1$ 时临界维数下环面上的自由映射均存在。

Method: 核心工具是乘积构造：若在 $\mathbb{T}^a$ 与 $\mathbb{T}^b$ 上各有临界维数的自由映射，并存在 $\mathbb{T}^a\times\mathbb{T}^b$ 上的 cross-free 映射（其混合 Hessian 处处可逆），则可得到 $\mathbb{T}^{a+b}$ 临界维数的自由映射，其 osculating 矩阵呈块三角结构。cross-free 映射对各变量可加；当某一因子为一维时环面情形下不存在此类映射。利用四元数乘法显式构造 $\mathbb{T}^2\times\mathbb{T}^2$ 与 $\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4$ 上的 cross-free 映射。

Result: 显式构造出 $\mathbb{T}^2\times\mathbb{T}^2$ 和 $\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4$ 上的 cross-free 映射；结合作者此前低维（$m\le5$）的显式构造，通过归纳证明了对每个 $m\ge1$，$Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset$。

Conclusion: 在临界维数 $q_m=m(m+3)/2$ 下，环面 $\mathbb{T}^m$ 上自由映射对所有 $m\ge1$ 均存在。

Abstract: The critical dimension for free maps on m-manifolds is $q_m=m(m+3)/2$. We show that $Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset$ for every $m\ge 1$. The main tool is a product construction: given free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^a$ and on $\mathbb{T}^b$, together with a cross-free map on $\mathbb{T}^a\times \mathbb{T}^b$, whose mixed Hessian is invertible everywhere, we obtain a free map in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^{a+b}$, the osculating matrix of the product being block-triangular. Cross-free maps are additive in each argument, and in the torus setting none exists when one factor is one-dimensional. We construct explicit cross-free maps on $\mathbb{T}^2\times \mathbb{T}^2$ and on $\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4$ using quaternionic multiplication. Combined with the low-dimensional cases $m\leq 5$, constructed explicitly by the author in a previous publication, these yield the result by induction.

</details>


### [222] [Spheres under a Cauchy-Riemann inequality in $E(κ,τ)$, and non-CMC Hopf tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23227)
*Hilário Alencar,Harold Rosenberg*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在$E(\kappa,\tau)$（$\tau\neq0$）中浸入的拓扑球面上，对Abresch–Rosenberg微分$\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$施加单向Cauchy–Riemann型不等式即迫使平均曲率$H$为常数；该拓扑假设是尖锐的，且$\det S<0$的闭曲面必为环面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: companion paper仅用$\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$的全纯性刻画CMC浸入，而本文旨在说明更弱的单向CR型不等式已足以强制$H$为常数，并检验拓扑假设的必要性与尖锐性。

Method: 结合Bers–Vekua相似性原理与线场的Poincaré–Hopf指标公式，给出对所有$E(\kappa,\tau)$（含圆球面）一致成立的证明，逻辑上独立于代数论证；并用Hopf型淹没$\pi:E(\kappa,\tau)\to M^2(\kappa)$构造反例。

Result: 在拓扑为球面的情形下，$\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$的单向CR型不等式（弱于全纯性）即蕴含$H$为常数；拓扑假设尖锐：每个Berger球面（含圆球面）上都存在满足同一不等式且具有常数界的紧致非CMC环面，即基中非恒定测地曲率简单闭曲线在$\pi$下的原像；此外，任何$\det S<0$处处成立的闭曲面（如每个Hopf管，$\det S\equiv-\tau^2$）必为环面。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$的单向Cauchy–Riemann型不等式足以在球面拓扑下强制常平均曲率，且该拓扑条件不可去除；同时$\det S<0$处处成立的闭曲面必为环面。

Abstract: For a smooth, connected, oriented surface $Σ$, topologically a sphere, immersed in the homogeneous space $E(κ,τ)$ with $τ\ne0$, we show that a one-sided Cauchy--Riemann-type inequality on the original Abresch--Rosenberg differential $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ -- much weaker than requiring $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ to be holomorphic -- already forces the mean curvature $H$ to be constant. The proof is uniform across every $E(κ,τ)$, including the round sphere, and combines the Bers--Vekua similarity principle with the Poincaré-Hopf index formula for line fields; it is logically independent of the algebraic argument used, in the companion paper~\cite{AlencarRosenberg2026}, to characterize CMC immersions by holomorphy of $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ alone. We also show that the topological hypothesis is sharp: on every Berger sphere, including the round one, there exist compact non-CMC tori -- the preimages under the Hopf-type submersion $π:E(κ,τ)\to M^2(κ)$ of simple closed curves in the base with nonconstant geodesic curvature -- satisfying the same inequality with a constant bound. As an elementary consequence of the classical Poincaré-Hopf theorem for line fields, we record that every closed surface on which $\det S<0$ everywhere -- a condition satisfied, in particular, by every Hopf tube, where $\det S\equiv-τ^2$ -- is a torus.

</details>


### [223] [Ricci-pinched 3-Manifolds with second-fundamental-form-pinched Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23261)
*Chao Xia,Jincong Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 建立了超二次体积增长条件下Ricci夹断三维流形的边界刚性定理：具凸边界、夹断Ricci张量与夹断第二基本形式的完备连通三维流形等距于欧氏半空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Ricci夹断三维流形刚性定理推广到带边界的设定，在超二次体积增长假设下研究凸边界流形的边界刚性。

Method: 采用位势论方法：构造并研究混合Dirichlet–Neumann p-容量位势，建立其自由边界水平集的单调性公式，并使用适配该情形的弱Gauss–Bonnet公式。

Result: 证明满足凸边界、夹断Ricci张量及夹断第二基本形式条件的完备连通三维流形等距于欧氏半空间。

Conclusion: 在超二次体积增长下，Ricci夹断三维流形的边界刚性成立，等距类被完全确定。

Abstract: We prove a boundary analogue of the rigidity theorem for Ricci-pinched three-manifolds under a superquadratic volume-growth assumption. More precisely, we show that a complete connected three-manifold with convex boundary, pinched Ricci tensor, and pinched second fundamental form is isometric to the Euclidean half-space. The proof is potential-theoretic. We construct and study mixed Dirichlet--Neumann $p$-capacitary potentials, establish monotonicity formulas for their free-boundary level sets, and use a weak Gauss--Bonnet formula adapted to this setting.

</details>


### [224] [Congruence theorems for capillary hypersurfaces and a substatic Heintze--Karcher inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23304)
*Felippe Guimarães*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了欧氏空间中支撑于超平面或球面上的毛细超曲面的刚性定理，并在substatic流形上建立了Heintze–Karcher型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Ros关于闭超曲面的经典刚性结果推广到带边界的毛细超曲面情形，并发展substatic流形中的相关几何不等式。

Method: 利用毛细边界条件的几何分析工具，证明支撑于超平面或球面上的毛细超曲面的同余定理；采用Heintze–Karcher型比较方法处理substatic流形。

Result: 获得毛细超曲面的同余（刚性）定理，并建立substatic流形中毛细超曲面的Heintze–Karcher型不等式。

Conclusion: 结果推广了Ros的经典定理，为毛细超曲面的刚性及substatic流形中的体积比较提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove congruence theorems for capillary hypersurfaces of Euclidean space supported on a hyperplane or on a sphere, extending a result of Ros for closed hypersurfaces. We also establish a Heintze--Karcher type inequality for capillary hypersurfaces in substatic manifolds.

</details>


### [225] [On Positively Curved Einstein Four-Manifolds with Euler Characteristic at Most Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23337)
*Liang Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明在四维流形同胚于 $S^4$ 或 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 时，正截面曲率完备可定向爱因斯坦流形必等距于标准球面或Fubini-Study复射影平面；并给出存在非平凡Killing场的应用推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于四维正截面曲率爱因斯坦流形的刚性猜想（应为round $S^4$或Fubini-Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$）长期未决；本文旨在部分情形下确认该猜想。

Method: 利用Euler示性数条件 $\chi(M)\le 3$ 结合Freedman分类定理将拓扑限制为同胚于 $S^4$ 或 $\mathbb{CP}^2$，在此拓扑前提下对完备可定向正截面曲率爱因斯坦四维流形进行刚性分析。

Result: 证明了完备、可定向、严格正截面曲率且 $\chi(M)\le 3$（即同胚于 $S^4$ 或 $\mathbb{CP}^2$）的爱因斯坦四维流形等距于round $S^4$ 或Fubini-Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$；并由此导出若此类流形还容许非平凡Killing场，则同样等距于这两个标准模型之一。

Conclusion: 在同胚于 $S^4$ 或 $\mathbb{CP}^2$ 的拓扑条件下（含存在非平凡Killing场的情形），四维正截面曲率爱因斯坦流形的刚性猜想成立。

Abstract: It is conjectured that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold with strictly positive sectional curvature must be isometric to either the round four-sphere $S^4$ or the complex projective plane $\mathbb{CP}^2$ with the Fubini--Study metric. In this paper, we confirm this conjecture when the manifold is homeomorphic to $S^4$ or $\mathbb{CP}^2$. More precisely, we show that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold $M$ with strictly positive sectional curvature and Euler characteristic $χ(M) \le 3$ (equivalently, by Freedman's classification theorem, homeomorphic to $S^4$ or $\mathbb{CP}^2$) is isometric to either the round $S^4$ or the Fubini--Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$. As an application, we obtain that a complete, orientable Einstein four-manifold with strictly positive sectional curvature admitting a nontrivial Killing field is isometric to either the round $S^4$ or the Fubini--Study $\mathbb{CP}^2$.

</details>


### [226] [Nonclosure of stationary Yang-Mills connections under weak convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23338)
*Xiaoli Han,Jiayu Li,Yang Wen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了SO(15)主丛B^5×R^15上一列稳定弱Yang-Mills联络，其弱极限为非稳定弱Yang-Mills联络，从而证明带孤立奇点的稳定弱Yang-Mills联络在弱收敛下不封闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Tian关于Yang-Mills紧性的工作中所提出稳定性问题的启发，研究稳定弱Yang-Mills联络在弱收敛下是否封闭。

Method: 在平凡向量丛B^5×R^15上，以结构群SO(15)构造一列具有孤立奇点的稳定弱Yang-Mills联络，并分析其弱极限。

Result: 该序列的弱极限是非稳定的弱Yang-Mills联络，即稳定弱Yang-Mills联络的弱极限可以不再稳定。

Conclusion: 结果确立了带孤立奇点的稳定弱Yang-Mills联络的弱封闭性失效，并给出了Ding-Li-Li关于调和映射所示现象的Yang-Mills类比。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of stationary weak Yang-Mills connections on the trivial vector bundle $B^5\times\mathbb{R}^{15}$ with structure group $\operatorname{SO}(15)$ whose weak limit is a nonstationary weak Yang-Mills connection. Motivated by the stationarity question arising in Tian's work on Yang-Mills compactness, our result establishes the failure of weak closure for stationary weak Yang-Mills connections with an isolated singularity. It also provides a Yang-Mills analogue of the phenomenon exhibited by Ding, Li and Li for harmonic maps.

</details>


### [227] [Free Immersions in Critical Dimension from Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24777)
*Minh-Tâm Quang Trinh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了$P^3 \times S^1$与$S^3 \times S^1$到14维空间的自由浸入，这是临界维数下首批非球面、非射影空间、非环面、非曲面的闭流形自由浸入例子。方法是将De Leo关于$m$-环面的构造（$m\leq5$）中的对称群$(m-1)$-环面替换为$\mathrm{SO}(3)$，并保持密切行列式为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前临界维数下已知的自由浸入例子仅限于球面、射影空间、环面和曲面。作者希望突破这一限制，找到新的闭流形自由浸入实例，特别是包含$S^1$因子的乘积流形，从而扩展对临界维数浸入问题的理解。

Method: 模仿De Leo对$m\leq5$的$m$-环面的构造：De Leo构造的映射关于$(m-1)$-环面李群的某个表示对称。作者将该对称群替换为$\mathrm{SO}(3)$，在保持相应构造框架的基础上构造$P^3\times S^1$与$S^3\times S^1$到14维空间的自由浸入，并验证其密切行列式为常数。

Result: 成功构造出$P^3 \times S^1$和$S^3 \times S^1$到14维空间的自由浸入，给出了临界维数下不属于球面、射影空间、环面或曲面的闭流形自由浸入的首批例子，且这些例子中密切行列式为常数。

Conclusion: 通过用$\mathrm{SO}(3)$替代De Leo构造中的环面对称群，可以推广自由浸入的构造，获得临界维数下新型闭流形的自由浸入。这表明临界维数自由浸入的存在范围比先前认为的更广。

Abstract: We construct free immersions of $P^3 \times S^1$ and $S^3 \times S^1$ into $14$-space. These are the first examples of free immersions in critical dimension of closed manifolds that are not spheres, projective spaces, tori, or surfaces. The idea is to mimic De Leo's construction for $m$-tori with $m \leq 5$. De Leo constructs a map that is symmetric with respect to a representation of the Lie group formed by the $(m - 1)$-torus; we replace this group with $\mathrm{SO}(3)$. In our examples, the osculating determinant is constant.

</details>


### [228] [A Generalization of Molino's Theory to Riemannian Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23341)
*Lily Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将经典 Molino 理论从紧流形上的 Riemannian foliation 推广到具有紧连通对象流形的 regular Riemannian groupoid，给出其基本结构描述，并厘清 basic Lie algebroid 的作用及与正规表示的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Molino 理论刻画了紧流形上 Riemannian foliation 的结构，但尚缺乏在带对称性的 groupoid 框架下的对应理论。作者希望将这一结构理论推广到 regular Riemannian groupoid，以统一描述 foliation 及其对称性。

Method: 观察到 regular Riemannian groupoid 的轨道叶状结构给出对象流形上的 Riemannian foliation；证明 Riemannian groupoid 的正规表示可自然延拓为对轨道叶状结构 Molino 结构的作 用；并利用 basic Lie algebroid 与同伦意义下伴随表示的 degree-one 上同调表示进行刻画，同时在 Riemannian Morita equivalence 下建立比较结果。

Result: 主要结果表明正规表示可自然作用于 Molino 结构，从而得到 regular Riemannian groupoid 的基本结构描述；阐明了 basic Lie algebroid 在 Molino 理论中的核心作用；将正规表示等同于同伦意义下伴随表示的 degree-one 上同调表示；并给出 Riemannian Morita equivalence 下的比较结果。

Conclusion: 对于 regular Riemannian groupoid，作者给出充分条件，使得相关 basic Lie algebroid 在拉回到公共 refinement 后同构，从而完成 Molino 理论在 groupoid 层面的推广。

Abstract: Riemannian groupoids describe Riemannian foliations together with their symmetries. In this article, we extend classical Molino's theory, which concerns the structure of Riemannian foliations on compact manifolds, to the setting of regular Riemannian groupoids with compact connected object manifolds.
  We observe that the orbit foliation associated with a regular Riemannian groupoid defines a Riemannian foliation on the object manifold. The main result shows that the normal representation of a Riemannian groupoid extends naturally to an action on Molino's structures of the orbit foliation, thereby yielding the fundamental structural description of a regular Riemannian groupoid.
  In addition to the main result, we clarify the essential role played by basic Lie algebroids in Molino's theory and identify the normal representation with the degree-one cohomology representation of the adjoint representation up to homotopy. We also establish comparison results under Riemannian Morita equivalence. For regular Riemannian groupoids, we give sufficient conditions for the associated basic Lie algebroids to become isomorphic after pullback to a common refinement.

</details>


### [229] [Liouville theorem for quasi-harmonic sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23359)
*Jiayu Li,Linlin Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 紧黎曼流形若其万有覆盖上存在（无全局增长条件的）凸函数，则该流形上不存在拟调和球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨流形几何性质（凸函数的存在性）对调和映射类对象存在性的限制，弱化以往所需的全局增长条件。

Method: 利用万有覆盖上的凸函数作为几何/分析工具，通过构造论证排除拟调和球的存在性。

Result: 证明此类紧黎曼流形不 admits 拟调和球，且凸函数无需满足全局增长条件。

Conclusion: 万有覆盖上凸函数的存在性足以排除拟调和球，拓宽了相应刚性结果的适用范围。

Abstract: Let $N$ be a compact Riemannian manifold whose universal covering supports a convex function. We prove that it admits no quasi-harmonic sphere. No global growth condition is imposed on the convex function.

</details>


### [230] [Conformal Killing--Yano Ricci solitons: Structure, compatibility, and rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23424)
*Mohammadjavad Habibivostakolaei,Abbas M. Sherif,Yen-Kheng Lim*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入共形Killing–Yano Ricci孤立子（CKY–RS）结构，将共形Ricci孤立子几何与CKY 2-形式耦合，其中孤立子场由CKY 2-形式的散度给出。文章构造守恒CKY–Cotton流并推导相容恒等式，随后在4维Lorentz号差下证明非退化闭合CKY形式迫使共形代表为局部Kerr–NUT–(A)dS，并在Einstein背景下给出特征值方程、Obata型Hessian方程及谱障碍与分类结果，最后给出静态球对称、BTZ等显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共形Ricci孤立子（CRS）时，往往缺乏对其隐藏对称结构的几何刻画。作者希望通过引入CKY 2-形式作为耦合对象，建立一个统一框架来研究CRS与CKY对称性之间的相容性，从而为广义相对论中的精确几何提供新的分类与障碍工具。

Method: 定义CKY–RS结构：孤立子场取为CKY 2-形式的散度；构造守恒的CKY–Cotton流，推导联系Cotton张量、CRS障碍张量与CKY 2-形式的相容恒等式。在4维Lorentz号差下，结合非退化与闭合性假设分析共形代表。对任意维数与号差的Einstein背景，推导共形因子的特征值方程与Obata型Hessian方程；利用紧致性或CKY轨道周期性得到谱障碍，并由Hessian方程进行分类。

Result: 在4维Lorentz号差、CKY形式非退化且闭合的条件下，CKY–RS结构迫使共形代表为局部Kerr–NUT–(A)dS；在Kerr–NUT–(A)dS背景上，闭合非退化CKY对应的共形形变必为平凡。对一般Einstein背景，共形因子满足特征值方程与Obata型Hessian方程；若背景紧致或CKY轨道周期，共形因子为CKY流的 invariant，并给出非平凡CKY–RS结构的谱障碍。由Hessian方程得到非平凡共形扇区的障碍与分类（含乘积/Brinkmann几何与Weyl对齐分支），并给出静态球对称几何、BTZ背景及含时间依赖共形平坦代表的CKY–RS显式构造。

Conclusion: CKY–RS提供了一个将共形Ricci孤立子几何与CKY对称性耦合的几何框架，能够产生强刚性结论（如4维下局部Kerr–NUT–(A)dS）、谱障碍与分类结果，并可显式构造多种背景，对广义相对论中具隐藏对称结构的精确几何研究具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a geometric structure -- a conformal Killing--Yano Ricci soliton (CKY--RS) -- that couples conformal Ricci soliton (CRS) geometry to conformal Killing--Yano (CKY) 2--forms. The soliton field of the CRS geometry is given by the divergence of the CKY 2--form. We introduce a conserved CKY--Cotton current and derive a compatibility identity relating the Cotton tensor, the CRS obstruction tensor, and the CKY 2--form. In 4--dimensional Lorentzian signature, we show that, under non-degeneracy and closedness assumptions on the CKY form, a CKY--RS structure forces the conformal representative to be locally Kerr--NUT--(A)dS. For a closed non-degenerate CKY on a Kerr--NUT--(A)dS background, the conformal deformation is necessarily trivial. For Einstein backgrounds of arbitrary dimension and signature, the conformal factor satisfies an eigenvalue equation and an Obata--type Hessian equation. If the background is also compact or a CKY orbit is periodic, the conformal factor is an invariant of the CKY--flow and we obtain simple spectral obstructions to non-trivial CKY--RS structures. From the Hessian equation, we obtain obstruction and classification results for the non-trivial conformal sector, including product/Brinkmann geometries and a Weyl--aligned branch. Finally, we give explicit constructions for static spherically symmetric geometries and BTZ backgrounds, including a CKY--RS realization with a time-dependent conformally flat representative. These results provide a geometric framework for studying CRS with hidden symmetry structure, with potential applications to exact geometries in general relativity.

</details>


### [231] [Sharp scalar and Ricci curvature pinching theorems for Lagrangian submanifolds in the homogeneous nearly Kähler Six-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23470)
*Zejun Hu,Linlin Sun,Jiabin Yin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 针对S^6(1)中连通闭Lagrangian子流形建立新积分不等式，证明标量曲率τ和Ricci曲率的Sharp pinching定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究齐性近Kähler球面S^6(1)中连通闭Lagrangian子流形的曲率pinching问题，寻求标量曲率与Ricci曲率的Sharp界限及其刚性分类。

Method: 首先建立一个新的积分不等式，然后将其应用于推导曲率pinching定理，分析达到界限时的子流形几何结构。

Result: τ≥23/8时，或τ=6且全测地，或τ=23/8且合同于Dillen–Verstraelen–Vrancken嵌入Berger球；Ric≥1/8时，或全测地，或有常截曲率K=1/16，或合同于Dillen-Verstraelen-Vrancken嵌入Berger球。

Conclusion: 所得pinching定理为Sharp的，界限达到时子流形被完全分类，推广了对S^6(1)中Lagrangian子流形曲率刚性的认识。

Abstract: For connected closed Lagrangian submanifolds of the homogeneous nearly Kähler sphere $\mathbb S^6(1)$, we first establish a new integral inequality. Then, as applications we prove sharp pinching theorems for the scalar curvature $τ$ and Ricci curvature Ric, respectively: $τ\ge \tfrac{23}{8}$ implies either $τ=6$ with it totally geodesic, or $τ=\tfrac{23}{8}$ with it congruent to the embedded Berger sphere of Dillen--Verstraelen--Vrancken; whereas ${\rm Ric}\geq\frac18$ implies that the submanifold is either totally geodesic, or has constant sectional curvature $K=\tfrac{1}{16}$, or is congruent to the embedded Berger sphere of Dillen-Verstraelen-Vrancken.

</details>


### [232] [Yau-type gradient estimates for p-harmonic functions on Riemannian manifolds with boundary under Dirichlet boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23471)
*Bingqi Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在完备黎曼流形（带紧边界）上，对正的 $p$-调和函数证明了 Yau 型梯度估计，并由此得到非负曲率情形的 Liouville 定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kunikawa 与 Sakurai 已对调和函数（即 $p=2$）建立此类估计，但将其推广到全部 $p$-Laplace 算子存在本质困难：线性理论中的截断方法依赖于边界距离的方向 Hessian，而 Laplacian 比较定理无法控制该量。

Method: 假设流形满足 Ricci 曲率下界、边界满足平均曲率下界，并给定 Dirichlet 条件与外法向导数的符号条件。用边界处的径向障碍函数（radial barrier）替代传统截断函数，并结合内部的内蕴极大值原理完成证明。

Result: 在 $p \neq 2$ 的全范围内证明了正的 $p$-调和函数的 Yau 型梯度估计，成功绕开了方向 Hessian 不可控的障碍。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Kunikawa–Sakurai 的调和函数梯度估计推广至一般 $p$-Laplace 算子；当 Ricci 曲率与边界平均曲率均非负时，可推出相应的 Liouville 定理。

Abstract: A Yau-type gradient estimate is proved for positive $p$-harmonic functions on complete Riemannian manifolds with compact boundary, under a Ricci lower bound on the Riemannian manifold and a mean curvature lower bound on the boundary, assuming the Dirichlet condition and a sign condition on the outward normal derivative. The result extends the estimate for harmonic functions by Kunikawa and Sakurai to the full range of $p$-Laplace operators and yields a Liouville theorem when the curvature hypotheses are nonnegative. The cutoff method of the linear theory does not extend when the exponent differs from two, since it requires a directional Hessian of the distance to the boundary that Laplacian comparison cannot control. That cutoff is replaced here by a radial barrier at the boundary and an intrinsic maximum principle in the interior.

</details>


### [233] [Embedded constant mean curvature tori in hemispheres of Berger spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23484)
*Jihao Liu,Xiang Ma*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在 κ>8τ²（τ≠0）条件下，Berger 球面 E³(κ,τ) 中存在含于开半球内的光滑闭合嵌入、亏格为一、常平均曲率的旋转曲面，从而否证 Fernández–Mira 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fernández 与 Mira 曾提出猜想，认为在相应几何条件下此类常平均曲率的闭合嵌入旋转曲面不存在。本文旨在检验并推翻这一猜想，从而修正对该问题的既有认识。

Method: 在 Berger 球面 E³(κ,τ) 的旋转对称框架下，构造亏格为一的旋转曲面并求解其常平均曲率方程；所得主要结果由生成式 AI 方法（GPT-5.6-sol、Fable 5 与 Danus 系统）产生，随后由作者进行验证。

Result: 对所有满足 κ>8τ² 且 τ≠0 的实数 κ、τ，Berger 球面 E³(κ,τ) 中均存在光滑闭合嵌入、亏格为一、常平均曲率且含于某个开半球内的旋转曲面。

Conclusion: 该构造直接否证了 Fernández–Mira 猜想。作为一项由 AI 辅助生成并经人工验证的数学结果，其可靠性有赖于作者的独立核验，同时也提示 AI 工具在具体几何构造与反例发现中可作为辅助手段。

Abstract: We prove that for all real numbers $κ,τ$ with $κ>8τ^2$ and $τ\neq 0$, the Berger sphere $\mathbb{E}^3(κ,τ)$ contains smooth closed embedded rotational surfaces of genus one with constant mean curvature which are contained in an open hemisphere. This disproves a conjecture of Fernández and Mira. The main result of this paper is obtained by generative AI, particularly GPT-5.6-sol, Fable 5, and the Danus system, and then verified by the authors.

</details>


### [234] [Construction of compact embedded $λ$-hypersurfaces via isoparametric hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23499)
*Lai Junqi,Wei Guoxin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造了 $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ 中拓扑型为 $S^1\times M$ 的闭嵌入 $\lambda$-超曲面，其中 $M\subset\mathbb{S}^n$ 是等主曲率重数的等参超曲面，对任意 $\lambda\ge 0$ 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广 Angenent、McGrath、Riedler（$\lambda=0$）以及 Cheng-Wei、Ross（$\lambda>0$）的结果，并补全 Riedler 构造中缺失的情形。

Method: 利用等参超曲面 $M$ 的等主曲率重数性质，构造乘积拓扑型 $S^1\times M$ 的闭嵌入 $\lambda$-超曲面。

Result: 对任意 $\lambda\ge 0$，在 $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ 中构造出拓扑型为 $S^1\times M$ 的闭嵌入 $\lambda$-超曲面，其中 $M$ 为等主曲率重数的等参超曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了既有结果并补全了 Riedler 构造的缺失情形。

Abstract: We construct closed embedded $λ$-hypersurfaces in ${\mathbb{R}^{n+1}}$ of topological type ${S^1\times M}$ for any ${λ\ge 0}$, where ${M\subset\mathbb{S}^n}$ is an isoparametric hypersurface with equal principal curvature multiplicities. This extends previous results of Angenent, McGrath, and Riedler (for ${λ= 0}$) as well as Cheng-Wei and Ross (for ${λ> 0}$), and in particular completes the missing case of Riedler's construction.

</details>


### [235] [Rigidity of Einstein Manifolds under Two Eigenvalue Conditions on the Curvature Operator of the Second Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23597)
*Haiqing Cheng,Kui Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个刚性定理：在第二类曲率算子的两个下界条件下，维数 $n\ge6$ 的闭爱因斯坦流形要么是平坦的，要么是球面空间形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究第二类曲率算子谱下界对闭爱因斯坦流形的刚性影响，并推广既有的 CW26 结果。文中条件设计使第二个条件严格弱于 CW26 的对应条件，且与第一个条件互不蕴含。

Method: 设第二类曲率算子 $\mathring R$ 的特征值为 $\lambda_1\le\cdots\le\lambda_N$，$N=(n-1)(n+2)/2$，$\bar\lambda$ 为其平均值。对 $L_1,L_2\ge0$ 及合适范围内的实数 $\alpha>1$，假设 $\lambda_1\ge-L_1\bar\lambda$ 且 $\frac1\alpha\sum_{j=1}^\alpha\lambda_j\ge-L_2\bar\lambda$；在此参数范围内 $L_2>\theta(n,\alpha)$，并在 $L_1$ 与 $L_2$ 之间附加一个显式关系，利用曲率算子谱估计与刚性分析进行证明。

Result: 在上述谱下界条件及 $L_1$ 与 $L_2$ 的附加显式关系下，证明了该闭爱因斯坦流形要么平坦，要么为球面空间形式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了第二类曲率算子部分特征值平均下界下的刚性刻画，推广并弱化了 CW26 中相应条件，说明这类谱约束足以强制流形具有高度对称的几何结构。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for closed Einstein manifolds of dimension $n\ge6$ under two lower bounds on the curvature operator of the second kind. More precisely, for $L_1, L_2\ge0$ and some real number $α>1$ in a suitable range, we consider \[ λ_1\ge-L_1\barλ, \qquad \frac1α\sum_{j=1}^αλ_j \ge-L_2\barλ. \] Here $λ_1\le\cdots\leλ_N$ are the eigenvalues of the curvature operator of the second kind $\mathring R$, $N=(n-1)(n+2)/2$, and $\barλ=N^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^Nλ_j$. Let $θ(n, α)$ be the constant appearing in \cite[Theorem~1.1]{CW26}; see (2). In the parameter range considered here, $L_2>θ(n, α)$, so the second condition is strictly weaker than the corresponding condition in \cite{CW26}, while the first condition and that condition do not imply each other. Under an additional explicit relation between $L_1$ and $L_2$, we prove that the manifold is either flat or a spherical space form.

</details>


### [236] [Convergence of cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian mean curvature flow in positive Kähler-Einstein manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23627)
*Naotoshi Fujihara,Toru Kajigaya,Albert Wood*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明闭正Kähler–Einstein流形中余齐性一Lagrangian的平均曲率流长期存在、保持嵌入并光滑收敛到极小Lagrangian；并推广到几乎爱因斯坦流形，得到f-极小Lagrangian的子收敛及解析情形下的光滑收敛。证明核心是约化为加权曲线缩短流，建立有限时间奇点刻画与长期子收敛定理，并用Łojasiewicz–Simon不等式升级为光滑收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lagrangian平均曲率流的长期行为与收敛性，并在更一般的几乎爱因斯坦Kähler流形（Ricci形式满足ρ=Cω+ndd^cf且C>0）上推广，以统一处理加权情形下的极小子流形收敛问题。

Method: 将流约化为紧二维orbifold上的加权曲线缩短流；建立Grayson型有限时间奇点描述与长期子收敛定理；最后用Łojasiewicz–Simon不等式把子收敛提升为全流的光滑图形收敛。正则性与精确性假设贯穿其中。

Result: 在自然精确性与正则性假设下，闭嵌入余齐性一Lagrangian的平均曲率流对所有时间存在、保持嵌入，并光滑图形收敛到极小Lagrangian；在几乎爱因斯坦情形下获得到f-极小Lagrangian子流形的子收敛，且当(M,g,f)解析时升级为光滑图形收敛。

Conclusion: 余齐性一Lagrangian的平均曲率流具有良好长期行为，且加权曲线缩短流框架可统一处理Kähler–Einstein与几乎爱因斯坦情形下的收敛性，解析性可进一步提升收敛强度。

Abstract: We prove that Lagrangian mean curvature flow starting from a closed, embedded, cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian in a closed, positive Kähler--Einstein manifold exists for all time, remains embedded, and converges smoothly and graphically to a minimal Lagrangian, under natural exactness and regularity assumptions. We also study the generalised Lagrangian mean curvature flow of Behrndt in Kähler manifolds which are almost-Einstein in the sense that the Ricci form satisfies $ρ= Cω+ ndd^cf$, and which satisfy $C>0$. With analogous assumptions on the flow, we obtain subconvergence to an $f$-minimal Lagrangian submanifold, with an upgrade to smooth graphical convergence in the case that $(M,g,f)$ is analytic. The proof proceeds by first reducing the flow to a weighted curve shortening flow on a compact two-dimensional orbifold. We then establish both a Grayson-type description of finite-time singularities and a long-time subconvergence theorem for weighted curve shortening flow in the orbifold setting. Finally, we use a Łojasiewicz--Simon inequality argument to upgrade to smooth convergence of the full flow.

</details>


### [237] [Extremal Kähler--Ricci solitons on Fano manifolds are Kähler--Einstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23630)
*Yasufumi Nitta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了Fano流形上的极值Kähler-Ricci孤立子必为Kähler-Einstein度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Calamai与Petrecca提出的公开问题，即极值Kähler-Ricci孤立子与Kähler-Einstein度量之间的关系。

Method: （摘要未给出具体方法，仅说明以完全一般性的方式给出证明。）

Result: 在Fano流形上，任何极值Kähler-Ricci孤立子都被证明是Kähler-Einstein度量。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了Calamai-Petrecca问题，表明在Fano情形下极值Kähler-Ricci孤立子不产生真正新的例子。
