We show that global sections induce an equivalence $\mathrm{LS}^{\mathrm{temp}}(BU(1),A) \simeq \mathrm{QCoh}(A)$ and we prove that $\mathrm{LS}^\mathrm{temp}(X,A)$ is a smashing localization of $\mathrm{LS}^\mathrm{gen}(X,A)$ if $X$ is an orbispace.

</details>


### [324] [Automated proofs of unstable Adams differentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23984)
*Jake Francis Baer*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于计算机的方法，通过系统应用不稳定 Leibniz 法则及 EHP 序列中映射的自然性，计算不稳定 Adams 谱序列中的微分，并给出不稳定 50-stem 内球面 2-primary 不稳定同伦群阶的上、下界表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定 Adams 谱序列中微分的计算通常依赖繁琐的人工推导，缺乏系统化、可复用的计算手段；同时球面不稳定同伦群的阶在较高 stem 范围内尚不明确，需要借助计算工具获得可靠的上、下界估计。

Method: 采用计算机辅助方法，系统应用不稳定 Leibniz 法则，并利用 EHP 序列中映射的自然性来推导微分；将结果整理为不稳定 Adams 图表与流程图（flow chart）形式的微分证明记录。

Result: 给出不稳定 50-stem 以内球面 2-primary 不稳定同伦群阶的上界与下界表格；提供若干微分的证明示例；并给出解读相关不稳定 Adams 图表及微分证明流程图的使用指南。

Conclusion: 该计算机辅助框架可系统化地推进不稳定 Adams 谱序列中微分的计算，为高维球面不稳定同伦群的阶提供有效上下界，并为后续计算提供了图表与流程图等可操作工具。

Abstract: We present a computer-based approach to computing differentials in the unstable Adams spectral sequence by systematically applying the unstable Leibniz rule and naturality with respect to maps in the EHP sequence. We record our results in tables of upper and lower bounds on the orders of 2-primary unstable homotopy groups of spheres through the unstable 50-stem. We provide examples of proofs for several differentials and give a guide to interpreting the associated unstable Adams charts and flow chart diagrams for differential proofs.

</details>


### [325] [Equivariant generating hypotheses for finite groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24390)
*Sihao Ma,XiaoLin Danny Shi,Zhouli Xu,Shangjie Zhang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文否定了Bohmann关于每个非平凡有限群G的等变生成猜想（即使检测所有RO(H)-分次同伦群），并通过圆幂映射与射影空间乘积的循环置换构造了任意长非零复合幂的ghost。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变生成猜想（Bohmann提出）是稳定同伦论中生成猜想在等变情形下的推广，作者希望验证其是否在非平凡有限群情形下成立，尤其是当对所有子群的RO(H)-分次同伦群都进行检测时。

Method: 利用圆幂映射（circle power maps）以及射影空间乘积的循环置换构造ghost，并借鉴Ma–Xu在motivic情形下的范畴方法与射影空间幂映射思想。

Result: 对每个固定的G及整除|G|的素数p，构造出有限G-谱上具有任意长非零复合幂的ghost；证明同伦模函子非满（nonfull）；并构造出具有同构完整同伦模但互不等价的有限G-谱。

Conclusion: Bohmann的等变生成猜想对所有非平凡有限群G均不成立，即使加强为检测所有RO(H)-分次同伦群也无济于事。

Abstract: We disprove Bohmann's equivariant generating hypothesis for every nontrivial finite group $G$, even when all $\RO(H)$-graded homotopy groups at every subgroup $H$ are tested. For each fixed $G$ and prime $p$ dividing $|G|$, we construct ghosts on finite $G$-spectra with arbitrarily long nonzero composition powers. We also prove that the homotopy-module functors are nonfull and construct non-equivalent finite $G$-spectra with isomorphic full homotopy modules.
  Our constructions use circle power maps and cyclic permutations of products of projective spaces, and are motivated by Ma--Xu's categorical method in the motivic setting and the projective-space power maps.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [326] [Higher-order and non-geometric symmetries and Noether's theorem in k-cosymplectic Hamiltonian field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23034)
*Manuel de León,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在$k$-余辛Hamilton场论框架下研究高阶对称性与非几何对称性，提出广义无穷小对称、高阶广义无穷小Cartan对称及良好非几何对称三类新对称，并分别建立Noether型定理给出守恒量，结果进一步推广至$k$-辛流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典场论中的对称性与守恒律关系（Noether定理）已有大量研究，但$k$-余辛Hamilton场论中标准Cartan对称之外的高阶与非几何对称性尚未被系统处理，需要发展统一的几何框架以刻画更一般的对称性及其守恒量。

Method: 在$k$-余辛Hamilton场论框架下引入广义无穷小对称概念（放宽标准对称条件），据此定义高阶广义无穷小Cartan对称和良好非几何对称两类新对称；对每类对称建立Noether型定理，为精确$k$-余辛Hamilton系统构造显式守恒量，并将整套结果平行推广到$k$-辛流形上的Hamilton系统。

Result: 对高阶广义无穷小Cartan对称和良好非几何对称分别建立了Noether型定理，给出精确$k$-余辛Hamilton系统的显式守恒量；结果推广了$k$-余辛情形下标准Cartan对称的已有工作，并平行推广至$k$-辛流形；通过具体例子验证了所提对称的存在性与适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为经典场论中广义对称及其守恒律提供了系统的几何处理方式，体现出在$k$-余辛与$k$-辛两种设定下的统一性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate higher-order symmetries and non-geometric symmetries within the framework of $k$-cosymplectic Hamiltonian field theory. We introduce the concept of generalized infinitesimal symmetry, which relaxes the standard symmetry condition, and then define two new classes of symmetries: higher-order general infinitesimal Cartan symmetries and good non-geometric symmetries. For each of these symmetry classes, we establish a Noether-type theorem that provides explicit conserved quantities for exact $k$-cosymplectic Hamiltonian systems. These results extend and generalize previous work on standard Cartan symmetries in the $k$-cosymplectic setting. Furthermore, we extend all of these results in a parallel fashion to Hamiltonian systems on $k$-symplectic manifolds, demonstrating the unifying nature of our approach. Concrete examples are provided to illustrate the existence and applicability of the proposed symmetries. Our framework offers a systematic geometric treatment of generalized symmetries and their associated conservation laws in classical field theories.

</details>


### [327] [Cohomology of $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ in trivial and in adjoint representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22598)
*Thiago Oliveira Ferreira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Hochschild-Serre谱序列，在特征零域上完整计算了射影特殊线性Lie超代数𝔭𝔰𝔩(n|n)在平凡系数与伴随系数下的上同调，并附带得到𝔰𝔩(m|n)平凡系数上同调，指出与既有文献的一处不符，给出n≤4的低次上同调群显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 𝔭𝔰𝔩(n|n)的实形式𝔭𝔰𝔲(n|n)，特别是𝔭𝔰𝔲(2,2|4)，在AdS/CFT等物理中有应用，其对偶上同调具有物理意义；同时文献中关于𝔰𝔩(m|n)平凡系数上同调存在不一致陈述，需要澄清。

Method: 利用与𝔭𝔰𝔩(n|n)相伴的Hochschild-Serre谱序列，在特征零域上分别就平凡系数与伴随系数进行完整计算，并顺势确定𝔰𝔩(m|n)的平凡系数上同调。

Result: 完整给出了𝔭𝔰𝔩(n|n)在平凡系数与伴随系数下的上同调；得到𝔰𝔩(m|n)平凡系数上同调并与文献中先前结论存在差异；列出了n≤4时的低次上同调群显式结果。

Conclusion: Hochschild-Serre谱序列是计算此类Lie超代数上同调的有效工具；结果可用于𝔭𝔰𝔲(n|n)的物理应用，并纠正文献中关于𝔰𝔩(m|n)上同调的一处陈述。

Abstract: We compute the cohomology of the projective special linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ over a field of characteristic zero in trivial and in adjoint coefficients in full detail through its associated Hochschild--Serre spectral sequences. Applications in physics exist for its real form, the projective special unitary Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{psu}(n|n)$ --- in particular, $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$ for AdS/CFT. We also find the cohomology of $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$ in trivial coefficients along the way and we point out a discrepancy with a previous statement in the literature. We present the explicit cohomology groups of low degree for $n\leqslant4$.

</details>


### [328] [Six-wave scattering in Hamiltonian Dysthe equations: Euler matching and integrability obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23175)
*Alex J. Sutherland,Solomon C. Yim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了时空Hamiltonian Dysthe方程中的有效六波相互作用，并与深水Euler Hamilton量的完全六次系数进行比较，给出共振集上的渐近公式与消失系数分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Dysthe类模型与完整Euler方程在六波（degree-six）相互作用层面的一致性，判定物理Dysthe模型能否支撑无穷多独立守恒/可积层次结构。

Method: 在一般二次色散共振片上，对色散施加小立方修正，解析计算六波系数W_6的渐近展开；结合Zakharov–Schulman条件对二次主符号作约束分类；在Craig–Guyenne–Sulem时间系数下计算Euler共振的对应表达式，并统计四元顶点收缩贡献。

Result: generic共振片上 W_6(Φ_{λ,ε}(z)) = ε·4BD/(aL(z)) + O_K(ε²)，故 BD≠0 时在相对开共振集上散射非零；CGS时间系数下 (μ/4)W_6^E(1+μΨ_μ(z)) = −8/L(z)+O_K(μ)，主项来自九个四元顶点收缩，其余连通六次贡献一致有界；并分类出使generic六波系数恒为零的系数值。

Conclusion: Zakharov–Schulman条件将任意正则C³二次主符号限制在 span{1,k,ω(k)}，即波作用量、动量与线性能量符号的张成；因此物理Dysthe模型无法支撑具有无穷多线性独立正则C³二次主符号的逆散射层次。消失集包含已知NLS、混合Chen–Lee–Liu、Calogero–Moser导数NLS与Hirota代表。

Abstract: We compute the effective six-wave interaction in spatial and temporal Hamiltonian Dysthe equations and compare it with the complete degree-six coefficient of the deep-water Euler Hamiltonian. On every compact subset $K$ of a generic quadratic-dispersion resonance patch, exact resonances persist under a small cubic correction to the dispersion, and \[ W_6\bigl(Φ_{λ,\varepsilon}(z)\bigr) =\varepsilon\frac{4BD}{aL(z)}+O_K(\varepsilon^2) \] uniformly in the resonance point $z$ and the deformation parameter $λ$. Here $L$ is the spectral diameter and $B,D$ are the local cubic and mean-flow coefficients. Thus $BD\ne0$ gives nonzero scattering on relatively open resonance sets. At the Craig--Guyenne--Sulem temporal coefficients, the corresponding exact Euler resonances satisfy \[ \fracμ{4}W_6^{\rm E}\bigl(1+μΨ_μ(z)\bigr) =-\frac{8}{L(z)}+O_K(μ), \] where $μ$ is the relative bandwidth. The leading term comes from nine contractions of quartic vertices; every other connected degree-six contribution is uniformly bounded. We classify the coefficient values for which the generic six-wave coefficient vanishes identically; in the temporal case, this also gives cancellation on every non-permutation four-wave resonance. Outside these sets, the Zakharov--Schulman condition restricts every regular $C^3$ quadratic leading symbol to $\operatorname{span}\{1,k,ω(k)\}$, the span of the symbols of wave action, momentum, and linear energy. Consequently the physical Dysthe models cannot support an inverse-scattering hierarchy with infinitely many linearly independent regular $C^3$ quadratic leading symbols. The cancellation sets contain known nonlinear Schrödinger, mixed Chen--Lee--Liu, Calogero--Moser derivative nonlinear Schrödinger, and Hirota representatives.

</details>


### [329] [Distributional Fractional Taylor Series and Interpretation of the Fractal and Number-Theoretic Explicit Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22759)
*Michel L. Lapidus,Matthew Overduin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义分形弦的框架下，利用分布论方法建立了带误差项的分布分形泰勒公式，以及精确的分布分形泰勒级数表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Lapidus与van Frankenhuisjen关于广义分形弦分形显式公式工作的启发，作者希望将分形显式公式进一步推广为分布意义下的分形泰勒展开，从而以分形复维数刻画分形结构。

Method: 在languidity假设下，将分数阶分布显式公式中的留数项表示为Dirac δ分布的分数阶$(-\omega)$阶分布导数$Y_\omega$作用于修正检验函数$\varphi(x)\ln^{k-1}(x)$，其中$k$取遍几何zeta函数$\zeta_\eta$在极点$\omega$处的重数；系数由$\zeta_\eta$在$\omega$处Laurent展开主部系数乘以$\Gamma(\omega)$给出。

Result: 得到了带误差项的分布分形泰勒公式，并在强languidity条件下得到无误差项的精确分布分形泰勒级数表示。

Conclusion: 该结果推进了以分形泰勒级数展开、借助分形复维数刻画分形的总体研究纲领。

Abstract: Motivated by the work of M. L. Lapidus and M. van Frankenhuisjen on fractal explicit formulas for generalized fractal strings, we develop in this paper a distributional fractal Taylor's formula with error term and an exact distributional fractal Taylor series representation for generalized fractal strings. The explicit formulas that the aforementioned authors developed provide a way of expressing a generalized fractal string $η$ in terms of the underlying fractal complex dimensions, under suitable growth assumptions on its geometric zeta function, $ζ_η$, known as the languidity assumptions. This involves a sum indexed by the complex dimensions of the generalized fractal string, summing over all residues of $ζ_η$, multiplied by the Mellin transform of a tempered or Schwartz test function $φ$, denoted by $\widetildeφ$. Under languidity assumptions, there is an error term present. Under stronger assumptions, that is, in the case of strong languidity, no error term is present and the resulting fractal explicit formula is said to be exact. In this paper, we express the residue term in the fractional distributional explicit formulas as the fractional $(-ω)^{\mathrm{th}}$-ordered distributional derivative of the Dirac $δ$ distribution, denoted by $Y_ω$, applied to modified test functions of the form $φ(x) \ln^{k-1}(x)$, where $k$ runs from $1$ to the multiplicity of the pole $ω$ of $ζ_η$. The coefficients in this sum are written in terms of the coefficients in the principal part of the Laurent expansion at $ω$ of the geometric zeta function $ζ_η$, multiplied by the gamma function evaluated at $ω$. The results obtained in this paper contribute to the broader program of characterizing fractals in terms of fractal Taylor series expansions involving their underlying fractal complex dimensions.

</details>


### [330] [Holomorphic Conformal Cartan Geometry: Conformal Gravity, Twistors, and Ambitwistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22861)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在四维全纯共形Cartan几何统一框架下，系统重述共形引力、twistor与ambitwistor理论，建立它们之间的规范论桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: twistor理论、ambitwistor理论与四维共形引力长期分散研究，缺乏统一几何框架；本文意在用共形Cartan几何提供一个统一描述。

Method: 从几何的旋量表示出发，构造sl(4,C)值Cartan连接、曲率2-形式及相关tractor丛；在实Lorentz切片上评估二次曲率作用量；构造局部twistor与对偶twistor丛作为相关旋量tractor丛；通过不变典范配对连接ambitwistor空间。

Result: 固定Weyl规范后二次曲率作用量重现四维Weyl-Bach共形引力；平行移动给出平坦空间经典关联关系与弯曲背景手征可积性约束（α面反自对偶、β面自对偶）；ambitwistor空间参量化复零测地线并对称容纳左右手Weyl曲率。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了共形Cartan几何与twistor及ambitwistor理论之间的直接规范论桥梁。

Abstract: Four-dimensional conformal gravity, twistor theory, and ambitwistor theory are reviewed within the unified framework of holomorphic conformal Cartan geometry. Starting from the spinorial representation of the geometry, the normal $\mathfrak{sl}(4,\mathbb{C})$-valued Cartan connection, its curvature $2$-form, and the associated tractor bundles are systematically formulated. Evaluated on the real Lorentzian slice, the quadratic curvature action is shown to reproduce four-dimensional Weyl--Bach conformal gravity upon fixing a Weyl gauge. Local twistor and dual twistor bundles are constructed as associated spin tractor bundles, illustrating how parallel transport yields the classical incidence relations in flat space and chiral integrability constraints in curved backgrounds: anti-self-duality for $α$-surfaces and self-duality for $β$-surfaces. Furthermore, by pairing local twistors and dual twistors via an invariant canonical pairing, ambitwistor space is shown to parameterize complex null geodesics and symmetrically accommodate both left- and right-handed Weyl curvatures. This framework establishes a direct gauge-theoretic bridge linking conformal Cartan geometry to twistor and ambitwistor theories.

</details>


### [331] [Long time stability of low--frequency packets of modes at finite total energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23119)
*Dario Bambusi,Antonio Ponno*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究FPUT α-模型的能量输运问题：给定小总能量E（与N无关），若初始能量仅分布在波数不超过θ_*的傅里叶模式上，则能量在长时间E^{-(1-ε/2)}内不会流向波数大于θ_*的模式。证明基于与Toda晶格的接近性，不使用其作用-角变量，而是利用Flaschka积分并构造合适的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPUT模型中能量是否会发生向高频模式的输运（即是否最终趋于能量均分）是经典且长期未决的问题。作者希望在长时间尺度上给出能量不流向更高波数的严格结论，并寻找不依赖Toda系统作用-角变量的证明途径。

Method: 考虑N个粒子、总能量E且与N无关的FPUT α-模型。利用Toda晶格与FPUT系统的接近性作为证明工具。不同于以往方法，作者不采用Toda系统的作用-角变量，而是使用Flaschka积分，并对其进行适当重组：重组后积分的二次部分用于控制能量所在波包，高阶部分则满足很好的估计。

Result: 证明了：若初始能量仅赋予波数满足πk/N≤θ_*的傅里叶模式，则在时间尺度数量级为E^{-(1-ε/2)}内，能量不会流向波数大于θ_*的模式，其中θ_*和ε均为小量。

Conclusion: FPUT α-模型在小能量、长时间尺度下表现出能量向高波数模式输运的抑制作用，即波数截断在长时间内保持。基于Flaschka积分重组的新方法为分析FPUT与Toda系统接近性提供了有效工具，避免了对Toda作用-角变量的依赖。

Abstract: We consider the FPUT $α$-model with $N$ particles and initial data with small total energy $E$, independent of $N$. In particular, we prove that if the energy is initially given to Fourier modes of wave number $πk/N\leq θ_*$, then it does not flow to modes of wave number larger than $θ_*$ up to long times of order $E^{-(1-ε/2)}$, $θ_*$ and $ε$ being small numbers. The proof relies on the closeness of the Toda lattice to the FPUT system. Unlike previous approaches, we do not make use of the action-angle variables of the Toda system, but we exploit the Flaschka integrals of the latter. Actually, we find a suitable recombination of the Flaschka integrals whose quadratic part is used to control the packet, the higher order part fulfilling very good estimates.

</details>


### [332] [Routing and memory in the three-dimensional mirror model on K-lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23244)
*Jian Gu,Paul Schaeffer,Qin Hao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维 Manhattan mirror 模型（K-lattice）中的退局域化数值可观测量，通过分离透射边界通道位置与通道间路径、按返回年龄分解重复顶点贡献等一系列实验，给出若干数值估计，但未证明无限体积退局域相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维 Manhattan mirror 模型（K-lattice）中的退局域化现象。核心动机在于通过数值实验分离不同机制：将透射边界通道的位置与它们之间的路由区分开来，以厘清哪些因素真正主导物理透射，并为严格证明提供可用的估计线索。

Method: 设计多组数值实验：(1) 分离透射边界通道位置与通道间路由，并构造一个保持全部透射与反射孔径集合的精确条件对照，比较物理透射相对于该对照的亏缺；进一步考察邻近透射通道相对于同势均匀子集的抑制。(2) 第二个实验按返回年龄（return age）分解重复顶点的贡献，测量带符号旧记忆余项的平方范数随尺度的衰减。(3) 采用环形（annular）实验量化递减的延续损失，并检验最初观察到的历史依赖性是否可独立复现。(4) 考察周期构型下辅助接触图的连通性与有限路由环。

Result: 相对于保持透射与反射孔径集合的精确条件对照，物理透射存在统计上可分辨的亏缺；邻近透射通道相对于同基数均匀子集也受到抑制。带符号旧记忆余项的平方范数在测量范围内与逆平方根衰减一致。环形实验显示延续损失递减，而最初看似存在的历史依赖性在独立复现中不成立。周期构型具有连通的辅助接触图，但只存在有限条路由环。

Conclusion: 结果给出了关于反射路由以及自适应选取的重访电荷的估计，这些估计可能有助于严格证明；但本研究并未建立无限体积的退局域相。

Abstract: We study numerical observables relevant to delocalization in the three-dimensional Manhattan mirror model, also known as the K-lattice. Our principal experiment separates the locations of transmitting boundary channels from the routing between them. We derive an exact conditional comparison that preserves all transmission and reflection aperture sets, and find a statistically resolved deficit of physical transmission relative to this comparison. Nearby transmitting channels are themselves suppressed relative to a uniform subset with the same cardinality. A second experiment resolves the contribution of repeated vertices by their return age. The squared norm of the signed old-memory remainder is consistent with an inverse-square-root decay over the measured range. Annular experiments quantify decreasing continuation losses, while an initially apparent history dependence fails independent replication. Finally, a periodic configuration has a connected auxiliary contact graph but only finite routing cycles. These results identify estimates on reflection routing and adaptively selected revisit charges that may assist a rigorous proof. They do not establish an infinite-volume delocalized phase.

</details>


### [333] [Resurgence and Complex Geodesics for the Heisenberg Heat Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23608)
*Xinxing Tang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究各向同性Heisenberg群上的复测地作用与热核复活（resurgence），刻画最小鞍点Borel芽解析延拓的有限奇点、方向Stokes算子与Stokes矩阵、大阶渐近及A₂汇合公式，并给出正Borel求和与复端点极限谱的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在非平凡几何背景（各向同性Heisenberg群）上系统建立热核的复活理论：明确解析延拓所触及的复作用奇点、Stokes跃迁与Borel求和的关系，并区分“外来支撑”与完整Borel奇异支撑，从而更精细地理解渐近展开的可和性与谱结构。

Method: 对最小鞍点Borel芽作解析延拓并分析其有限非零奇点；在非共振端点构造行有限方向Stokes算子，计算正向射线Stokes矩阵及其对数并比较本原外来支撑与Borel奇异支撑；在竖直轴附近由最近Borel奇点导出一致两项大阶渐近，利用三次共振给出一致A₂汇合公式；结合原始Fourier围道识别被选取的复活扇区，并讨论正Borel求和与复端点谱。

Result: 实端点（r>0,y≠0）时，解析延拓达到的非零有限奇点恰为有限复作用差；非共振端点得到行有限方向Stokes算子与显式正向射线Stokes矩阵及其对数；竖直轴附近获得由最近Borel奇点支配的一致两项大阶渐近；三次共振具一致A₂汇合公式；正Borel求和水平端点精确；非零各向同性复端点无有限正规轨迹，带符号竖直作用格构成完整极限谱；作用与Stokes结构在各向同性Hⁿ上与维数无关。

Conclusion: 该工作给出Heisenberg群上热核复作用与复活结构的统一描述：奇点对应复作用差，Stokes数据可显式计算，渐近与共振可用A₂汇合刻画，且关键结构与维数无关，为几何背景下的Borel求和与谱问题提供框架。

Abstract: We study complex geodesic actions and heat-kernel resurgence on isotropic Heisenberg groups. For real endpoints with $r>0,y\ne0$, the nonzero finite singularities reached by analytic continuation of the minimizing-saddle Borel germ are exactly the finite complex action differences. For nonresonant endpoints, we construct row-finite directional Stokes operators and compute the positive-ray Stokes matrix and its logarithm explicitly; the primitive alien support can be strictly smaller than the full Borel singular support.
  Near the vertical axis, we obtain uniform two-term large-order asymptotics from the nearest Borel singularity. Cubic resonances admit uniform $A_2$ confluence formulas. We also identify the combination of resurgent sectors selected by the original Fourier contour. Positive Borel summation is exact at horizontal endpoints. At nonzero isotropic complex endpoints, no finite normal trajectories exist, but the signed vertical action lattice is the complete limiting spectrum. The action and Stokes structures are independent of the dimension on isotropic $H^n$.

</details>


### [334] [Local conservation laws and symmetries of three-dimensional incompressible Euler flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23772)
*Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了三维齐次不可压Euler方程在速度与压力表述下、允许时间与位置显式依赖及任意有限阶导数的所有光滑局部守恒律与有限阶局部对称特征/伴随对称，证明经典质量、动量、角动量、动能与螺旋度守恒及广义含时动量（模去相应等价类）已穷尽分类；每个对称特征在解上等价于由点变换诱导者，每个伴随对称可用乘子代表，其与Euler方程之积恒为散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 守恒律与对称性是流体方程的核心结构，但三维齐次不可压Euler方程是否还存在经典守恒量之外的高阶局部守恒律与对称特征，此前缺乏完整分类。作者旨在给出该方程全部光滑局部守恒律与有限阶对称的彻底刻画，厘清经典守恒量、含时动量以及伴随对称的相对完整性。

Method: 分类分为三步：先利用确定方程分类伴随对称；再验证显式乘子恒等式；最后建立乘子限制与守恒流类之间的对应。对对称与伴随对称问题，通过最高阶导数响应的共同分析，将问题降至至多一阶，从而完成有限阶局部对称特征与伴随对称的分类。

Result: 所有光滑局部守恒律（模解上零流与恒零散度流）被经典质量、动量、角动量、动能、螺旋度守恒以及广义含时动量穷尽；所有有限阶局部对称特征在解上等价于点变换诱导的对称；所有伴随对称均存在乘子代表，其与Euler方程乘积恒为散度，且这些代表穷尽了所有局部守恒律乘子在解上的限制。

Conclusion: 三维齐次不可压Euler方程在局部、有限阶框架下不含超出已知经典守恒量与含时动量的隐藏守恒结构，对称与伴随对称亦由点变换及乘子代表完全刻画，给出了该方程的完整局部对称—守恒分类。

Abstract: We classify all smooth local conservation laws of three-dimensional homogeneous incompressible Euler flow in velocity and pressure, allowing explicit time and position and derivatives of arbitrary finite order. Modulo currents vanishing on solutions and currents with identically zero divergence, the classical mass, momentum, angular-momentum, kinetic-energy and helicity balances, together with generalised time-dependent momentum, exhaust the classification. We also classify all smooth finite-order local symmetry characteristics and adjoint symmetries: differential expressions satisfying the linearised Euler equations and their formal adjoint on solutions, respectively. Every symmetry characteristic is equivalent on solutions to one induced by a point transformation. Every adjoint symmetry has a multiplier representative, whose product with the Euler equations is identically a divergence. These representatives exhaust the restrictions of all local conservation-law multipliers to solutions. The conservation-law proof has three stages: classification of adjoint symmetries using their determining equations, verification of explicit multiplier identities, and the correspondence between multiplier restrictions and current classes. A common analysis of highest-derivative responses reduces both symmetry problems to order at most one.

</details>


### [335] [Sum of Three Squares and Particle in a Cubic Box - Degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23794)
*S. Pratik Khastgir*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出将正整数表示为三个自然数平方和方式数的闭式精确公式，用于确定立方盒中量子粒子能级的简并度，公式基于对前五百万个数简并度列表的观察总结，未提供严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方盒中量子粒子能级简并度等价于将一个正整数写成三个自然数平方和的方式数，这是数论经典问题，作者希望提供闭式精确解。

Method: 不追求严格证明，从可由简并度列表推断的简单情形出发逐步构造公式，并利用 Mathematica 生成前五百万个数的简并度列表进行细致观察。

Result: 得到该问题的闭式精确公式，并通过大量例子与暴力法数值计算的简并度进行对照验证。

Conclusion: 作者提供了三平方和表示数的闭式公式，虽无证明但实例验证充分，可用于计算立方盒能级简并度。

Abstract: We address the problem of determining the degeneracy of a particular energy eigenvalue corresponding to a quantum particle trapped in a cubic box. In other words, we aim to find the number of ways a specific positive integer can be written as the sum of three natural number squares. This is a classic problem in number theory. We provide a closed-form, exact solution to the problem. We do not claim to provide proofs, as we have none; instead, we build up the formulae starting from simple cases where the answer could be deduced by studying the list of degeneracies. We present ample examples that could be verified against the degeneracy calculated numerically via the brute-force method. We arrived at the solution by meticulously studying the list of degeneracies for the first five million numbers, generated by Mathematica$^{\circledR}$.

</details>


### [336] [Quantum modularity in refined topological recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23864)
*Veronica Fantini,Kento Osuga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Weber型精细谱曲线上精细拓扑递归自由能的Borel可和性与量子模性，发现Stokes常数生成函数为Jacobi量子模函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证精细拓扑递归在Weber型精细谱曲线上的预期性质（Stokes射线成对出现、Borel-Laplace和等于Barnes双伽马函数），并探究其非预期的量子模性特征是否能推广。

Method: 对精细拓扑递归计算出的自由能进行Borel可和性分析与量子模性研究，通过Borel-Laplace求和及Stokes常数生成函数的分析进行证明。

Result: 一方面验证了Stokes射线成对出现，自由能的Borel-Laplace和与Barnes双伽马函数一致；另一方面意外证明每条射线上Stokes常数的生成函数是Jacobi量子模函数。

Conclusion: 量子模性可能是精细拓扑递归的一般特征，可推广至Weber型精细谱曲线之外的情形。

Abstract: In this paper we study the Borel summability and quantum modularity of the free energy computed by refined topological recursion on the Weber-type refined spectral curve. On the one hand, we verify expected properties, namely, Stokes rays come in a pair and the Borel-Laplace sum of the free energy coincides with the Barnes double gamma function. Somewhat unexpectedly, on the other hand, we prove that the generating function of the Stokes constants along each ray is a Jacobi quantum modular function. Our proof suggests that quantum modularity may be a general feature of refined topological recursion, applicable beyond the Weber-type refined spectral curve.

</details>


### [337] [Recursive construction of spectral basis and its application to matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24418)
*A. Acus,A. Dargys*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出广义谱基的递推构造公式，并以各向异性介质中电磁波传播的非对角化矩阵为例进行符号计算演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在符号计算中处理非对角化矩阵（如描述各向异性介质电磁波传播的矩阵）时，需要有效的广义谱基构造手段，而现有方法在通用性和可递推构造方面存在不足。

Method: 提出一种用于构造广义谱基的递推公式，并将其应用于非对角化矩阵的符号计算；方法具有一般性，可推广至Clifford代数中的多重向量等对象。

Result: 导出了广义谱基的递推构造公式，并成功应用于各向异性介质中电磁波传播所对应的非对角化矩阵的符号计算示例。

Conclusion: 该递推构造方法通用性强，不仅适用于非对角化矩阵的符号计算，还可推广到Clifford代数的多重向量等其他对象。

Abstract: We prove a recursive formula for construction of generalized spectral basis. Example of application is presented for symbolic computation with non-diagonalizable matrix that describes electromagnetic wave propagation in anisotropic media. The method is general enough and can be applied, for example, to multivectors of the Clifford algebra.

</details>


### [338] [The Hadamard parametrix on globally hyperbolic spacetimes with Robin boundary conditions: Fundamental solutions and Hadamard states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24465)
*Beatrice Costeri,Claudio Dappiaggi,Benito Alberto Juárez-Aubry*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带类时边界的整体双曲时空上的Klein-Gordon方程与Robin边界条件，证明超前/推迟基本解的存在唯一性及波前集特征，并引入Robin-Hadamard两点关联函数，建立该类时空上Radzikowski定理的对应版本，最后通过形变论证证明其存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带类时边界的时空（如AdS型背景）在物理与数学上具有重要意义，但经典Hadamard态与Radzikowski微局部刻画通常针对无边界的整体双曲时空。该工作旨在将Klein-Gordon量子场论中的基本解、波前集传播及Hadamard两点函数理论推广到含类时边界且采用Robin边界条件的情形。

Method: 在d≥2维整体双曲时空(M,g)上研究Robin边界条件下的Klein-Gordon方程：先证明超前与推迟基本解的存在唯一性及其结构性质；在∂M无穷小凸及破碎零双特征线的边界反射局部有限假设下，利用Melrose-Sjöstrand奇异性传播定理刻画内部波前集。继而定义Robin-Hadamard两点关联函数为M×M上的正分布，规定其反对称部分与奇异性结构，并给出局部Hadamard形式：除标准有向Hadamard参量基外，还包含反射零测地线贡献，其系数由输运方程与边界Robin匹配条件确定，可处理任意有限次反射。最后从适当静态背景的基态出发，用形变论证证明存在性。

Result: 证明了Robin边界条件下超前与推迟基本解的存在唯一性；在给定几何与反射假设下刻画了基本解在内部M̊的波前集；给出Robin-Hadamard两点关联函数的局部Hadamard形式，并证明其与波前集条件等价，从而在有类时边界情形下建立Radzikowski定理的对应版本；进一步证明所考虑时空类上Robin-Hadamard两点关联函数的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作将Klein-Gordon场在带类时边界时空上的量子化理论系统化：基本解、奇异性传播、Hadamard参量基的边界反射修正以及Radzikowski型微局部刻画均被推广至Robin边界条件，并通过形变论证确立了Robin-Hadamard态的存在性。

Abstract: On a $d$-dimensional, $d\geq 2$, globally hyperbolic spacetime with a timelike boundary $(\mathcal{M},g)$, we investigate the Klein-Gordon equation with Robin boundary conditions. First of all, we prove existence and uniqueness of the advanced and retarded fundamental solutions, discussing in addition their structural properties. Secondly, under the hypothesis of infinitesimal convexity of $\partial\mathcal{M}$ and of local finiteness of boundary reflections for broken null bicharacteristics, we characterize their wavefront set in the interior $\mathring{\mathcal{M}}$, using the propagation of singularities theorem of Melrose and Sjöstrand.
  In the second part of the paper, we introduce a notion of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation function as a positive distribution on $\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{M}$ with prescribed antisymmetric part and singular structure, the latter being characterized in terms of its wavefront set. We complement this definition with a local Hadamard form. Up to a smooth remainder, this consists of the standard directed Hadamard parametrix together with additional contributions associated with the reflected null rays. Their coefficients are determined by transport equations supplemented by Robin matching conditions at the boundary. The construction applies to an arbitrary, but finite number of reflections. We prove that this local formulation is equivalent to the wavefront set condition, thereby establishing a counterpart of Radzikowski's theorem in the presence of a timelike boundary. Finally, starting from the ground state on suitable static backgrounds and using a deformation argument, we prove existence of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation functions on the class of spacetimes considered.

</details>


### [339] [Payoffs and perception mediate environmental feedback in an N-player trust game with Q-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24493)
*Ruqiang Guo,Zhaoyi Hu,Fangfang Wang,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将固定角色的两群体层级信任博弈与中心化内生环境耦合，用异质表格Q学习智能体考察环境反馈如何通过物质激励与感知状态改变信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任通过学习形成，而集体行为又会改变后续决策的环境。强化学习模型可刻画适应过程，生态演化模型可刻画行为-环境反馈，但内生环境如何通过物质激励与感知状态改变信任仍不清楚。

Method: 构建固定角色、两群体的层级信任博弈，参与者为异质表格Q学习智能体，并耦合中心化内生环境。反馈对比时，将固定收益基线与反馈分支从同一学习状态初始化以控制先前学习的影响，随后改变收益参数或观测到的环境层级进行比较。

Result: 随收益乘数增大，固定收益博弈中的成功信任由低水平跃升至较高的有限时域水平，由风险偏好型投资者引领；环境反馈在感知层级边界附近促进信任，但在高回报运行点强化信任下降。高回报运行点下，收益反馈强化了学习驱动的信任衰退，驱动越强、时域越长，该负面效应越深。

Conclusion: 与固定收益信任博弈相比，环境反馈将过去的集体结果带入未来收益与观测状态，从而系统性地改变信任的动态演化。

Abstract: Trust develops through learning, while collective behavior can alter the environment in which later decisions are made. Reinforcement-learning models describe adaptation, and eco-evolutionary models describe behavior-environment feedback, but how an endogenous environment changes trust through material incentives and perceived states remains unclear. We couple a fixed-role, two-population hierarchical trust game populated by heterogeneous tabular $Q$-learning agents to a centered endogenous environment. For the main feedback comparisons, we initialize the fixed-payoff baseline and feedback branches from the same learned state to control for prior learning. We then compare responses when payoff parameters or the observed environmental tier vary. As the payoff multiplication factor increased, successful trust in the fixed-payoff game crossed from a low to a higher finite-horizon level, led by risk-seeking investors. Environmental feedback promoted trust near perception-tier boundaries and reinforced its decline at the high-return operating point. At the high-return operating point, payoff feedback reinforced the learning-driven decline in trust. Stronger drive and longer horizons deepened this negative effect. Compared with a fixed-payoff trust game, environmental feedback carries past collective outcomes into future payoffs and observed states.

</details>


### [340] [Proactive Incentive Regulation in Multi-Agent Systems with Environmental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24506)
*Xinyang Cao,Shijia Hua,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建资源状态耦合激励强度的多智能体环境反馈博弈框架，比较资源充裕与匮乏条件下惩罚机制的时机效应，发现充裕时惩罚可将公地悲剧转为支持全合作的双稳态，匮乏时惩罚则引发内点稳定、异宿环与Hopf分岔等复杂动力学，抑制稳定合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境反馈系统中理性自利行为会侵蚀合作与环境可持续性；惩罚性激励机制虽被认为有效，但其在不同环境条件下的实施时机尚不明确，需厘清"何时干预"这一问题。

Method: 建立多智能体环境反馈博弈框架，令激励强度与资源状态相耦合；在资源充裕与资源匮乏两种条件下，通过理论分析考察系统动力学随激励强度的变化，包括稳定性、异宿环与Hopf分岔等。

Result: 理论分析表明：在资源充裕状态下施加惩罚，可将系统从公地悲剧转变为支持完全合作的双稳态；在资源匮乏状态下施加惩罚，则导致内点稳定性、异宿环和Hopf分岔等复杂动力学，阻碍稳定合作的出现。

Conclusion: 激励监管的时机至关重要：在环境条件良好时进行前瞻性干预，比环境退化后再延迟执行惩罚更能有效维持合作。

Abstract: In environmental feedback systems, self-interested behaviors of rational agents often undermine cooperation and environmental sustainability. Although punitive incentive mechanisms are widely recognized as effective in addressing such social dilemmas, the timing of their implementation under different environmental conditions remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we develop a multi-agent environmental feedback game framework with coupled incentive intensities across resource states. We investigate how system dynamics vary with incentive intensity under both resource-abundant and resource-scarce conditions. Theoretical analysis shows that imposing penalties in resource-abundant states can transform the system from a tragedy of the commons into a bistable regime that supports full cooperation. In contrast, imposing penalties in resource-scarce states leads to complex dynamical behaviors, including interior stability, heteroclinic cycles, and Hopf bifurcations, which hinder the emergence of stable cooperation. These results highlight the importance of timing in incentive regulation. Proactive intervention under favorable environmental conditions is more effective in sustaining cooperation than delayed enforcement after environmental degradation.

</details>


### [341] [Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras from Ševera bicomplexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24781)
*Eugenia Boffo,Domenico Fiorenza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Ševera双复形，证明在适当假设下，谱序列第二页的微分d₂是E₂上的Batalin-Vilkovisky算子，当E₂为自由秩1 A-模时可将A的交换代数结构提升为Gerstenhaber代数乃至BV代数，并以Cartan calculus视角统一审视BV代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BV代数结构通常以较抽象的方式出现，缺乏统一的构造框架。Ševera对奇辛流形光滑函数代数上BV结构的描述提供了一个原型，作者希望提炼其内在机制，给出一个更一般、更结构性的构造原理，并借助Cartan calculus的视角加以澄清。

Method: 引入Ševera双复形(M,d₀,d₁)：微分d_i是关于A-模结构M的阶数≤i的Grothendieck微分算子，A为分次交换代数，并要求(M,d₀)具有与d₀同阶的收缩同伦K。在此框架下利用谱序列与Cartan calculus分析第二页微分d₂，考察其作为BV算子的性质及对E₂代数结构的提升。

Result: 在适当假设下，与(M,d₀,d₁)相联系的谱序列第二页上的微分d₂是E₂上的BV算子；若E₂是自由秩1 A-模，则A的分次交换代数结构被提升为Gerstenhaber代数结构，进一步选取E₂中d₂-闭的基元素后提升为Batalin-Vilkovisky代数结构。

Conclusion: 该构造统一了BV代数结构的来源，Ševera关于奇辛流形光滑函数代数上BV结构的结果是其典型例子；全文以Cartan calculus为统一视角，并在结尾简要讨论了向导出Cartan calculus推广的可能性。

Abstract: A Ševera bicomplex is a bicomplex $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ whose differentials $d_{i}$ are Grothendieck differential operators of order $\leq i$ with respect to a given $A$-module structure on $M$, where $A$ is a graded commutative algebra. One also requires that $(M,d_{0})$ admits a contracting homotopy $K$ that is a differential operator of the same order as $d_{0}$. Under suitable assumptions, the differential $d_{2}$ of the second page of the spectral sequence associated with $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ is a Batalin-Vilkovisky operator on $E_{2}$, so that when $E_{2}$ is a free rank 1 $A$-module, the graded commutative algebra structure of $A$ is enhanced to a Gerstenhaber algebra structure, and the choice of a $d_{2}$-closed basis element for $E_{2}$ further enhances this to a Batalin-Vilkovisky algebra structure. The prototypical example of this construction is Ševera's description of the Batalin--Vilkovisky algebra structure on the algebra of smooth functions of an odd symplectic manifold. Throughout the whole article we look at Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras through the lenses of Cartan calculus. Indications of a generalization to derived Cartan calculus are briefly discussed in the concluding section.

</details>


### [342] [Constraints for Physical Gauge Groups Coming from the Causal Action Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24888)
*Felix Finster,Niels Gresnigt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了因果费米子系统中有效局部规范群的约束，重点分析了由双线性对数项产生的关于规范势的二次约束，揭示了李代数与Clifford代数结构之间的联系，并针对$\mathrm{U}(N)\times\mathrm{U}(N)$规范群给出了系统推导与实例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果作用原理框架下，需要确定有效局部规范群所受的约束条件，尤其是来自双线性对数项的二次约束，以理解规范群结构与底层几何/代数结构的关系。

Method: 从对应规范群$\mathrm{U}(N)\times\mathrm{U}(N)$的一般手征势出发，系统推导规范势的二次约束，分析其与李代数和Clifford代数结构之间的对应关系，并结合具体实例进行说明。

Result: 表明规范势的二次约束建立了李代数结构与Clifford代数结构之间的联系，并在$\mathrm{U}(N)\times\mathrm{U}(N)$框架下系统化地给出了这一联系的具体形式。

Conclusion: 因果作用原理中有效局部规范群的约束可将李代数与Clifford代数结构相互关联，为研究因果费米子系统的规范结构提供了新的代数视角。

Abstract: The constraints for the effective local gauge groups stemming from the causal action principle for causal fermion systems are reviewed. The constraint which are quadratic in the gauge potentials (coming from the so-called bilinear logarithmic terms) are shown to make a connection between the structures of Lie algebras and Clifford algebras. This connection is worked out systematically starting from general chiral potentials corresponding to the gauge group $\mathrm{U}(N) \times \mathrm{U}(N)$. The general results are illustrated in various examples.

</details>


### [343] [Don't mind the gap: Absolutely Continuous Edge Spectrum in a Mobility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25000)
*Simon Becker,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维格点哈密顿量与磁薛定谔算子在迁移率隙（体能谱可能局域化的能量区间）中的边缘谱，证明非零体拓扑指标在该区间内诱导绝对连续谱，且绝对连续谱重数下界由指标绝对值给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体-边对应通常假设体能隙中体能谱缺失；本文将其推广到迁移率隙情形，即体能谱存在但局域化，从而刻画该区间内边缘态的谱性质。

Method: 在适当的局域化与边界条件下，对二维格点哈密顿量和磁薛定谔算子引入辅助空间切割构造（第2.3节），分析半空间算子在该能量区间内的谱测度与绝对连续谱重数。

Result: 证明常数非零体拓扑指标蕴含整个迁移率隙区间属于半空间算子的绝对连续谱；在区间内几乎每一能量处，绝对连续谱重数不小于拓扑指标绝对值。

Conclusion: 结果给出了迁移率隙中体-边对应的谱论版本，将拓扑不变量与边缘态绝对连续谱的下界重数直接联系起来。注：第2.3节辅助空间切割构造的第一版由ChatGPT 6生成。

Abstract: We study the edge spectrum of two-dimensional lattice Hamiltonians and magnetic Schrödinger operators in a mobility gap, an energy interval in which the bulk spectrum is potentially localized rather than necessarily absent. Under suitable localization and boundary assumptions, we prove that a constant nonzero bulk topological index implies that the entire interval belongs to the absolutely continuous spectrum of the corresponding half-space operator. The absolutely continuous spectral multiplicity is bounded below by the absolute value of the index at almost every energy in the interval. The first version of the auxiliary-space cut construction in Section 2.3 was generated using ChatGPT 6.

</details>

### [344] [Affine and Lattice Structures in Regular $\mathbb{N}$-Graded Vertex Operator Algebras with Gorenstein $V_0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22780)
*Gaywalee Yamskulna*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 研究零权空间 $V_0$ 为非平凡有限维局部 Gorenstein 代数的正则 $\mathbb{N}$-分次顶点算子代数，探讨在放弃 CFT-type 条件后强有理 VOA 的结构性质如何延续，涉及 Leibniz 代数 $V_1$ 中半单/可解李子代数诱导的不变双线性型、仿射 VOA 及 Heisenberg/格 VOA 的嵌入，并通过共形平移格 VOA 给出显式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强有理 VOA 理论依赖 CFT-type 条件 $V_0=\mathbb{C}\mathbf{1}$。作者希望放宽该条件，考察在 $V_0$ 为非平凡有限维局部 Gorenstein 代数时，原有结构结论（如不变双线性型、仿射化、格嵌入、亏格）哪些仍然成立、需要哪些附加假设，从而厘清 CFT-type 假设的本质作用与结构性障碍。

Method: 将 $V_1$ 视为左 Leibniz 代数，研究其半单李子代数：在 $\ker(L(-1)|_{V_0})=\mathbb{C}\mathbf{1}$ 下用乘积 $u_1v$ 构造不变对称双线性型；结合 $C_2$-余有限与非退化条件分析各单分量生成的正整级仿射 VOA 及其可积作用。对可解 $V_1$，利用 $V_0$ 的 Frobenius 结构选取非退化子空间 $M\subset V_1$，在准原初条件下证明 $M$ 交换并生成 Heisenberg VOA；再附加半单性、满秩整性、余圈相容性，构造共形嵌入的正定偶格 VOA $V_K$。最后用共形平移格 VOA 作为显式模型对比三类结构。

Result: 在半单情形，$u_1v$ 诱导不变对称双线性型；在 $C_2$-余有限与非退化下，带非零型的简单分量生成正整级仿射 VOA 并在 $V$ 上可积作用。在可解情形，Frobenius 结构给出非退化 $M\subset V_1$，准原初条件下 $M$ 交换且生成 Heisenberg VOA；加半单性、满秩整性与余圈相容后，$V$ 含共形嵌入的格 VOA $V_K$，$K$ 正定偶，秩 $\dim_{\mathbb C}M$，最小范数至少 4。共形平移格 VOA 表明正则性本身不足以保证任意 Heisenberg 零模作用的半单性。

Conclusion: 放弃 CFT-type 后，强有理 VOA 的若干结构特征在适当附加假设下仍可恢复，但相应条件（如准原初性、满秩整性、余圈相容）并非技术冗余，而是真实的结构性障碍；共形平移格 VOA 揭示了格定理假设的必要性。

Abstract: We study regular $\mathbb{N}$-graded vertex operator algebras $V=\bigoplus_{n\geq 0}V_n$ whose weight-zero algebra $V_0$ is a nontrivial finite-dimensional local Gorenstein algebra, asking which structural features of strongly rational vertex operator algebras persist without the CFT-type condition $V_0=\mathbb{C}{\bf 1}$.
  For semisimple Lie subalgebras of the left Leibniz algebra $V_1$, assuming $\ker\left(\left.L(-1)\right|_{V_0}\right)=\mathbb{C}\mathbf{1}$, the product $u_1v$ induces an invariant symmetric bilinear form. Under $C_2$-cofiniteness and a nondegeneracy condition, each simple component with nonzero form generates an affine vertex operator algebra at positive integral level and acts integrably on $V$.
  When $V_1$ is solvable, the Frobenius structure of $V_0$ yields a distinguished nondegenerate subspace $M\subset V_1$. Under a quasi-primary condition, $M$ is abelian and generates a Heisenberg vertex operator algebra. With additional semisimplicity, full-rank integrality, and cocycle compatibility assumptions, $V$ contains a conformally embedded lattice vertex operator algebra $V_K$, where $K$ is positive-definite and even, with rank $\dim_{\mathbb{C}}M$ and minimum norm at least $4$.
  Conformally shifted lattice vertex operator algebras provide explicit models illustrating the distinction among weight-one Lie, mode-generated Lie, and lattice structures. They also show that regularity alone does not ensure semisimplicity of arbitrary Heisenberg zero-mode actions, so the additional lattice-theorem hypotheses represent genuine structural obstructions.

</details>


### [345] [The Grothendieck Ring of Strictly Weakly Integral Fusion Categories of Rank 6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23559)
*Kai Wang,Jingcheng Dong,Libin Li*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究秩为6的严格弱整融合范畴，完整分类了其Grothendieck环，得到12个候选环，其中8个可由融合范畴实现，4个被范畴化障碍排除，结合已有结果完成了秩不超过6的严格弱整融合范畴Grothendieck环的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合范畴的Grothendieck环分类是理解其结构与范畴化可能性的核心问题，低秩情形是系统推进分类的自然切入点，而秩6的严格弱整情形尚未完成。

Method: 针对秩为6的严格弱整融合范畴，通过枚举与分析其Grothendieck环的结构（基于融合规则与弱整维数约束），再用范畴化的必要障碍判定各候选环可实现性。

Result: 得到12个候选Grothendieck环；其中8个可由融合范畴实现，其余4个被必要障碍排除不可范畴化。

Conclusion: 结合此前已知结果，秩不超过6的严格弱整融合范畴的Grothendieck环分类得以完整给出。

Abstract: In this paper, we study strictly weakly integral fusion categories of rank 6 and give a complete classification of their Grothendieck rings. We obtain twelve candidate Grothendieck rings. Eight of them can be realized by fusion categories, while the remaining four are ruled out by necessary obstructions to categorification. Together with previously known results, this completes the classification of Grothendieck rings of strictly weakly integral fusion categories of rank at most 6.

</details>

### [346] [Kodaira fibres and wrapped Floer cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23884)
*Yanki Lekili*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 研究相对极小复椭圆纤维化奇异纤维F的邻域，证明其为Weinstein区域，并计算其cocore的wrapped Floer上同调，阐明bulk形变后的wrapped Fukaya范畴等价于显式代数（仿射型乘法preprojective代数或含关系quiver代数），并给出形式性与mirror等价应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复椭圆纤维化的奇异纤维邻域具有丰富的辛几何与镜像对称结构，但其Fukaya范畴及bulk形变下的代数刻画尚不完整；本文旨在给出统一的Weinstein/Legendrian手术描述并显式计算其wrapped Floer不变量，从而联系仿射曲面与代数环面商栈的镜像等价。

Method: 沿用奇异纤维的复结构与非零全纯2-形式Ω，把F的小邻域实现为Re Ω的Weinstein区域，其完备化为Legendre手术，cocore由横截于F各分支的全纯圆盘完备化得到；对任意系数域与乘法bulk类计算这些cocore的wrapped Floer上同调；利用cocore生成性，将bulk形变的wrapped Fukaya范畴描述为显式代数的perfect module范畴。

Result: 对任意系数域和每个乘法bulk类，cocore的wrapped Floer上同调集中在零次；cocore生成整个范畴，故bulk形变wrapped Fukaya范畴等价于显式代数的perfect module范畴：正规交叉纤维对应仿射型乘法preprojective代数，II、III、IV型对应带关系的quiver代数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出仿射plumbing dg代数的形式性，并导出在平凡bulk类下与消解仿射曲面的mirror等价，以及在单位根bulk类下四类星形纤维与代数环面商栈的mirror等价。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a singular fibre of a relatively minimal complex elliptic fibration with smooth total space, and let $Ω$ be a nonvanishing holomorphic two-form near $F$. We show that a small neighbourhood of $F$ is a Weinstein domain for $\mathrm{Re}\,Ω$ whose completion is a Legendrian surgery, with cocores obtained by completing holomorphic disks transverse to the components of $F$. For every coefficient field and every multiplicative bulk class, we compute the wrapped Floer cohomology of these cocores and prove that it is concentrated in degree zero. The cocores generate, so the bulk-deformed wrapped Fukaya category is equivalent to the category of perfect modules over an explicit algebra: a multiplicative preprojective algebra of affine type for the normal crossing fibres, and a quiver algebra with relations for types $II$, $III$ and $IV$. Applications include formality of the affine plumbing dg algebras, mirror equivalences with resolved affine surfaces at the trivial bulk class, and with quotient stacks of algebraic tori at root-of-unity bulk classes for the four star-shaped fibres.

</details>
